patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.




vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.

tamiḻ vaḷam








1
vērccoṟ cuvaṭi
1. ar
	ar = olik kuṟippu.
(a)	aravam = oli.
	arā, aravu, aravam = irivatu, oliyaṟivatu, pāmpu
(b)		ari = ar eṉṉum oli tōṉṟac ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭi 		allatu tiṉ.
	arāvu = tēy. aram = arāvuvatu. arampam = arāvuvatu, 		aṟuppatu.
	ari = aṟu. kōṭu (kiḷi) + ari = kōṭari - kōṭāri -kōṭāli.
	arivāḷ = arikiṉṟa vāḷ. arivāḷmaṇi.
	ari = aḻi. ari = aḻippatu, pakivaṉ, ciṅkam.
	kōḷ + ari = kōḷari.
	araṅku = aṟutta aṟi, ici nāṭaka mēṭi.
	araṅkam = āṟṟiṭi aṟukkappaṭṭa nilam.
	tiruvaraṅkam - srīraṅkam.
	arakku = tēy, aḻi.
	arakkaṉ = irāṭcataṉ.
	araṉ = aḻippavaṉ, tēvaṉ, civaṉ.
	aracu = pakivari aḻippavaṉ, vēntaṉ. aracu + aṉ = aracaṉ.
	aracu = talimiyāṉa allatu teyvam taṅkum maram.
	aracaṉ - aricaṉ - ariyaṉ - rāyaṉ - Roy
2. aṟu
	ar - aṟu.
	aṟu = veṭṭu, piḷa, piri, nīṅku, nīkku.
	aṟuvāḷ = aṟukkiṉṟa vāḷ.
	aṟavu = nīkkam, variyaṟu - variyaṟavu.
	aṟuti = muṭivu.
	aṟi = aṭi, aṟukkappaṭṭa allatu pirikkappaṭṭa iṭam. 
	uṟuppaṟi = uṟuppaṟukkappaṭṭatu. variyaṟu - variyaṟi.
	aṟuku = aṟuttaṟut tōṭip paṭarum pul.
	aṟuvi = aṟukkum tuṇi.
	aṟuppu = payiri allatu tāliyi aṟuttal.
	aṟuvaṭi = aṟuppu.
	aṟṟam = piṟar illāta camiyam.
3. ar
	ar - aru = aḻi, kuṟi, neruṅku.
	aruku = kuṟivāku, neruṅku, pakkam.
	arukaṇmi - arukāmi.
	arukaṉ = neruṅkiṉavaṉ, urimiyuṭiyavaṉ
	arukati = urimi.
	arumi = kuṟivu, kūṭāmi, ciṟappu, viruppam.
	arumivanta - arumanta.
	aruntal = kuṟivu, vili yuyarvu.
	aritu = kūṭātatu, varuttamāṉatu.
4. aḷ
	ar - aḷ - aṇ - aṭu.
(a)	aḷ = neruṅku, neruṅkac cērtteṭu.
	aṇ, aṇṇu = neruṅku.
	aṇmu = neruṅku. 
	aṇmi, aṇumi, aṇimi = camīpam.
	aṇuku = neruṅku.
	aṇṭi = pakkam. 7ām vēṟṟumi urupu
	aṭu = neruṅku, aṭar = neruṅku. aṭavi = neruṅkiṉa kāṭu,
	aṭukku = neruṅkavi.
	aṭutta = neruṅkiṉa, pakkamāṉa. iṉṉoru.
(b)	aṭi = aṭuttatu, appam. aṭi - āṭi.
	pālaṭi = pālāṭi, caṅku.
	aṭukkum = neruṅkum, takum. 'uṉakkitu aṭukkumā?.'
aṭaṅ° =neruṅku,uḷḷami.
	aṭakku (piṟa viṉi).
	aṭakkam = oṭukkam, uḷḷīṭu, putippu.
	aṭi = neruṅku, cēr, peṟu.
	(ṭa = ya) ayal - acal = pakkam, aṉṉiyam
5. ar
	ar = civappu a - i. r - l
	Ürattam= ªcannīr,Ülattakam= ªcampañcuk°ḻampu.	arakku = civanta meḻuku.
	aruṇaṉ = civappu. 
	aruṇam = kālic cūriyaṉ.
	ariṇam = civappu, māṉ. 
	irattam = cennīr.
	iratti, ilanti = civanta paḻattiyuṭiya muṭceṭi.
	irāki = kēḻvaraku.
6. i
	i - kīḻuṟaṟ kuṟippu.
	iṟaṅku = kīḻē vā.
	iru = kīḻ uṭkār, taṅku, vācañcey.
	kuṭiyiru = vācañcey.
	iruppu = taṅkal, rokkam.
	irukki = ācaṉam.
	iḻi = iṟaṅku, kīḻāku.
	iḷi = iḻivu.
7. kil
	i - kil.
(a)	kil - kal = tōṇṭu, tōṇṭuṅ karuvi.
	kil - kiḷ - kīḷ - kīḻ.
(b)	kiḷ, kiḷḷu = nakattik kīḻē pati, kiḷḷukkīri.
	kiḷḷi, kiḷḷi - kiḷi = kaṉikaḷik kiḷḷuvatu.
(c)	kiḻi = kīṟu, tuṇiyik kiḻi.
	kiḻi = muṭiccu, tuṇi, paṭam.
	kīḷ = kiḻi. 'kīḷār kōvaṇam'.
(d)	kīḻ. kīḻkku - kiḻakku = kīḻiṭam, oru tici.
(e)	kīṟu = kōṭu kiḻi. kīṟṟu = kīṟi aṟutta tuṇṭu, tuṇṭu.
	kiṟukku = kōṭu kīṟu, pittiyam.
	kīṟal = kōṭiḻittal, eḻuttaṟiyāmi uṇarttuñ col.
	kīccu = kīṟu.
8. i
	i = piṉṉiṭaṟ kuṟippu.
(a)	iṭaṟu = piṉviḻat taṭukku.
	iṭi = piṉṉiṭu, tōṟṟōṭu. iṭakki = tōṟṟa ki.
	iṭam = tōlvi, iṭar = tuṉpam.
	o.nā : valakki = veṟṟi peṟṟa allatu valiya ki.
	iṭiñcal = piṟ celuttum taṭi.
(b)	iṭa = iḻuttup paṟi.
	iṇuku, iṇuṅku = iḻuttup paṟi.
	iḻu = piṉṉukkuk koṇṭu vā.
	iṟi = taṇṇīri iḻu, piṉṉukkut teṟi, teṟi.
9. iḻu
	i - iḷ - iḻu = piṉṉukkuk koṇṭuvā.
	iḻu - iḻuku = piṉṉōkkit taṭavu.
(a)	iḻuppu = iḻuttal. iḻuvi = iḻuttuk kaṭattal.
	iḻutu = iḻukum mi.
	iḻutu - eḻutu = iḻuttu vari.
	ilakku, ilakki = eḻutu. ilakku = eḻuttu.
	ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam = nūṟṟokuti.
	ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam = moḻiyoḻuṅku.
	o.nā : E. literature from letter; grammer from Gk. grammos, letter
	ilakkam = eḻuttu, eṇkuṟi, eṇ.
(b)	iḻu - īr = iḻu, iḻuttaṟu.
	iḻu - icu - ici. iḻuppu = icivu = jaṉṉi.
	iḻukku = iḻukuvatu, vaḻukkal, vaḻu.
	iḷi = mūcciḻu.
10. iḷ
	i - iḷ.
(a)	iḷi = mūcciḻu, meli.
	iḷippu = mūcciḻuppu, melivu, taḷarcci.

	īḷi = kōḻi, iḷippu.
	iḻi = iḻuttuc cetukku, nūliḻu.

	iḻippu = kāca nōy.
	iḷittavaṉ = melintavaṉ, ciṟiyavaṉ.
	'ūruk kiḷittavaṉ piḷḷiyār kōyil āṇṭi'
(b)	iḷam, iḷami = ciṟu paruvam.
	iḷaval = tampi. iḷiñaṉ = vālipaṉ, vīraṉ.

(c)	iḷappam = tāḻvu, iḷakkāram = meṉmipaṟṟi mēṟcellal.

	ikaḻ = eḷimiyā yeṇṇu.

	mītu = mēl, 7ām vēṟṟumi urupu.
(d)	mīṟu = varampu kaṭa.
	mīṟu - vīṟu = perumi, perumi koḷ.
	vīṟṟu + iru = vīṟṟiru = perumiyuṭaṉiru.
(e)	mettu = mēṟkoḷ, tōṟkaṭi. metti = mēlvīṭu.
	meccu = uyarttip pēcu.
41. ai.
	ai = viyappu, perumi.
	ai viyappākum' (tol. uri. 89)
	aiyaṉ = periyōṉ, talivaṉ, tanti, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ, āciriyaṉ, muṉivaṉ, civaṉ.
	aiyaṉār = cāttaṉār.
	tam + aiyaṉ = tamiyaṉ = aṇṇaṉ.
	aiyi = ammi, pārvati. 
	aiyā = aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi.
	aiyō = (aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi) irakkakkuṟippu.
42. ō.
	ō = olikkuṟippu.

(b)	ol = olikkuṟippu. ol - kol = olikkuṟippu.

43. ō.

	ō - o
	oyyāram = uyaram, uyarvu.
	oyil = uyarvu, uyarak kutittaṭikkum kummi.
44. u.
	ō - u = uyarakkuṟippu.
	uka = uyar
	uccam = uyarnili.

45. ō.
	ō = poruntu.
(a)	ō + iyam = ōviyam = oppaṉi, cittiram.

48. kaḷ
	kaḷ = karuppu.
(a)	kaḷḷam = karuppu, maṟivu.
	kaḷḷaṉ, kaḷvaṉ = maṟivāyk koḷpavaṉ.
	kaḷavu = maṟivu, tiruṭu.
	kaḷā, kaḷavu = oru karuppup paḻam, paḻukkum maram.
	kaḷi = karuppu maṇ, kaḷi pōṉṟa uṇavu.
	kaḷ = pulaṉi maṟikkum matu. kaḷi = kaṭkuṭiyaṉ, makiḻ.
	kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ = karuppaṉ.
(b)	kāḷam = karuppu.
	kāḷi = karuppāṉa pēyt talivi.
	am + kāḷam (kāḷi) + ammi = aṅkāḷammi.
	kāḷāṉ = karuṅkāḷāṉ.
	kāḷi = kariya erutu, erutu.
(c)	kaḷ - kara = maṟi.
	karavu = kapaṭam, kaḷavu.
49. kaṇ.
	kaḷ - kaṇ. = karuppāṉa viḻi.
(a)	kaṇ = viḻi, kaṇ pōṉṟatu, 7ām vēṟṟumi urupu
	kaṇṇu = maṉatāl pār, karutu.
	kaṇṇiyam = matippu.
	kaṇvāy = ciṟu vāykkāl.
	kaṇṇāḷaṉ, kaṇṇavaṉ, kaṇavaṉ = maṉivikkuk kaṇ 		pōṉṟavaṉ.
	kaṇṇi = kaṇ kaṇṇāyk kaṭṭiya māli.
	kaṇu = kaṇ pōṉṟa variyiṭam.
(b)	kaṇi = aḷaviṭu. kaṇiyaṉ = kaṇippavaṉ, cōtiṭaṉ.
	kaṇitam = kaṇakku. kaṇakku + aṉ = kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkap piḷḷi.
	kaṇakku = varavu celavuk kuṟippu.
	kaṇi - kuṇi = aḷaviṭu.
(c)	kaṇ - kāṇ = pār. kāṭci = aṟivu.
	kāṇi = mēṟpār, mēṟpārkkum nilam, pirivu.
	kāṇam = mēṟpārvi, mēṟpārvi nilam, pirivu.
	kaṇkāṇam = mēṟpārvi. kaṇkāṇi = mēṟpārppavaṉ.
	kaṇkāṇiyār = attiyaṭcar.
	mā + kāṇi = mākāṇi, mā + kāṇam = mākāṇam.
50. karu.
	kaḷ - karu = karuppāku.
(a)	karuppu = karumi, pañcam, pēy.
	karuku = karuppāku, tīntupō.
	karukku = kaṣāyam, paṉimaṭṭiyiṉ kariya ōram, kūr.
	karukal = poruḷ viḷaṅkāmi.
	karumpu = kariya taṇṭuḷḷa taṭṭi.
	karunāṭakam - karunaṭam - kaṉṉaṭam = oru nāṭu, oru moḻi.
(b)	karampu = karumaṇ. karampi = kāynta kaḷimaṇ, oru payaṟu.
(c)	kari = aṭuppuk kari, yāṉi.
	karical = kariya nilam,
	kariccāṉ = karikkuruvi. kāri = kariyatu.
	kariyaṉ = tirumāl, kaṇṇaṉ.
(d)	karu - kaṟu.
	kaṟu = karuppāku, mukaṅkaru, kōpi.
	kaṟuvu = kōpam, varmam, kaṟam = varmam.
51. kala.
	kala = kūṭu.
(a)	kalappu = cērppu, kalappaṭam = iḻikalavi.

	kalampakam - kalampam = kalavi.

(b)	kalakam = caṇṭi. kalāpam = kalakam, caṇṭi.
	kalām = caṇṭi.
(c)	kalaṅku = pala poruḷ kūṭu, mayaṅku.
	kalakkam = mayakkam.
52. kuḷ.
	kuḷḷam, kuḷḷal, kuḷḷi = kuṟumi.
(a)	kuñcu, kuñci = paṟavip piḷḷi.
	kuccu, kucci = ciṟu kampu.
	kuṭṭi = ciṟiyatu, piḷḷi. kuruḷi = kuṭṭi.
	kuṭṭi, kuṇṭu = ciṟu kuḷam; kuḻi = ciṟu vaḷi.
	kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi = kuṟukiyatu.
	kukkal = kuḷḷanāy.
	kuṉṟu = kuṟi, ciṟumali. kuṉṟi = ciṟu muttu.
	kuṉṟu + am = kuṉṟam.
(b)	kuṟu - kuṟaḷ = kuṟu māṉuṭam, kuṟu veṇpā.
	kuṟaḷi = kuṟum pēy.
	kuṟuku = ciṟu, kuṭṭiyāku.
	kuṟukku = kuṟukiya vaḻi, ūṭu, naṭumutuku.
	kuṟi = taṉmi kuṟintatu. kuṟṟam = kuṇak kuṟivu.
	kuṟṟi - kucci = turumpu. kuṟu + il = kuṟṟil - kuccil
	kuṟumpu = kuṟumali, kuṟumaliyaracaṉ, avaṉceyyum 			cēṭṭi, cēṭṭi.
	kuṟumakaṉ - kuṟumāṉ = ciṟuvaṉ.
	kūḻal = kuṟukiyatu.
	kūḻi = kuṟumpacu.
	kūḻi = kuṟukiya muṭi.
53. kum
	kum = kuvi, tiraḷ.
	kum + al = kummal. kummi = ki kuvittāṭum āṭṭam
	kum + ar = kumar. kumar + i = kumari.
	kumar + aṉ = kumaraṉ. kumaraṉ - kumāraṉ.
	kumiḻ, kumiḻi = kuvitta nuri, kuvinta kaṭṭi.
	kumiḻ - cimiḻ - timil.
	kumi + al = kumiyal, kumi - kuvi.
	kuvi - kuviyal, kuvāl, kuvavu.
	kuvi = kuviyal, tiraṭci. kuvi - kuki.
	kuppu - kuppal = kuviyal.
	kuppuṟu = kuvi, talikīḻāku.
	kuppi = kuvinta mūṭi. kuppi = kuviyal, tūcikkuviyal.
	kuppam = kuppikkāṭṭu ūr.
	kuppaṉ = paṭṭik kāṭṭāṉ.
	kumpu = kuvi, kūṭu, kuviya vēku. kumpal = kūṭṭam.
	kumpi = kuvinta vayiṟu.
	kumpiṭu = kikuvi.
	kumpam = kuvinta kuṭam. kumpā = kuvinta pāttiram.
	kūmpu = kuvi, pāymaram. kūppu = ki kuvi.
	kommi = tiraṭci.
54. koḷ.
	koḷ = vāṅku, peṟu, piṭi, mikutiyāyeṭu.
	koḷvaṉi = peṇ koḷḷal.
	koḷḷi = cūṟi, vili.
	koṇṭi = koḷḷi, māṭṭum koṭukku.
	koḷḷi = neruppup piṭitta kaṭṭi, neruppu.
	koḷu = poruṭ kuṟippu.
	koḷuvu = poruttu, māṭṭu.
	koḷuttu = paṟṟavi, poruttu, pukaṭṭu
	koḷi = paṇṇamittal
	koḷ - kōḷ = koḷḷutal, kollutal, piṭittal, 				  peṟutal, karuttu, koḷki, tītu collal.
	kōḷāṟu = koḷḷum vaḻi, ceppañ ceyyumnili, paḻutu.

55. civa
	cival = cennilam. civali = ceṅkāḷi.
(a)	civam = civappu, tīkkaṭavuḷ, civaṉ.

	civam - civaṉ, civi = umi
	civa - tuva - tuvar = civappu, kācukkaṭṭi, ataṉ cuvi.
		tuvari = civanta payaṟu.
(b)	civa - cem.

	cekkar = cevvāṉam. cemmāṉ = cakkiliyaṉ.
	cempu = civanta ulōkam, ataṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nīrppāttiram.
	cemmal = nērmiyuḷḷavaṉ, talivaṉ.
	ceppam = cīrāṉa nili.
	cevvi = ceppam, cīr, cevvi = takunta camiyam.
	cevvaṉ = cevvi.
(c)	civa - cē.
	cēntaṉ = civantavaṉ, murukaṉ.
	cēy = civantavaṉ, kuḻanti, murukaṉ
	cēyōṉ = murukaṉ.
56. cuḷ

57. cur.
	cur = neruppuk kuṟippu.

58. curi

59. cul

60. curu.

61. cuḷi.

62. curuḷ

63. cuṭu.

64. teḷ
	teḷ - teḷḷu = māvit teḷivākku, tūymiyākku.

	tēṉ - tī - tīvu, titti, teviṭṭu
65. tēy.
(a)	tēy - tēyu = tēyntuṇṭākum neruppu.
	tēyvu - tēvu, tēvaṉ, tēvati = nerupput taṉmiyuḷḷa 		teyvam, kaṭavuḷ.
	tēy - teyvu - teyvam.
	tēvu - tē.
(b)	tēy - tī = neruppu. tī - tīmi, tīṅku.

	tīvam - tīpam = viḷakku.
	tī - tīy = cuṇṭu, kāy, vāṭu.
66. paku
	paku = vaku.
(a)	paku + al = pakal = naṭu, naṭuppakal, pakalvēḷi, pirivu.
	pakalōṉ = cūriyaṉ. pakal - pāl = pirivu.
	paku + ai = paki = piḷavu, piriviṉi.
	paku + am = pakkam - pakkal. pakkam = pakuti, titi.
	paku + ti = pakuti - pāti. paku + pu = pakuppu.
	pakku - paṅku.
	pāku - pākam. pākkam = pakkam, ūrppakuti.
	pāku - pāṅku - pāṅkar. pāṅku = pakkam, taṉmi.
	pāṅku + aṉ = pāṅkaṉ.
(b)	paku - pā. pātti = pakukkappaṭṭa ceyp pākam. pātīṭu = pakuttal.
(c)	paku - vaku. vaku + ai = vaki. vaku + pu = vakuppu.
(d)	pakir - vakir.
67. peḷ
	peḷ = virumpu, kātali.
	peṭpu = viruppam.
	peḷ - peṇ = virumpappaṭum pāl.
	peḷ + ti = peṭṭi - peṭi - pēṭi - pēṭu
	pēṭu + aṉ = pēṭaṉ = āṇṭaṉmiyuḷḷa peṇ.
	pēṭu + i = pēṭi = peṇṭaṉmiyuḷḷa āṇ.
	peṇ - piṇā - piṇavu - piṇaval. piṇā - piṇi.
	peṇ - pēṇ = virumpu, virumpip pātukā.
68. poḷ
	poḷ = tuḷiyiṭu.
	poṇṭāṉ = eli pottuk kiḷampum vaḷi.
(a)	poḷi = veṭṭu, varappu.
	poḷ - pol.
	pol + am = pollam = oṭṭi.
	poḷḷāp piḷḷiyār = pollāp piḷḷiyār.
	pokku = tuḷiyuḷḷatu, uḷḷīṭaṟṟa tāṉiyam, poy
		pokkuvāy = pallaṟṟa vāy
	pokku + aṇam = pokkaṇam = pi.
	poy = uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu, meyyallātatu.
	po = tuḷiyiṭu. pottal = tuḷi.
	poḷ - pōḻ = piḷa, veṭṭu.
	poḻil = veṭṭappaṭuvatu, cōli.
(b)	pōḻ = veṭṭu, tuṇṭu. pōḻtu = iruḷip piḷakkum cūriyaṉ.
	pōḻtu - poḻutu - pōtu = vēḷi.
69. pō
(a)	pō = cel. pōtu = pō. pōku = pō.
	pōkku = cellal, ātaravu.
	pōkku - pōṅku = pōkum muṟi, mātiri.
(b)	pōtum = cellum, vēṇṭiya aḷavākum.
	pōtiya, pōnta = aḷavāṉa, pōtumāṉa.
	pōku = nīḷ.
	pōtu = virinta arumpu.
	pōnti = virinta paṉi ōli.
	pōnti - pottakam - puttakam.
70. vaḷ
	vaḷ = vaḷi.
	vaḷḷam = vaṭṭak kalam.
	vaḷākam = cūḻnta iṭam, vaḷār = vaḷinta pirampu.
	vaḷi = vaḷiyal, vaḷinta caṅku, vaḷinta uttaram, 				vaṭṭat tuvāram.
	vaḷi + al = vaḷiyal, vaḷi + vi = vaḷivi.
	vaḷi + am = vaḷiyam.
	vaṭṭam = vaḷiyam, vaṭṭakkācu, vaṭṭi, pakuti.
	vaṭṭi = vaṭṭakkācu, kaṭaṉukkuc celuttum kācu, vaḷinta peṭṭi.
	vaṭṭil = vaṭṭak kalam.
	vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa cil.
	vaṭṭaki, vaṭṭāram = iṭappakuti.
	vaṇaṅku = uṭampu vaḷi.
	vaḻaṅku = vaḷintu koṭu
	vaṇar = vaḷinta yāḻuṟuppu.
	vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku. vaṅki = vaḷintatu.
	vaṇṭi = vaṭṭac cakkaram, cakkarattiyuṭiya cakaṭam.
	vaṇṭu = vaḷiyal, vaṭṭamāṉa vaṇṭu.
	vaṇṭi - paṇṭi - pāṇṭi - pāṇṭil.
	pāṇṭi = vaṭṭāṭal. pāṇṭil = vaṭṭak kiṇṇam, 				uruṭciyāṉa erutu.
	pāṇṭiyaṉ = vīraṉ.
	vaḷi = vaḷintu vīcum kāṟṟu.
	vāḷi = vaḷiyam, vaḷinta piṭi, vaḷintuviḻum ampu
	vāṇam = vaḷiyum veṭivaki. vāṇam - pāṇam = ampu.
71. veḷ
	veḷ = veḷḷiyāku.
	veḷi = veḷḷiyāṉa iṭam. veṭṭa = veḷḷiyāṉa.
	veḷḷāḷaṉ = veṇkaḷamaṉ.
	veḷḷāṭṭi = veḷḷāḷappeṇ, vēlikkāri.
	veḷḷari = veṇkōṭuḷḷa kāy.
	veḷḷi = veḷḷit tuṇi, cuṇṇāmpu, kaḷḷamaṟṟavaṉ.
	veḷḷanti = kaḷḷamiṉmi.
	veḷḷam = veḷḷiyāṉa putu nīr.
	veḷḷi = veḷḷiyāṉa ulōkam, naṭcattiram.
	veḷḷili - veṟṟili.
	veḷḷil - viḷa - viḷā - viḷavu = veḷḷōṭuḷḷa paḻamaram.
	veḷil - veḷiṟu = veḷḷimaram.
	veḷu = veḷḷiyākku, tuvi, aṭi.
	veḷku - veṭku = nāṇattāl mukam veḷu.
	viḷaṅku = oḷiviṭu, pulaṉāku.
	viḷakku = viḷaṅkac ceyvatu.
72. vēku.
	vē = vēku, eri.
	vēkam = virivu, kaṭumi.
	venti = venta kīri.
	vēmpu = cūṭāṉa paḻam paḻuppatu. allatu vēṉilil taḻippatu.
	vēṉal = veppam, vēṉil = kōṭi.
	vekki = vēṉal, veppu = cūṭu, veppu - veppam.
	vempu = veyiliṟ kāy. vempal = kāyntapaḻam.
	vētu = cūṭu, ottaṭam, vetuvetuppu = cūṭu.
	vetumpu = cuṭu, vētaṉi = nōvu.
	vemmi = cūṭu, kaṭumi, viruppam.
	veyyil - veyil = veppamāṉa au
73. vēḷ
	vēḷ = virumpu.
	vēḷ - vēṇṭu = virumpu, keñcik kēḷ.
	vēḷ - vēṇ + avā = vēṇavā.
	vēṭki = viruppam, tākam, viṭāy = viruppam, tākam.
	vēḷvi = oṉṟi virumpic ceyyum yākam.
	vēḷāṇmi = virumpic ceyyum upacāram.
	vēḷāḷaṉ = uḻavaṉ, vēḷir = oru kulat talivaṉ, 				kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ.
	vēḷāṇ = uḻavar kuṭi. vēḷāṉ = oru paṭṭam
	vēḷāṭṭi = vēḷāḷap peṇ. vēḷam = vēḷāḷap peṇṭir 			ciṟikkaḷam.
	vēṇmakaṉ - vēṇmāṉ = kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ.
vēṭṭam, vēṭṭi = virumpi vilaṅkip piṭittal. 
vēṭṭuvaṉ, vēṭuvaṉ, vēṭaṉ = vēṭṭiyāṭupavaṉ.
2
pōliki yuruppaṭikaḷ
1. a (eḻuttum collum colluṟuppum kuṟiyum)
1. eḻuttu
a1	a. pe. n. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ mutaleḻuttu; first letter of the Tamil alphabet.
vaki. uyirkkuṟil : short vowel.
colvaki : aṅkāttalāl oru māttiri yaḷavolikkum aḵṟiṇic cevippulap poruḷākiya taṟcuṭṭolip peyar.
vēṟṟumippāṭu. 2ām vaki, 3ām pirivu. e-ṭu : avvi, avvāl, avviṟku, avviṉ, avvatu, avvil.
2. col
a2   a. pe; n. 1 aḻaku; beauty. "avvāy vaḷarpiṟi cūṭi" (perum pāṇ. 412), 2. civaṉ, Siva. "āru maṟiyā rakāra mavaṉeṉṟu" (tiruman. 1751). 3. tirumāl Vishnu "av veṉ coṟporuḷāvāṉ" (pākava. cicupā. 20). 4. nāṉ mukaṉ, Brahma. (takka yākap. 65, uri), 5. cukku; dried ginger (pari. aka.) 6. tippili; long pepper. (pari. aka.)
a3   a. pe. e; adj. puṟaccuṭṭu: demon. prefix: a = anta. 1. cēymic cuṭṭu; pref. to nouns, expressing remoteness. e-ṭu: ap piyaṉ. 2. muṟpeyarc cuṭṭu; pref. to nouns, referring to their antecedents. e-ṭu : paṇṭit tamiḻa katti āṇṭavar cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar. am mūvēn tarum iṉṟilli. 3. ulakaṟi cuṭṭu; pref. expressing world-wide eminence. 'attam perumāṉ' (cīvaka. 221)
3. colluṟuppu (i; ind)
a4   a, akaccuṭṭu: demon. base : mutaṉili - 1. cēymic cuṭṭu; base of the dem. pron. expressing the remote person or thing
	   	e-ṭu : avaṉ, aṅku, aṉṟu. 2. muṟpeyarc cuṭṭu; 		base of the dem. pron. referring to its antecedent. 		e-ṭu : ōrūril ōraracaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉukku iru 		makkaḷ. 3. ulakaṟi cuṭṭu: base of the dem. pron. 		expressing world-wide eminence. e-ṭu: "avaṉaṉṟi 		ōr aṇuvum aciyātu" (paḻamoḻi). 4. potu 			niliccuṭṭu: base of the dem. pron. expressing 			generality or indefiniteness.  e-ṭu "avaravar 			akkaṟikku avaravar pāṭupaṭuvār" (paḻamoḻi).
a5		a. iṉmi yaṉmi maṟutalip poruḷil varum muṉ ṉoṭṭu pref. implying negation, privation or contrariety.
	e-ṭu : iṉmi - avalam = valam illātatu 			(tuṉpam), tuyaram.
		aṉmi - akālam = kālam allātatu.
		maṟutali - acuraṉ = curaṉukku māṟāṉavaṉ.
a6   	a. oru coṟcāriyi; a euphonic augment.
	   	e-ṭu : vaṇṇārappēṭṭi.
a7   	a. meyyeḻuttuc cāriyi; an enunciative augment.
	e-ṭu:
	"meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum"	(tol. 46)
	"valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa"	(tol. 19)
a8   	a. ōr aciccol; an expletive generally in poetry.
	e-ṭu : "taṉvaḻiya kāḷi" (cīvaka.494).
a9   	a rakarattil toṭaṅkum cila camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉoṭṭu; euphonic prothesis of some sanskrit words beginning with ra. e-ṭu: ratna - arataṉam.
a10  	irakkam, viyappu, tuyaram mutaliyaṉa uṇarttum oru kuṟippuc colluṟuppu: a part of an interjection expressing pity, wonder, grief etc.
"uṇṇā ṇoḷiniṟā ṉōṅku pukaḻceyyāṉ
tuṉṉaruṅ kēḷir tuyarkaḷiyāṉ - koṉṉē
vaḻaṅkāṉ poruḷkāt tiruppāṉēl a ā
iḻantāṉeṉ ṟeṇṇap paṭum"	(nālaṭi. 9)
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil 'a ā' irakkap poruḷil vantatu.
a11		a. oru palaviṉpāṟ peyarīṟu; a neut. pl. noun suf.
		e-ṭu: cila pala.
a12	a. āṟām vēṟṟumi aḵṟiṇip paṉmi yurupu genitive neut. pl. ending e-ṭu: eṉa kikaḷ.
a13	a. oru palaviṉpāl viṉimuṟṟīṟu; a neut. pl. verbal ending. e-ṭu: vantaṉa. vanta. periyaṉa. periya.
a14	a. oru viyaṅkōḷ viṉi yīṟu; an opt. verbal ending. e-ṭu: varappuyara. viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyellām muṟṟē.
a15	a. iṟantakāla nikaḻkālat terinilip peyarecca vīṟu a rel. participial ending. e-ṭu: vanta, varukiṉṟa.
a16	a. oru (kuṟippup) peyarecca vīṟu; an adj. ending. e-ṭu, nalla, ciṟiya.
a17	a. oru nikaḻkāla (etirkālap poruṭṭu) viṉiyecca vīṟu; an infinitive suf. e-ṭu: uṇṇa.
a18	a. oru collākka īṟu; formative particle.
e-ṭu: kul-kal-kala, piḷ-piḷa.
a19	a. cila peyarkaḷiṉ ākāra vīṟṟuk kuṟukkam; shortening of 'an' ending certin nouns e-ṭu: irā-ira, kaḷā-kaḷa, nilā-nila, puṟā-puṟa.
a20	a. kuṟilil iṟum vaṇṇappāṭṭuc cīrkaḷiṉ allatu aci kaḷiṉ vaṇṇak kuḻippu vāypāṭṭīṟu; ending of rhythmatic patterns of metrical feet and syllebles ending in a short vowel. e-ṭu: taṉa, taṉaṉa, tāṉa, tatta, tanta, tayya, taṉatta, taṉanta.
a21	a. neṭuṅkaṇakkup peyaruṟuppu: part of a name of the alphabet. 'aā' = neṭuṅkaṇakku. e-ṭu: uṉakku 'aā' teriyumā? iti 'a'ṉa 'ā'vaṉṉā eṉṟatu paṇṭi vaḻakku.
a22	a. ukaramutal tiripu collākkattil ukaramutal akara mutalākat tirivatuṇṭu. e-ṭu: uki (celuttu) - aki, kuṭṭi - kaṭṭi, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu, kuṭi(tal) - kaṭi(tal), curi (vaḷiyal) - cari, tuḷir - taḷir, tuṇi- taṉi (ettuṇi-ettaṉi, varuntuṇiyum - varuntaṉiyum), nuri (veṇmi) - nari, puri - pari(vaḷi), puram (uyarnta iṭam allatu kaṭṭiṭam, e-ṭu: paraveḷi, kōpuram) - param - varam -varaṉ (mēlāṉa vīṭṭunilam), muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku, muṭi - maṭi (iṟa) muyaṅku - mayaṅku.
it tiripiṉāl, cila coṟkaḷiṉ mūlattiyum cila coṟkaḷip puṉiyum vakiyiyum aṟiyalām.
4. kuṟi
a23	a.1. eṭṭu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kuṟiyāka 'a' cuḻiyiṉṟi eḻutappaṭuvatu; symbol of the number eight, written without the loop.
	2. 	aimpuḷḷuḷ (pañcapaṭciyuḷ) oṉṟāy vallūṟṟik kuṟikkum eḻuttu; letter representing the vulture one of the five astrologically important birds.
a24	a. moḻi mutal eḻuttukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. e-ṭu: avaṉ
varalāṟu
1. eḻuttu :	ā-a. akaram ākārattiṉ kuṟukkam. uyi rolikaḷellām perumpālum iyaṟkiyāka neṭilākavē tōṉṟip piṉpu kuṟukiṉa. itaṉ viḷakkatti neṭuṅ kaṇakku eṉṉum urup paṭiyiṟ kāṇka.
2. col :	am = aḻaku. am-a2.
a2 = eḻuttukaṭku mutalākiya akaram pōl ulakaṅkaṭku mutalākiya iṟivaṉ.
"akara mutala veḻuttellā māti
ðèõù¢ ºîø¢«ø »ô°."	(kuṟaḷ. 1)
iṟivaṉik kuṟiñci nilattuc cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉar civaṉ eṉṟum mulli nilattu māyōṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉar tirumāl eṉṟum, kuṟittaṉar. ki.mu. 1500-ām āṇṭupōl nāvalan tēyattiṟkuṭ pukunta ciṟu teyva vēḷvimata āriyar, civaneṟiyum tirumāl neṟiyum ākiya iru tūya tamiḻ mataṅkaḷiyum āriyappaṭuttaṟku muttirumēṉi (tirimūrtti)k koḷkiyip pukutti, nāṉmukaṉ (piramaṉ) eṉṉum paṭipput teyvattip putuvatākap paṭittu, at teyvattiyum akarattāṟ kuṟittaṉar.
cukku, tippili eṉṉum maruttuvac carakkukaḷ, ētēṉumoru karaṇiyam paṟṟi akaram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntirukkalām, atip piṟkālattār 'a' (a2) eṉṟu kuṟittirukkalām.
(puṟaccuṭṭu ā = anta. "āyīriyala" eṉṟu (tol. 17) ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, āyāḷ (maliyāḷam), āvūru (teluṅku) eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkilum, ākāram collākavē vaḻaṅkivarutal kāṇka.
ā - a3.
3. colluṟuppu
ā(akaccuṭṭu)-a4 . ā-āku-āṅku-aṅku, ātu-atu.
al - a5. ō.nā. nal - na.
a6	(coṟcāriyi) peyarecca vīṟā yirukkalām.
a7	(eḻuttuc cāriyi) neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ mutaṉmiyum paluk keḷimiyum paṟṟiyatu.
a8	(aciccol) kuṟippuc peyarecca vīṟāyirukkalām. acic coṟkaḷellām poruḷ kuṉṟiya allatu iḻanta coṟkaḷē. 
a9 (ayaṟcol muṉṉoṭṭu) palukkeḷimiyum āṭciyum paṟṟiyatu. palukku=uccarippu
a10	(irakkak kuṟippu) iyaṟki paṟṟiyatu. ā - a. o.nā : E. ha. hah.
a11	(palaviṉpāṟ peyarīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a12	(āṟām vēṟṟumi aḵṟiṇip paṉmiyurupu) palaviṉ pāṟ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟīṟu. kikaḷ eṉa- eṉa kikaḷ (coṉ muṟi māṟṟu).
a13	(palaviṉpāl viṉimuṟṟīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a14	(viyaṅkōḷ viṉimuṟṟīṟu) allīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyariṉ īṟukēṭu. ceyyal - ceyya ceyal - ceya.

ivaṟṟil toḻiṟpeyarē viyaṅkōḷāy niṟṟal kāṇka.
a15 (iṟantakāla nikaḻkālat terinilip peyareccavīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
cey = ceyki. cey + atu = ceyyatu - ceytu = ceykiyu ṭiya - vaṉ - vaḷ - var - tu - vi. ceykiyuṭiyavaṉ - ceytavaṉ = ceytāṉ. viṉiyuṭimi viṉi muṭintamiyiyuṇarttum.
uṇṭu eṉṉum oṉṟaṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉimuṟṟu, iṉṟu irutiṇi īreṇ mūviṭa aimpāṟ potuvāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, atu eṉṉum cuṭṭuc colli īṟākak koṇṭa ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉic collum, mutaṟkālattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
uḷ + atu = uḷḷatu. uḷ + tu (atu) = uṇṭu.
ceytu + a = ceyta = ceyta - vaṉ - vaḷ - var - atu - vi āṉa anta.
ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccol mutaṟkālattil muṟṟu viṉiyākavum, piṉṉar muṟṟu viṉiyum eccamumākavum vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ aimpālīṟu peṟṟa kālan toṭaṅki eccamākavē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. ceytu eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉiccol pōṉṟē, ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉiccollum akaravīṟu peṟṟup peyareccamākum.
ceykiṉṟu + a = ceykiṉṟa - ceykiṟa.
ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉic col eccap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
viṉiccol vakikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ iṭinilikaḷum, īṟukaḷum, avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷil virivāka viḷakkappeṟum.
a16   (kuṟippup peyareccavīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a17   (nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccavīṟu) allīṟṟut toḻiṟ peya riṉ īṟukēṭṭāl nērntatu.
ceyyal (vēṇṭum) - ceyya (vēṇṭum).

(tol. eḻut. toki. 14)
a18	poruḷ vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiyatu.
a19	palukkeḷimi nōkkiyatu.

4. kuṟi
a23	(eṭṭeṉṉum eṇkuṟiyum vallūṟṟiṉ kuṟiyum)
eṇvakippaṭṭa poruḷiṉ allatu poruṭṭokutiyiṉ peyar mutaleḻuttākavum, vallūṟṟiṉ peyar oṉṟaṉ mutaleḻut tākavum irukkalām. irupeyarum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa pōlum.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
eḻuttukkaḷi yellām uyirum meyyum eṉak kūṟupaṭutti yum, uyireḻuttukkaḷiyum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiyum piṟappum oliyum paṟṟi muṟippaṭuttiyum, uyirukkum meykkum pōṉṟē uyirmey kaṭkum vēṟu varivaṭivamittum, vaṇṇamāli (alphabet) mutaṉ mutalāka amikkap peṟṟatu tamiḻilēyē. uyirum meyyum maṭṭuṅ koṇṭatu kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, avaṟṟoṭu uyirmeyyuṅ koṇ ṭatu neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum. iruvakik kaṇakkilum akaramē tamiḻiṉ mutaleḻuttām.
vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta piṉṉum, nīṇṭakālam eḻuttiṉṟit tam mutaṉūlākiya vētatti vāymoḻiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantataṉāl, aḵtu 'eḻutāk kiḷavi' eṉappaṭṭatu. tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉpē, mutaliṟ kiranta veḻuttiyum piṉpu tēvanākari yiyum 'avar' amittuk koṇṭaṉar. iṟṟit tamiḻeḻuttiṟkum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttiṟkum yātoru toṭarpu milli. itaṉ viḷakkatti 'neṭuṅkaṇakku' eṉṉum uruppaṭiyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
ki.mu. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tiruvaḷḷuvar 'akaramutala tamiḻ eḻuttellām' eṉṉātu "akara mutala eḻuttellām" eṉṟu potup paṭak kūṟi yiruppatāl, teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻeḻuttōṭu ataṉ vaḻip paṭṭa kiranta veḻuttiyum, vaṭanāṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttiyum avar aṟintiruttal vēṇṭum.
'kuvā kuvā'veṉṟu aḻuvatākac collappaṭum kuḻavi  vāyiṉ mutaloliyi 'aā, aā' eṉṟu aḻum oliyākavum koḷḷalā meṉiṉum, atu muḻittal moḻiyic (inarticulate speech) cērnta voliyākalāṉ iḻittal moḻiyic (articulate speech) cērnta aā voliyākātu. ata ṉālēyē akara mutala moḻiyellām eṉṉātu "akara mutala eḻuttellām" eṉak kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar. piṉīciyam, epirēyam, arapi, kirēkkam, ilattīṉam, āṅkilam mutaliya mēli moḻikaḷiṉ kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum akaramē mutaleḻuttām. āṅkila akaram  tamiḻakaratti yottu nērākavum olikkum; ati yovvātu kōṇiyākavum olikkum. ak kōṇiyoli, vaṭārk kāṭṭu āmpūr makkaḷ kāy eṉṉum colliluḷḷa ākārattiyo lippatu pōṉṟatu. avvoli centamiḻiṟ koḷḷappaṭātu. eḻuttiṟ kuriya nāṉilikaḷuḷ iṟutinili yaṭinta ellā moḻiyeḻuttuk kaḷum, akarattiyē mutalāka vuṭiyaṉa. 
ceṉṉip pa. ka. ka. ta. akaramutalic cīrtiruttam
ce.pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutali,
(1)	vaṭacol muṉvit teḻutappaṭum akara muṉṉoṭṭiṟku raṅkam - araṅkam eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyum.
(2) 	iṉmi aṉmi maṟutalip poruḷil varum akaratti 'na' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi avviyayattiṉ tiripeṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟku muṟiyē arūpam, appirāmaṇaṉ, atarmam eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyum uḷḷatu.
araṅkam eṉṉum col aṟukkappaṭṭatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyik kuṟik kum tūya teṉcol eṉṟum, srīraṅkam eṉa vaḻaṅkum tirunakariṉ paḻampeyar tiru varaṅkam eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭivē yeṉṟum, araṅkam eṉṉum teṉ collē vaṭamoḻiyil raṅka eṉat tirinta teṉṟum, araṅkam eṉṉum uruppaṭiyil veḷḷiṭi maliyeṉa viḷakkappeṟum.
al eṉṉum teṉcollē, aṉ eṉattirintu vaṭamoḻiyil 'na' eṉa ilakkaṇap pōliyāka māṟiyuḷḷati, al eṉṉum urup paṭiyiṟ kaṇṭu teḷika.
iṉi, akarac cāriyikku eṭuttukkāṭṭiya 'tamiḻap piḷḷi' eṉṉum kūṭṭuc colliyum tamiḻ + piḷḷi eṉap pakukkātu tamiḻaṉ + piḷḷi 
yeṉap pakuttu, nilimoḻiyīṟu keṭṭu varumoḻi mutal valimikka puṇarcciyākak koḷvatē takkatām eṉavum aṟika. 
ittakiya maṟuppellām, iṉivarum uruppaṭikaḷil tiruttam eṉṉum talippiṉ kīḻēyē kuṟikkappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttuc cāriyi. oru collākka īṟu, cilapeyarkaḷiṉ ākāra vīṟṟuk kuṟukkam, vaṇṇak kuḻippu vāypāṭṭīṟu, neṭuṅ kaṇakkup peyaruṟuppu eṉṉum aimporuḷum ceṉṉi yakara mutaliyiṟ kuṟikkap peṟavilli.
2. avari
avari avari.
vaḻaṅkum iṭam - tamiḻakam.
col vaki : aḵṟiṇi ōraṟivuyirp poruṭpeyar.
iyal viḷakkam - perumpāṉmi pacci niṟamum ciṟupāṉmi veṇṇiṟamum cenniṟamuḷḷatum, perumpālum taṭṭi vaṭivāṉatum, iru viralam (inches) mutal aṟuviralam vari nīṇṭu pala vakippaṭṭi ruppatum, vīṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil viḷivikkappaṭuvatum, naḷi (kārttiki) cili (mārkaḻi) cuṟava (ti) mātaṅkaḷil ūr koṭiyākavum (creeper) ciṟappāka ivar koṭiyākavum (climber) paṭarkoṭiyiṟ kāyppatum kaṟi vakiyākac camikkap paṭuvatum uṭal nalattiṟkēṟṟatum, cuviyuḷ ḷatumāṉa kāy vaki a kind of bean including many varieties.
maṟupeyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkum iṭamum:

vēṟṟumippāṭu. mutalvaki. e-ṭu : avariyi, avariyāl, avarikku, avariyiṉ, avariyatu, avariyil.

collamivu
'am' vēr allatu aṭi mutaṉili. 'aval', amal eṉpataṉ tiripāki mēṉ mutaṉiliyāka niṟkum toḻilākupeyar. 'ai' paṇpi yīṟu. lakaram rakaramāṉatu pōlit tiripu.
col varalāṟu :
ammutal = amuṅkutal, amukkutal.
am - ammi = amukki yarikkuṅ kal.
am - (amu) - (amuku) - amuṅku - amukku - amukkam
amukku - amukkal - amukkalāṉ = koppuḷaṅkaḷi amuṅkac ceyyum taḻi.
am - amiḻ - āḻ - aḻuntu - aḻuttu - aḻuttam.
amukkuvataṉālum aḻuttuvataṉālum ōriṭam paḷḷamākum; oru poruḷ taṭṭiyākum.
am - amal - aval = 1. paḷḷam. "Üõô¤ö¤ò¤Âñ¢ ñ¤¬ê «òø¤Âñ¢." (puṟam. 102 : 3). 2. viḷinilam. 3. kuḷam. 4. taṭṭiyāka iṭitta nellarici allatu kamparici. paḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu kuḷḷak karuttun tōṉṟum.
o.nā : paḷ - paḷḷam. paḷ - paḷḷi = 1. kuḷḷam. 2. paḷḷiyāṭu. paḷḷiyaṉ = kuṟukit taṭittavaṉ. paḷḷiyāṭu = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭu. kuḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu cappik karuttut tōṉṟum.
ñ - õ., pōli o.nā : cemmi - cevvi.
cuma - cumal - cuval = cumakkun tōṭpaṭṭi.
aval - (avali) - avari = cappiyāṉa allatu taṭṭi yāṉa kāyvaki.
o.nā : ayil - ayili - ayiri.
la - ra, pōli.
avari vakikaḷiṟ cila uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppiṉum, perum pāṉmi nōkki avi vilakkākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum, piñcu niliyilēyē camikkumāṟu maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatāl, ellā vakikaḷum piñcu niliyil cappiyākavē yiruttali nōkkuka.
ovvoru poruḷum ataṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟiyē peyar peṟuvatu marapu. vāḻi, kattari, muruṅki, veṇṭi, pūcaṇi, curi, pīrkku, puṭali, pākal mutaliya piṟa kāyvakikaḷ ellāvaṟṟōṭum oppunōkki, avari yoṉṟē taṭṭiyā yiruttalik kaṇṭaṟika.
paḷḷam eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ mēlmaṭṭattiṉ tāḻvu; kuṭṭi eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uyarattiṉ tāḻvu; cappi eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ tiṇ ṇattiṉ allatu puṭippiṉ 
tāḻvu. im muk karuttum ōriṉappaṭṭaṉa. āḻamillāta kiṇṇam taṭṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
poruḷvaki :
(1)	āṭṭuk kompavari = āṭṭuk kompupōl vaṭi vuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles goat's horn in shape
(2)	ārāl mīṉavari = ārāl mīṉ pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles sand - eel in shape.
(3)	āṉik kātavari = āṉik kātu pōl vaṭivuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that resembles elephant's ear in shape.
(4)	kaṇuvavari = koṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷiṟ kāykkum avari; kind of bean that brings forth fruits at joints also.
(5)	koḻuppavari = kuṭṭiyākak koḻutta catiyuḷḷa avari; kind of bean that is short and fleshy.
(6)	kōḻiyavari (kōḻikkā lavari) = kōḻi viral pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avari; canavalia gladiata
(7)	civappavari - cevvavari = cenniṟa avari; a red variety of bean, lablab cultratus.
(8)	ciṟṟavari - maṇiyavari = a small variety of bean.
(9)	tīvāntara vavari = kīḻit tīviṉiṉṟu vanta avari; a bean from the eastern islands (w).
(10)	nakaravari - dolichos rugosus (W)
(11)	pālavari - veḷḷavari; a white variety of bean,
"avarik koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarkkāy
«è£ì¢ðî ñ£è."		(puṟam. 120: 10-11)
(12)	pēravari - a large variety of bean.
(13)	muṟukkavari = tirukal muṟukalākavuḷḷa avari; psopha carpus tetragonolobus.
iṉapporuḷkaḷ
(1)	kappal avari = french beans
(2)	kāṭṭavari = koḻuppavari pōṉṟatāyuḷḷa mocci (cēlam vaḻakku); wild bean, lablab vulgaris.
vīṭṭup puṟattil viḷivikkappaṭum avariyi nōkki mocci kāṭ ṭavari eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, avarikku vīṭṭavari eṉa ōr aṭimoḻip peyar peṟappaṭum.
vīṭṭavarikkum kāṭṭavarikkum vēṟupāṭu. 
	vīṭṭavari	kāṭṭavari
(1)	vīṭṭup puṟattil	kāṭṭup puṟattil
	viḷikkappaṭuvatu	viḷikkappaṭuvatu.
(2)	koṭiyākap paṭarvatu	kuttuc ceṭiyāka vaḷarvatu.

	camikkappaṭuvatu	tiṉṉappaṭuvatu.
(4)	tōl melliyatu	tōl valliyatu.
(3) ayalnāṭṭu (cīmi) avari = marutaṅkāy pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatum nīṇṭatumāṉa veḷināṭṭuk kāyvaki; a kind of foreign vegetable. ayal nāṭṭi allatu mēl nāṭṭip potu makkaḷ 'cīmi' eṉṉum vaṭacollāṟ kuṟippar. iṅku vaṭacol eṉṟatu vaṭanāṭṭuc colli.
(4) cīṉiyavari - kottavari - kuttavari = kottuk kottākak kāykkum kāyvaki; a species of pulse whose fruits are in bunches.
(5) cuṭaliyavari = cuṭukāṭṭup pakkattil iyaṟkiyāka viḷivatum, cīṉiyavari pōṉṟatum, uṇṇap paṭātatumāṉa kāy vaki; a species of inedible bean growing wildly near burial or burning grounds. itu pēyavari yeṉavum paṭum.
(6) pāṭavari - vāḷavari - tampaṭṭavari - cāṭṭavari = vāḷpōl nīṇṭu paṭṭiyāka virukkum kāyvaki; sword-bean, canavalia ensiformis.

avarik kāy eṉpatu maliyāḷattil avarakka eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. amarakka eṉpatu avarakka eṉpataṉ tirivu.
tuḷuc coṟkaḷuḷ rakaramuḷḷavi oruvakiyiyum ṭakaramuḷ ḷavi maṟṟoru vakiyiyum kuṟippaṉavākap parō emaṉō tiraviṭa akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tuḷu akaramutaliyil aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭavilli.
iṇimoḻi:
avari tuvari.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
"avari koyyuna rāra māntum"	(puṟam. 215: 5).
"ciṟukoṭik koḷḷē poṟikiḷa ravariyoṭu"	(puṟam. 335:5)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷ, paṇṭik kālattil avariyum kāṭṭil, moccipōla viḷikkappaṭṭa teṉṟō, mocciyum avari yeṉap paṭṭateṉṟō, karuta iṭantarum.
koṭṭiyavari yeṉṟu patārtta kuṇa cintāmaṇi kuṟitti ruppatu. oru taṉi vakiyākat teriyavilli.
cīrtiruttam:
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka tamiḻ akaramutali, kōḻiyavariyi vāḷavari (sword-bean) yeṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷatu.
ārāl mīṉavari, āṉikkātavari, koḻuppavari, cuṭali avari mutaliya peyarkaḷ atiṟ kuṟikkappeṟa villi.
mēl nāṭṭut tamiḻaṟiñar tam aṟiyāmiyāl avarikkāy eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa avarakka, amarakka eṉṉum coṟkaḷiyum avariyik kuṟikkum taṉic coṟkaḷākak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
3. iñci
col : iñci
vaḻakkiṭam :	iñci1 - ilakkiyam
	iñci2 - tamiḻakam
colvaki : peyarccol
iñci1 = kaṭṭiṭa vaṭivāṉa iṭappeyar
iñci2 = aḵṟiṇi ōraṟivuyirp poruṭpeyar.
vēṟṟumippāṭṭu vaki.
mutalvaki (cāriyi yillatu), e-ṭu: iñciyi, iñciyāl, iñcikku, iñciyiṉ, iñciyatu, iñciyil.
iyal viḷakkam
iñci1 - cempurukkic cāntāka vārttu iṟukak kaṭṭiya tiṇ ṇiya kōṭṭi matil vaki.
iñci2 - pittattip pōkkuvatum, maruntukaḷilum kaṟivaki kaḷilum perumpālum kūṭṭuc carakkākac cērkkappaṭuvatum, kārppuc cuviyuḷḷatumāṉa, kiḻaṅkuḷḷa pūṇṭuvaki.
poruḷum āṭci mēṟkōḷum
iñci1, pe. tiṇṇiya kōṭṭi matil vaki
"koṭuṅka ṇiñci"	(patiṟṟup. 16:1).
"uyarvakalan tiṇmi yarumiyin nāṉkiṉ
amivaraṇ eṉṟurikku nūl"	(743)
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kuṟittuḷḷa tiṇmi eṉṉum vakippaṭi. "cempupuṉin tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum purici" yē (puṟam. 201) iñci eṉṉum matil vakiyākum.
iñci2, pe. 1. iñcip pūṇṭu; ginger-plant. "mañcaḷum iñciyum mayaṅkaril valayattu" (cilap 10:74). 2. iñcik kiḻaṅku; ginger-root.
āṭci mēṟkōḷ :
iñci1 - "cempiṭṭuc ceyta viñcit tirunakarc celvan tēṟi" (kamparā. yutta. 160) 
ce.co.pi. akaramutaliyil iṉṉum eṇ mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭap peṟum virivañci avi iṅku viṭappaṭṭuḷa.
iñci vaki:
iñci māṅkāy allatu māṅkāyiñci. ūṟukāyākap payaṉpaṭuvatu; ayapēṭi - pipēnaca.
kūṭṭuc col:
iñcik kiḻaṅku pe. ginger-root.
iñcittēṟu, pe. iñcit tuṇṭu; small piece of green ginger.
iñcippāku, pe. iñci iḷakiya (lēkiya) vaki; a kind of ginger electuary.
iñcip pāvi, pe. iñcik kiḻaṅku (malipaṭu. 125, uri). ginger, as bearing the shape of a doll.
iñci yūṟukāy = kāyac carakkuc cērttu eṇṇeyil ūṟa vitta iñci, ginger-pickle
iñci vēr, pe. iñcik kiḻaṅku ginger - root.
iñcic 'curacam' eṉpati iñcikkarukku eṉṟum, iñci 'murappā' eṉpati iñci vaṭippu eṉṟum, collalām.
marapu vaḻakku : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅkupōṟ pañcarittal(tontaravu ceytal).
toṭarmoḻi : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅku.
uvamip paḻamoḻi : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅku pōla.
collamivu :
iñci (iraṇṭiṟkum potu. 'iḷ' vēr; 'iñcu' mutaṉili; 'i' viṉimutalīṟu.
col varalāṟu:
iñci1 : ul - ol. ollutal = poruntutal. ul - ur - uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal. ceṟital, valiyuṟutal. uṟu - uṟuti = tiṇṇam, valimi.
ur -uram = valimi uṟu - uṟa. uṟattal = ceṟital, iṟukutal.
"viṟappum uṟappum veṟuppum ceṟivē" (tol-col.348).
uṟanta viñci = iṟukiya matil.
àô¢ - à÷¢ - Ü÷¢ = ªêø¤¾ (î¤õ£.), vaṉmi (cūṭā.)
aḷḷal = nerukkam. aḷḷākutal = ceṟital.
aḷḷiruḷ = ceṟinta iruṭṭu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital (cīvaka. 614).
uḷ - iḷ - (iy) - (iyñcu) - iñcu.
o. nō:	kuḷ - (kuy) - (kuyñcu) - kuñcu.
	puḷ - piḷ - piy - (piyñcu) - piñcu.
	koḷ - koy. toḷ - toy. poḷ - poy. 
iñcutal = ceṟital, iṟukutal, tiṇital.
iñcu - iñci = tiṇinta matil vaki.
perumpālum cempu puṉintiyaṟṟiyatākavum ciṟupāṉmi aritta cāntiṭ ṭamittatākavum, pakivarāl eḷitākak tākka muṭiyāvāṟu tiṇṇitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa matil, ēṉivaki matil kaḷiṉum mika iṟukiyiruttal paṟṟi iñci yeṉappaṭṭatu.
iñci2 : 'ī' aṇmiyic cuṭṭumāṟu utaṭṭip piṉṉukku iḻut tolikkum uyiroli. ī - īl - īr - īrttal = iḻuttal. īrtal = iḻut taṟuttal, pallāṟ kaṭittiḻuttu urittal, iḻuttal, aṟuttal.
īl - il - iḷ - iḻu.
o.nā: koḷ kompu - koḻukompu, koḷnaṉ - koḻunaṉ.
iḻuttal = piṉṉiḻuttal, uḷḷiḻuttal, uṟiñcutal.
iḷ - (iy) - (iyñcu) - iñcu.
iñcutal = nīri uḷḷiḻuttal, nilattil nīr cuvaṟutal.
iñcu - iñci = nīri uḷḷiḻuttut tiraṇṭirukkum kiḻaṅku vaki, aḵtuḷḷa pūṇṭu.
iñci kāyntu nīr vaṟṟiṉāṟ cukku. iñci x cukku.
cukku = nīr cuṇṭi vaṟaṇṭiruppatu.
cuḷḷutal (cuḷḷeṉal) = kāytal, nīr vaṟṟutal.
cuḷ - cuḷku - cuṭku - cukku.
o. nō: veḷ - veḷku - veṭku.
koḷ - koḷku - koṭku - kokku = vaḷinta kaḻuttuḷḷa paṟavi.
iṉaccol:
(1)	tiraviṭam : maliyāḷam - iñci, kuṭakam - iñci. kōttam - iñc, pirākirutam (pāli) - ciṅki, ciṅkivēra.
(2)	āriyam : (camaṟkirutam) - cruṅkavēra, parci (parji) - ciṅkivēra.
	ME. gingivere, OFr gengibre, LL gingiber. L. zingiber, Gk. zingiberis, Skt. srngavera. E. ginger,
(3)	piṟamoḻik kuṭumpam - malāy : iñcivēr.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu :
iñci toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil viḷikkappeṟṟuvaruvatu, 
"ceyyāp pāvi vaḷarntu kaviṉ muṟṟik 
kāyaṅ koṇṭaṉa"	(malipaṭu. 125-6)
"iñcivī virāya pintār pūṭṭic 
cāntupuṟat teṟinta tacumputuḷaṅ kirukki"
	(patiṟṟup. 42: 10-11)
"mañcaḷum iñciyum mayaṅkaril valayattuc
ceñcuḷip palaviṉ pāṟpaki yuṟukkum"	
(cilap. 10 : 74 - 5)
eṉṉum paṇṭiyilakkiyap pakutikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻakatti liruntu paṇṭi nāḷilum mēṉāṭukaṭku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa carakkukaḷil iñciyum oṉṟākum. atu vērāyiruppatāl iñci vēreṉṟum, kiḻaṅkāyiruppatāl iñcik kiḻaṅkeṉṟum, pāvi pōṉṟiruppatāl iñcippāvi eṉṟum collappaṭum. iñcivēr eṉṉum collē mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ palvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻakkup peṟṟatu.
tiruttam
camaṟkiruta āriyar iñci yeṉṉum teṉcolli āriyac collākak kāṭṭa vēṇṭi, cruṅkavēra eṉattirittu māṉkompu pōṉṟatu eṉap poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar. itu ceyaṟki yāṉatum poruttamaṟṟatum ākum eṉpati, varalāṟu maṭṭumiṉṟic colvaṭivum teḷiyakkāṭṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil cruṅka eṉpatu kompu eṉṟumaṭṭum poruḷpaṭum. vēra eṉpatu uṭampik kuṟikkum. iv viraṇṭum eṅṅaṉam iṇintu iñciyik kuṟikkum? iṟṟi yaṟiviyalkaḷit teḷiya vaṟinta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarum, camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyilum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu akaramutaliyilum, ākkacuppōrṭuc 
ciṟṟakaramutaliyilum, tiraviṭac coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyilum camaṟ kiruta āriyar kūṟṟiyē taḻuviyiruppatu mikamika viyappiṟkiṭamāṉatē. av vakara mutalikaḷuḷḷum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭakaramutali iñci vēr eṉpati maliyā (malāy)c collākak kuṟittiruppatu iṉ ṉum viyappāṉatē. itaṟkut tamiḻar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷ eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutiyiruppatē aṭippaṭiyākum. vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭamoḻiyākiya parciyil ciṅkivēra eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatāl maṭṭum, atu vaṭacollākiviṭātu. am moḻi atip piṟkālattu vaḻakkēṟṟiyirukkalām.
4. pati
(1)	col :-pati.
(2)	colvaki :- (part of speech of word-class) - viṉi. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉiyum (e. va.) ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉiyum (e. .).
(3)	puṭipeyarcci (conjugation) : 8āvatu vaki. patikiṟēṉ (ni. kā.) patintēṉ (i. è£.), pativēṉ, (e. kā.).
(4)	poruḷum āṭcimēṟkōḷum : (meanings and illustrative quotations.)
ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (gerunidial form)
pati - tal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. pativēṭṭil eḻututal, to register, enter in writing 2. peyarp paṭṭiyiṟ cērttal, to take on the roll.
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. āḻtal, to sink in, as the foot or a wheel in mud; to enter, penetrate, as into a soft body. vaṇṭic cakkaram cēṟṟiṟ patikiṟatu. (ulaka vaḻakku). 2. tāḻntiruttal, to be low-lying. as land; to be depressed, sunk, hollow, worn away. nilam patinti rukkiṟatu. (u.va.) 3. taṅkutal, to settle, abide; to perch, roost, as a bird, "paticeṉṟu patintaṉaṉ"(taṇikippu. piramaṉ.2). 4. niliyātal, to be permanent, as a post. eṉakku araṇmaṉiyiṟ patinta vēliyilli. (nāñ. u. va.) 5. ūṉṟutal, to be absorbed, engrossed, involved, as the mind in any object. maṉam pāṭattiṟ patikiṟatā? (u. va.) 6. muttiri, eḻuttaccu mutaliyaṉa aḻuntutal, to be imprinted, impressed, marked, stamped, engraven, indented. 7. atikāram peṟutal, to be invested with power, authority. (W.). 8. iṟaṅkutal, to decline after meridinal transists; to be near setting, as a heavenly body; to descend, alight, as a bird. 9. vili taṇital, to be low, as price. viliyip patiyak kēṭṭāṉ. (u.va.) 10. amitiyātal, to be mild, gentle or tractalle; to become submissive, modest or humble. piyaṉ paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ patintirukkiṟāṉ. (u. va.) 11. añci yiṇaṅkutal allatu piṉvāṅkutal, to quail, flinch.
peyareccam : (adjectival participle)
i. kā. pe. patinta nilam, low land.
ceyaṟpeyarum toḻiṟpeyarum : (gerund and verbal noun.)
patital, patiki, pativatu, the act of registering, sinking etc.
pativu, registration, sinking etc.
toḻiṟpeyarum, toḻilākupeyarum : (verbal noun and metonymical verbal noun.) 
pati = 1. patiki, penetration, transfixion, thrust. "nuṇṇilivēl patikoṇṭu," (cīvaka. 1186). 2. patiyam, cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 3. nāṟṟu, sapling for transplantation. 4. pativiḷakku, lamp fixed on a pot while exorcizing devils (W.). 5. à¬øõ¤ìñ¢, adobe, residence (î¤õ£.), "patiyiṟ kalaṅkiya mīṉ" (kuṟaḷ. 1116), 6. vīṭu, home, house (tivā.) 7. kōyil, temple (caṅ. aka.) 8. kuṟi collum iṭam. an oracular shrine, "patiyirunta pati yellām pativākac ceṉṟēṉ" (nāñ. marumak. mā.) 9. ūr, town, city, village. "patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi" (cilap. 1:15) 10. ñālam (pūmi), the earth. (tilava. tiva.) 11. kutiri, horse. (aka. ni.)
pativu = 1. aḻuntuki, impression, indentation. 2. paḷḷam, lowness of a surface; depression. nilam pativāyirukkiṟatu. (u. va) 3. patiyam. cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 4. viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ cāyvu declination of a heavenly body. (W). 5. eḻuvataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) nili, situation of the moon before rise. (W.) 6. patukkam, stooping, crouching, lurking as a thief of a beast ready to spring on its prey; ambush. 7. maṉam ūṉṟuki. engrossment, absorpion in an object or pursuit. 8. kaṇakkup patiki, registering, entering, as in account. 9. pativu ceyyappaṭṭatu. that which is registered. 10. patiyappaṭṭatu. registry, entry. 11. tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭa celavu, allotment, as in a budget. "intak kōvilil caṅku ūtuvataṟkup pativu ēṟpaṭa villi" (nāñ. u. va.) 12. nilippu (nilivaram), permanence. "intavūril avaṉ pativāyirukkiṟāṉ" (u. va.) 13. vaḻakkam, custom, habit. 14. amiti, submission, obedience, humility. "pativāy naṭantukoḷ" (u.va.) 15. vilittaṇivu. lowness of price (W.).
patiyam = 1. nāṟṟu muṭiyi iraṇṭoru nāṭkuc cēṟṟil patittu vikki, temporary planting of a cluster or saplings in mud. 2. ūṉṟi naṭuñ ceṭi koṭi kiḷi mutaliyaṉa, slip, shoot, graft. 3. ilippāci, a species of duckweed. "ceṟiyaṇip patiyattiṭi vaḷariḷañ cēlkaḷ" (iraku. nāṭṭup.7).
patiyam - patikam = "patikam parikkuṅ kuḻal" (maṟici. 17.)

pāta tāmari (pātat tāmari) = pe. tiruvaṭit tāmari; lotus - like feet.
pāta tūḷi (pātattūḷi) = pe. periyōr aṭippoṭi; dust of the feet of great persons "ēttuvārkaḷuḻakkiya pātatūḷi paṭutalāl" (tiv. periyāḻ. 4:4.6).
pāta pūci (pātappūci) = pe. kuru mutaliyōriṉ tiruvaṭi kaḷik kaḻuvi malariṭṭu vaḻipaṭuki; worshipping the feet of a religious preceptor or revered person by washing and adorning them with flowers.
pāta mayakku = pe. 1. aṭi mayakku; aṭi muṟi māṟṟakkūṭiya ceyyuḷ; stanza whose lines are capable of transposition. 2. vēṟu pulavar pāṭiya mūvaṭikaḷōṭu tāṉ ōraṭi pāṭi muṭikkum miṟippā vaki; a kind of artificial stanza of four lines the first three of which are taken from works of other poets while the last is composed by the author. (yāp. vi. 96. pak 504.).
pāta muttiri = pe āciriyaṉ tiruvaṭic cuvaṭu; imprint of a guru's feet (W.).
pāta mūlam = pe. 1. kutikāl heel, (yāḻ aka.) 2. vīṭu pēṟ ṟiṟkuk karaṇiyamāṉatum (kāraṇamāṉatum) aṭikkalamākak karutappaṭu vatumāṉa tiruvaṭi; feet of a deity or sint considered as the source of bliss and a refuge. "niṉiyumiṉ piṇṭināta ṉalaṅkiḷar pātamūlam" (cīvaka. 511).
pāta veṭippu = pe. pitta veṭippu; fissure-foot
pātam - pāti = pe. 1. oṟṟiyaṭi vaḻi; foot-path. beaten track. 2. vaḻi, way, path, road (pi.ṅ.). 3. muṟi way, method, mode, manner.
pati - vati, ce. kuṉṟiya vi. 
õî¤è¤«øù¢ (ï¤ è£.), vatintēṉ (i. è£.), vativēṉ (e. kā.)
vati - tal = (ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal, to slay, to dwell, abide; to sojourn. "vatimaṇ vampalar vāyaviḻn taṉṉār" (paripā. 10:20). 2. tuyilutal, to sleep "āṟṟā ṇiṉiyunaḷ vatintak kāl" (kalit. 126).
vati = pe. 1. vilaṅku paṟavi mutaliyaṉa taṅkumiṭam; lir, nest. "māvati cēra" (kalit. 119). 2. kāl patiyuñ cēṟu, mire. "ceṅkayal vatikkuti koḷum puṉalatu" (tēvā. 413:7), (piṅ).
kūṭṭuc col: (Compound words)
patipaṭi = pe. maṟintu niṟkuñ cēṉi. army lying in ambush (W.). patipaṭi - patippaṭi. "periya tiruvaṭiyip patippaṭiyāka vittu vantu" (tiv. tiruneṭun. 23, viyā. pak. 213).
pati miṉukki = pe. (iṭattit tulakkum) tuṭippam; broom, as cleaning a place. (tilava. tila.)
patiyañ carukkari = pe. oṭṭum patattiluḷḷa carukkari; molasses in a viscous condition (nāñ.).
patiyari = pe. nāṟṟu. saplings for transplanting (aka. ni.).
patiyeḻu - tal = (ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) valaci pōtal; to flee from home or town from fear of the kind or a hostile army.
patiyeḻavu, patiyeḻuvu = pe. valacipōki; flight from home or town from fear of the kind or a hostile army. "patiyeḻa vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi" (malipaṭu. 479). "patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi" (cilap. 1:15).
pativāḷar, pe. 1. āvaṇam, oppantam, caṭṭatiṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟip pativu ceyyum araciyal aluvalar. Registrar of title-deeds, contracts, rules and regulations of corporations etc. (ikkāla vaḻakku). 2. palkalikkaḻaka aluvalakat talivar; Registrar of a University (i. va).
pativiṭam = pe. oḷittirukkumiṭam; hiding place, ambush.
pativiḷakku = pe. pēyōṭṭutaṟkākac kuṭattiṉ mēṟ patiyavitta viḷakku; lamp fixed on a pot while exorcizing devils (W.).
pativuc cāppāṭu = pe. uṇṭiccāliyil mātak kaṇakkāka ēṟpāṭu ceytu uṇṇum uṇavu; regular boarding at a hotel or mess on monthly account.
pativēṭu = pe. kaṇakkup patiyum pottakam; register, account-book, ledger.

"pattik kaṭaluṭ patitta parañcōti" (tiruvāca. 11:12). 2. maṇi mutaliyaṉa iḻittal; to infix, insert, ingraft, inlay, as gems; to enchase. 3. kaṟ pāvutal; to pave, as floor with bricks or stones. 4. tāḻttutal, to lower height, price, etc; to set lower, insert deeper. 5. patiyam pōṭutal; to plant a custer of saplings temporarily in mud; to plant a shoot, runner or creeper. 6. ac ciṭutal, to print, reprint, edit. 7. eḻututal to inter in a register. 8. ati kāraṅ koṭuttal; to invest with power, authority or prerogative (W.)

mūlamum tirivum (origin and derivation)
paḷ - paṭu - paṭi - pati. paḷ - paḷḷam.
paṭutal = tāḻa viḻutal, viḻutal, toṭutal, patital.

paṭital = tāḻttal, kīḻppaṭutal, paṇital, patital.
patital = āḻa iṟaṅkutal, poṟittal, pativu ceytal.
iṉac col : (cognates and allied words)
1. tiraviṭam:
maliyāḷam - pati, pativu.
patikka, to impress. patiyuka, to be impressed, be pressed down. pati, being fixed in, pressed down. pativu, settlement, custom.
patam, softness, elasticity, yielding temper. patama, patuma. pliancy. patarmma, rottenness of rice through damp, softness of mind. patukka, to be soft, tender, pliable. patuppu, softness. patuppikka, to soften patam, the right degree of ripeness, temperature, etc.
teluṅku - pataṉu, patuṉu, moisture, dampness, wetness; ripeness maturity temper.
kaṉṉaṭam - pata, proper or good state or condition, proper degree or temperature, the seasoning of any food, the right degree of a ripeness, keenness of edge or sharpness hata, proper condition
tuḷuvam - pataṉuṉi, pataṇuṉi, to become soft.
kuṭakam - pata soft.
kōttam - patm, temper of iron
mālattam - petke, to be soft. patō, sharp.
kuvī - pete. soft and damp.
pirākuvī - putēṉ, cold, cool, putī, coldness, frost.

(4) poruḷum āṭci mēṟkōḷum : (meanings and illustrative quotations)
ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (Gerundial form)
poṟu-ttal, (ªê.kuù¢ø£ vi.) 1. cumattal, to bear, sustin. "civiki poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntā ṉiṭi" (kuṟaḷ. 37). "iciyilā yākki poṟutta nilam" (kuṟaḷ. 239). 2. tāṅkutal. to prop, to support, "ālattu... neṭuñ ciṉi vīḻpoṟuttāṅku" (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital, to put on. "ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟintāṉ" (upatēcakā. uruttirāk. 44). 4. uṭaṉ koṇ ṭiruttal, to continue, to possess, as one's own body. "poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ" (tiruvāca. 23:6). 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. to endure, suffer. 6. kuṟṟam poṟuttal, to bear with to put up with. 7. iḷakkāraṅ koṭuttal, to indulge to allow. 8. maṉṉittal, to excuse, forgive, pardon. "tammi yikaḻvārp poṟuttal tali" (kuṟaḷ. 151). 9. piṟa karuttu vēṟupāṭṭi allatu mata vēṟupāṭṭip poṟuttuk koḷḷutal, to tolerate. 10. poṟuppu (uttaravātam) ēṟṟal, to take responsibility to be accountable for. 11. kālan tāḻttal, to delay, to postpone. 12. uvamiyākap peṟutal, to be similar. "ulakam poṟukkāta tōḷāy" (cīvaka. 402). 13. aḻuttutal, to press heavily, as a load.
(ªê.kuù¢ø¤ò vi.) 1. poṟumiyāyiruttal, to be patient, exercise forbearance. 2.viṉiyiṭi niṉṟu koḷḷutal. to stop, wait halt in doing anything. 3.vili koḷḷutal, to cost, as an article; to be spent or expended on. 4. tōṇi taṭṭippōtal, to run aground, as a vessel, to strand. 5. māṭṭik 
koḷḷutal, to become fixed, jammed, wedged in (W.) 6. kaṭamiyākak cumarutal, to devolve upon, as a duty resposibility expenses. 7. iṇaṅki yiruttal, to consent yield, comply with 8. mikuntiruttal, to be excessive. 9. kaṉattal, to be heavy, weight.

caṟṟē poṟu, wait a little.

toḻiṟ paṇpuppeyar : (abstract noun).
poṟuppu, responsibility (uttaravātam, va.).
poṟuppāḷi. a responsible person poṟuppukkāraṉ (yāḻa. aka.) poṟuppuḷḷa patavi. a responsible post. poṟuppuḷḷavaṉ. one in a responsible post or place of trust, a responsible man. poṟuppaṟṟavaṉ, an irresponsible man. poṟuppaṟṟa taṉam, irresponsibility, indifference to a trust.
poṟumi, patience. poṟāmi. e.ma. (neg) intolerance,,nvy, jelousy poṟumicāli. patient person. poṟumikkāraṉ, patient man (W.). poṟuti. forbearance, forgiveness.
toḻilāku peyarum ataṉ tiripukaḷum : (Metorymical verbal noun and its derivatives)
poṟi = pe. 1. cumi(pāram). (tivā.) "kuḻiyu piḻiyum poṟiyā" (kalit. 90); burden. load.. 2. kaṉam, "poṟi tantaṉa kācoḷir pūṇ" (kamparā. uyut. atikā. 40); weight, heaviness. 3. cūl (karuppam) pregnancy (cūṭā.).
4. ciṟu kuṉṟu, "aṟiyum poṟiyum maṇanta taliya" (puṟanā. 118); hillock. 5. mali. "neṭum poṟi miciya kuṟuṅkāṟ koṉṟi" (aiṅkuṟu. 430); mountin. 6.ñālam (pūmi. va.) piṅ.). "poṟitarat tiraṇṭa tāru" (iraku. tacarataṉ kāpa. 50), earth. 7. tuṉpantāṅkun taṉmi. "vaṉmiyuḷ vaṉmi maṭavārp poṟi" (kuṟaḷ. 153), forbearance. 9. aṭakkam. "poṟiyu nāṇu nīṅkiṉār" (kamparā. uyut miṭci. 38); modesty meekneas. 10. valimi. "pōtakātipaṉ mutalivāyiṭip poṟi taḷarntu" (pārata. āti. vēttirakīya. 1); strength, fortitude.
poṟi - poṟṟi = pe. 1. kaṟpāṟi (tiv. tiruccan. 52. vyā) rock. 2. ciṟumali (piṅ.). "poṟṟimāl varikaḷōveṉ puyaneṭum poruppu mammā" (kamparā. ilaṅki kāṇ. 22); hillock, mound 3. mali. (piṅ.) "poṟṟiyuṟ ṟeṭuttāṉ" (tēvā. 1218.10); mountin.
tūṇkaḷ maṇṭapattit tāṅkuvatupōl malikaḷ vāṉa mukaṭṭit tāṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum paṇṭik karuttuppaṟṟi mali poṟiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
"Atlas.... (Atlas-antos (1) Greek god of the older family. who held up pillars of universe; (2) the mountin in Libya regarded as supporting the heavens)"
"Atlantic... 1. Pertining to mount Atlas in Libya; hence applied to sea near western shore of Africa, & later to whole ocean between Europe and Africa on east & America on west... (f. L. f. Gk. Atlantikos. f. Atlas)" eṉṟu erutantuṟic ciṟṟakaramutali (the concise oxford dictionary) kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
poṟiyaṉ = pe. 1. cumappavaṉ. "puṉṉilip pavattuk kellān tāṉoru poṟiyaṉāki" (upatēcakā. civapuṇ. 344); bearer, sustiner. 2. maliyaracaṉāṉa cēraṉ. "yāṉik kaṭumāṉ poṟiya" (puṟanā. 53); Cera king, as lord of the mountinous region in the Tamil country. 3. poṟumikkum poṟikkuñ ciṟanta tarumaputtiraṉ; Dharmaputra, the eldest of the the Pandavas, as embodiment of patience.
kuṟumpoṟi = pe. ê¤Áñ¬ô (ð¤é¢.), "variyaka naṇṇik kuṟum poṟi nāṭi" (patiṟṟup. 74 : 7)
kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ = mullinilat talivaṉ (iṟi. 1 : 18) chief of the sylvan or pastral tract.
irumpoṟi = pe. cērar paṭṭap peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu (patiṟṟup. 89 : 9). e-ṭu : cēramāṉ kaṇikkālirumpoṟi; a title of the Cera kings
poṟiyāḷaṉ=pe. 1. poṟumicāli 2. tarumaputtiraṉ (piṅ.).
poṟiyāṭṭi = 1. poṟumiyuḷḷavaṉ. "curuṅkum maruṅkuṟ perum poṟi yāṭṭiyi" (tirukkōvi. 353); patient woman. 2. kāvu (pali) koṭukkum pūcārip peṇ. "kāṉappali nērkkaṭavuṭ poṟiyāṭṭi vantāḷ". (periyapu. kaṇṇap. 65); priestess who offers animals to god in sacrifice.
poṟiyilāṉ = pe. 1. poṟumiyillātaṉ, impatient person.. 2. vēṭaṉ, savage hunter.
poṟinili = pe. maṉatti oru vaḻippaṭuttutal (tāraṇi. õ.); concentrated attention in yogic meditation.
