18
ti.mu.ka araciṟkup pārāṭṭu
kaṭanta mūṉṟariyāṇṭuk kālattiṟkuḷ ti.mu.ka aracu tamiḻ viṭutalikkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum ceytuḷḷa ākkap paṇikaḷ mūṉṟu. avi, intiya amipput tiṭṭattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ peyar ceṉṉi nāṭu eṉṟu iruntatit tamiḻnāṭu eṉat tiruttiyatu, tamiḻk karaṇattic caṭṭamuṟippaṭic cellupaṭiyākkiyatu, kōvil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuk kaṭanta mūvāyiram āṇṭāka vilakkappaṭṭu irunta tamiḻi mīṇṭum pukuttiyatu, eṉpaṉa. am mūṉṟum muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟantavi.
tamiḻar muṉṉēṟum vaḻi tamiḻēyātalāl āṅkilattiṟkum āriyat tiṟkum aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭanta tamiḻi avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu viṭuvittu araciyal moḻiyum mataviyal moḻiyumākkiyatu, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku uyirnāṭit tiṭṭamākum.
civaneṟiyum, tirumāl neṟiyum tamiḻar kaṇṭa camayaṅkaḷē yātalāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻar kaṭṭiya kōvilkaḷil tamiḻar toṉṟu toṭṭu vanta tamiḻ muṟiyit taḷḷiviṭṭu aṟiyāta ayaṉ moḻiyil vaḻi paṭa, aṟiyāmiyum aṭimittaṉamumaṉṟi vēṟōru karaṇiyamumilli.
pirāmaṇar, kālattiṟkēṟpat tam karutti māṟṟik koḷḷavum, āṇṭi mutal aracaṉ varippaṭṭa eppatavikkum tammi ēṟṟava rākkik koḷḷavum iyalpākavē tiṟam paṭittavar. iṉṟu pirāmaṇi yatti naṭatti varupavar aṭimittaṉamum taṉṉalamum mikka pōlit tamiḻarē. vaṭamoḻiyi aṟavē vilakka tamiḻip paṇṭupōṟ kōvil vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyākka vēṇṭumeṉpatu ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakattiṉ aṭippaṭik kuṟikkōḷ. ati eḷitāka niṟivēṟṟa vaḻivakutta ti.mu.ka aracē tamiḻ nāṭṭi vaḻi vaḻi āḷka. ati āṟṟaluṭaṉ naṭattivarum aruṭ celvaṉār nīṭu vāḻka!
iṉi, immaṭṭil niṉṟuviṭātu, tamiḻiṉ uṇmiyāṉa iyalpi yum varalāṟṟiyum aṟintu, tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkut tamiḻilum veḷiyulakat tiṟku āṅkilattilum veḷippaṭuttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻ ki.mu. 50,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭintu ulakeṅkum tirintum, citintum paraviyuḷḷatu. kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumākum. tamiḻar teṉ ṉāṭṭup paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷēyaṉṟi, āriyarpōl veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantavarallar.
tamiḻic cevviyākak kāttaṟkum vaḷarttaṟkum, tamiḻp pulavarum tamiḻukkuc cārpākavum māṟākavum iruppavarip pirittaṟital vēṇṭum. tamiḻ nalattiyē pēṇi ataṉ tūymiyip pōṟṟik kākkum paṇpaṭṭa tamiḻp pulavar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ kūṭṭattār; tannalattiyē pēṇit tamiḻik kāṭṭik koṭukkum, paṇpaṟṟa tamiḻp pulavar viyāpuri kūṭṭattār. viyāpuri kūṭṭat tārit tuṇikkoṇṭu tamiḻi vaḷarppatu tiruṭaṉit tuṇikkoṇṭu paṇappeṭṭiyik kāppatu pōlum.
ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali tamiḻ camaṟkirutak kiḷiyum paṉmoḻik kalaviyumākum eṉṟu ayalār karutumāṟu viyāpurip piḷḷiyit tuṇikkoṇṭu tamiḻppakivarāṉa pirā maṇap pulavar tokuttatu. ātalāl mutaṟkaṇ tirutta vēṇṭum. ati moḻinūlaṟivum tamiḻmoḻi yārāycciyum coṟṟokuppup payiṟciyum oruṅkē yuṭiyavarē ceyyaviyalum. paṇṭārakar (Dr.) paṭṭam peṟṟavarellārum tamiḻatikārikaḷallar. ātalāl, ceṉṉi akaramutaliyit tiruttavō, vērccol allatu coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali tokukkavō oru kuḻu amarttuvateṉpatu, kuruṭarik koṇṭu ōr ōviyattit tiruttavō variyavō ēṟpāṭu ceyvatē yokkum. paṇṭārakar paṭṭam peṟṟavar tām āynta poruḷilaṉṟi, ellāt tuṟiyilum valluna rākār. avar naṭippiyum ēmāṟṟiyum aṟiyavalla mutalamiccarē pāratitācaṉ kuṟitta tamiḻāynta tamiḻ makaṉ āvar.
mutaṉmoḻi, alavaṉ 1971
19
maṉōṉmaṇīya āciriyar cuntaraṉār
tamiḻ vāḻtti iṉip pāṭavēṇṭiya muṟi
nīrārum kaṭaluṭutta nilamaṭantik keḻiloḻukum
cīrārum mukameṉavē tikaḻ nāvaṟ kaṇṭamitil
takkaciṟu piṟinutalum tāṅkunaṟum poṭṭaṇiyum
tekkaṇamum atiṟciṟanta teṉmoḻinaṟ ṟirunāṭum
attakum poṭ ṭarumaṇampōl aṉittulakum iṉpamuṟa
etticiyum pukaḻmaṇakka irunta perum tamiḻaṇaṅkē!
palluyirum palavulakum paṭittaḷittut tuṭikkiṉumōr
elliyaṟu paramporuḷmuṉ iruntapaṭi iruppatupōl
kaṉṉaṭamum kaḷiteluṅkum kaviṉmaliyāḷamun tuḷuvum
uṉvayiṟṟiṟ piṟanteḻuntē oṉṟupala vāyiṭiṉum
āriyampōl ulakavaḻak kaḻintoḻintu citiyāvuṉ
cīriḷamit tiṟamviyantu ceyalmaṟantu vāḻttutumē!
kuṟippu:-
(1)	iruḷ kavinta tamiḻ vāṉiṉ viṭiveḷḷi pōṉṟum, āriya aṭimittaṉattil ayarntuṟaṅkum tamiḻaṉukkup paḷḷiyeḻucci pāṭum akavaṉ pōṉṟum, taṉittamiḻ vēntar maṟimaliyaṭikaṭku muṉceṉṟu kaṭṭiyaṅkāraṉ pōṉṟum tōṉṟiyavar cuntaraṉār. avariyaṟiyātu oru cila vaṭacoṟkaḷ avar pāṭaliṟ pukuntuviṭṭaṉa. atu avar virumpic ceytataṉṟu. ikkālattil avar irunti ruppiṉ, mēṟkāṭṭiyavāṟē pāṭiyiruppār allatu tirutti yiruppār.
(2)	avar pāṭalil "āriyam pōl... citiyāvuṉ" eṉpatē uyir nāṭiyāṉa aṭi. ati nīkkiṉ, atu avar pāṭalā kātu. atōṭu avarukkum tamiḻukkum pēriḻukkāṉa peruṅkuṟṟamākum.
(3)	āriyaṉ nilattēvaṉ; camaṟkirutam tēvamoḻi eṉṉum ēmāṟṟiṉālēyē vaṭacol tamiḻiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatu. tami ḻukku vaṭacol tēviyilli, 229ām puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭṭil, vaṭakku kiḻakku eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaṭkut talimāṟāka ūci (utīcī) pāci (prācī) eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷ vēṇṭātu vantu tamiḻiṉ tūymi kulit talik kāṇka. "aṉṉiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam" auviyār pāṭṭil 'aṉṉiyum tantiyum' eṉṟiruptē takkatām. kuṭaṉ piṟantē kollum viyātika eṉpatu 'uṭaṉ piṟantē kollum piṇikaḷ' eṉṟē iruttal vēṇṭum.
(4)	āriyakkuṭumpa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷellām, a (ā), i (ī), u (ū) eṉṉum tamiḻc cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi ṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyaviyē. tamiḻi vaṭamoḻit tuṇi yiṉṟip pēcavum eḻutavum iyalum. āyiṉ, tamiḻt tuṇiyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻiyip pēcavō eḻutavō iyalātu.
(5)	vaṭamoḻiyil aintiliru pakuti tamiḻ, aintiloru pakuti mēliyāriyam, aintiloru pakuti vaṭatiraviṭamāṉa pirākirutam, aintiloru pakuti putitākap puṉiyappaṭṭatu.
(6)	moḻiyārāycciyum, tamiḻ varalāṟṟaṟivum illāta cilar, tam paṭṭam pataviyit tuṇikkoṇṭu, iḷiñariyum, potu makkaḷiyum mayakkalām. āyiṉ ēmāṟṟu nilikkātu.
(7)	tamiḻ 'ceññāyiṟu' pōṉṟu iyalpākavē 'centamiḻ' ākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
(8)	The Lemurian Language and its Ramifications eṉṉum nūl aṭuttu varum. atik kaṇṭu teḷika.
20
taṉittamiḻ itaḻāciriyar tavaṟu
'taṉittamiḻitaḻ'kaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil mēṉmēlun tōṉṟik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. avi taṉittamiḻuṇarcci paravivaruvati aṟi vikkum vakiyil varavēṟkattakkaṉa vēṉum, avaṟṟuṭ perum pālāṉa moḻiccārpi mutaṉmiyākak koḷḷātu, kaṭciccārpum mataccārpum taṉippeyar viḷamparamumē kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭi ruppatu mika varuntattakkatām.
mēlum cila itaḻkaḷ peyaraḷavilēyē taṉittamiḻāyuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟil ilakkaṇap piḻikaḷum malintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, cila itaḻkaḷ collārāycciyil iṟaṅkiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻp pulami peṟṟup paṉmoḻi payiṉṟu, pallāṇṭu moḻiyā rāycci ceytavarē, tamiḻc collārācci ceytal kūṭum. cilar ārāycciyurimi pēccurimi pōṉṟu aṉivarkkum potuveṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉaṅ karutu vataṟku, araciyal taṇṭaṉiyō talimippulavar atikāramō iṉmiyē karaṇiyamākum.
"kuṭṭutaṟkō piḷḷippāṇ ṭiyaṉilli
kuṟumpiyaḷa vākkātik kuṭintu tōṇṭi
eṭṭiṉamaṭ ṭaṟuppataṟkō villiyilli
iraṇṭoṉṟā muṭintutali yiṟaṅkap pōṭṭu
veṭṭutaṟkō kaviyoṭṭak kūttaṉilli
viḷiyāṭṭāyc (collāyvu virintu ceytu)
teṭṭutaṟkō aṟivillā (iḷiñaruṇṭu
teṉṉitaḻā ciriyareṉat) tiriya lāmē"
ituvari taṉittamiḻ itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta vaḻuuc col lārāyccikaḷuḷ, teṉ collārāycci yoṉṟum vaṭacollārāycci yoṉṟum, iṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ.
1. teṉ collārāycci :
māṟṟup peṇ - māṭṭup peṇ, nāṟṟup peṇ - nāṭṭup peṇ eṉpaṉa tavaṟu.
maṇavāḷaṉ - maṇāḷaṉ, maṇavāṭṭi - maṇāṭṭi, maṇavāṭṭu - maṇāṭṭu, maṇāṭṭup peṇ - māṭṭup peṇ. maṇāṭṭup peṇ - ṇāṭṭup peṇ - nāṭṭup peṇ.
māṭṭup peṇ, nāṭṭup peṇ eṉpaṉa koccit tiripukaḷ; ātalāṟ koḷḷattakkaṉavalla. 
2. vaṭa collārāycci :
palticikki - pattiriki, eṉpatu tavaṟu.
patra(va)=ciṟaku, tūvu, ili, poṉṉat takaṭu, tirumukam, āvaṇam pottaka ēṭu.
patra(va)-patrikā(va) = ili, tirumukam, viḷamparat tuṇṭu, aḻippitaḻ, āvaṇam, ceytittāḷ.
itaḻ, ēṭu, ōli, tāḷ, maṭal mutaliya teṉ coṟkaḷ, mutaṟkaṇ iliyiyum, pūvitaḻiyuṅ kuṟittavi, piṉṉar eḻutiya allatu acciṭṭa tāḷkaḷiyum uṇarttutali oppunōkkik kāṇka.
vaṭa collit teṉ colleṉṟu kūṟuvataṉāl tamiḻa ṉukku aṟiyāmip paṭṭam varuvatōṭu, vaṭamoḻi kaṭaṉ koṇṭa teṉ collum vaṭa collāki, tamiḻukkut tīṅkē nēru eṉpatit teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
iṉi, aḷaviṟku miñciya itaḻkaḷ veḷivantu, taṉittamiḻ vaḷarcci taṭiyuṇṇumaḷavu oṉṟōṭoṉṟu pōṭṭiyiṭuvatiṉum, taliciṟanta oṉṟiraṇṭē veḷivantu virintu taṉittamiḻip parappuvatu ukanta
tātaliṉ taṉittamiḻ itaḻāciriyar aṉivarum oṉṟukūṭi oṟṟumi
yāka oru muṭivu ceytukoḷvatu naṉṟeṉak karutukiṟēṉ.
21
vāḻnāṭ pallāṇṭu varampu viḻākkaḷ
makkaḷiṉ maṇṇulaka vāḻnāṭ pērelli varavarak kuṟintu, iṉṟu nūṟāṇṭākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.
"māntarkku vayatu nūṟalla tilli" eṉpatu kapilarakaval. oruvar tām niṉittavuṭaṉ etirvantu niṟkum uṟaviṉariyō naṇpariyō pārttu, uṅkaṭku akavi nūṟu (nūṟāṇṭu) eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. 'makkaḷ nūṟāṇṭu vāḻkki' eṉṟu oru nūlum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
"āṟilum cāvu nūṟilum cāvu" eṉṉum uṇmiyiṉālum, piḷḷi piṟantu ōrāṇṭiruppatum uṟutiyaṉmiyālum, karuvilēyē iṟantu cāppiḷḷiyum veḷippaṭuvataṉālum, kāl nūṟṟāṇṭu, ari nūṟṟāṇṭu, mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭu, nūṟṟāṇṭu mutaliya pallāṇṭu vāḻvukaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟanta nāḷum āṇṭu niṟivu nāṭkaḷum perumpālum celvap peṟṟōrāl allatu uṟṟōrāṟ koṇṭāṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kuḻaviyō piḷḷiyō iḷantiyarō vaḷarcci muṟṟiya āḷō cētamiṉṟik kākkappaṭṭu vantamipaṟṟi, iṟivaṉukku naṉṟi yoṭu kāṇikki celuttuvatum, iyaṉṟaḷavu paṇañ celaviṭṭu uṟṟār uṟaviṉaruṭaṉ uṇṭāṭi makiḻvatum, ik koṇṭāṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ nōkkamākum.
aracaṉ allatu araci piṟantanāḷ āṭṭi viḻā, nāṇ maṅkalam eṉṟum, veḷḷaṇi viḻā eṉṟum collappeṟum.
mēṉāṭukaḷil tōṉṟiya kāl nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā veḷḷiviḻā (Silver Jubilee) eṉṟum, ari nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā poṉ viḻā (Golden Jubilee) eṉṟum, mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻā auḷi viḻā (Platinum Jublee) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. auḷi viḻāvi muttu viḻā eṉṟum vaḻaṅkalām.
ari nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum, mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa aṟupāṉ āṇṭu viḻā vayira viḻā (Diamond Jublee) eṉappaṭum. vikuttōriyā (Victoria) araciyār āṭciyiṉ aṟupāṉ āṭṭi viḻā 1897-ām āṇṭu koṇṭāṭappaṭṭatu. vayira viḻāvi maṇiviḻā eṉṟuṅ kūṟalām.
aṟupatu nāḻiki oru nāḷeṉṟum, aṟupatu nāḷ oru perumpoḻutu eṉṟum kālak kaṇippuṇmiyum 'aṟupatiṟkumēṟ kiṟukiṟuppu' eṉṉum 
kīḻnāṭṭuk koḷkiyum, valuviṟakkat (Climacteric) toṭakkam aṟupatā māṇṭeṉṉum, mēṉāṭṭuk koḷkiyum ki.mu. 57-l toṭaṅkiya vikkirama cakāttam eṉṉum aṟupāṉāṇṭu māṉattiṟkum, aṟupāṉāṭṭi viḻāviṟkum karaṇiyamā yiruntirukkalām.
mukkāl nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa eṇ pāṉ āṭṭi viḻāvik katiriya viḻā (Radium Jubilee) eṉṉalām.
nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kāla viḻāvi aruṭkatir viḻā eṉal takum. iraciya tēcac cākciyā nāṭṭu cirāli micilimōv (Shirali Mislimov) eṉpavar 168 āṇṭukaḷum avaruṭiya tuṇiviyār pakku tāḻi (Baku Tadzhi) eṉpār 107 āṇṭukaḷum vāḻntaṉar.
22
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliya māṇpu
'maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyam' eṉpatu taṉiyurimi nūl niliyam eṉṟum potuppayaṉ nūlniliyam eṉṟum, irunili koṇṭatu. aṭikaḷ uṭampōṭirunta kālattil, 'maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyam' eṉpatu, pallavapurattil aṭikaḷ māḷikiyil aṭi kaṭkē muḻuvurimiyāṉa
tāyiruntatu. tamiḻ, camaṟkirutam, āṅkilam ākiya mummoḻikaḷil ēṟattāḻa nālāyiram arumpottakaṅkaḷ kōṇiniṟak karaṭṭuttāḷāl aṭṭiyiṭappaṭṭu, viḷinta nelvayal pōlap patiṉeṇ nilippēḻikaḷilum orē tōṟṟamākak kāṭci yaḷittaṉa.
pottakaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttuvatilum pōṟṟikkāppatilum, aṭikaḷ pōṟ ciṟantār ulakil oru cilarē. āyiṉum aṭikaḷiṉ muḻu nampikkikkuriya iraṇṭoruvarkkēṉum pottakaṅkaḷi iraval koṭuttuvantatu viyakkattakkatē.
"eṉ nūlakatti ōrilakkattiṟkum eṉ illatti ōrilakkattiṟ kum viṟṟuviṭṭu iṟutik kālattil nāṭṭuppuṟattil vatintu amiti yākak kālaṅkaḻikka virumpukiṉṟēṉ. tiruppatip palkalikkaḻakatti eṉ nūlakam vāṅkumāṟu kēṭṭataṟku, ariyilakkantāṉ taramuṭiyum eṉṟu colli viṭṭaṉar" eṉṟu aṭikaḷ orumuṟi eṉṉiṭam coṉṉa
tuṇṭu. aṭikaḷ nūlniliyam tiruppati ceṉṟiruppiṉ, tiruppatit tiruvēṅkaṭamuṭiyār tirukkōvilukkuc celpavarkkuk kūṭap payaṉa
paṭṭiruppatu aritē. iṉi aṭikaḷ taṅkaḷ nūlakam potu makkaṭkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu vēṇmuṟi (Will) eḻutiviyā tirunti ruppiṉ aṭikaḷ pottakaṅkaḷellām paḻam pottakak kaṭikkārar vayappaṭṭup palvēṟiṭañ
ceṉṟu paṭippavar pārvikkum iṭamiṉṟi maṟintupōyirukkum.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ maṟinili yaṭintapiṉ avarkaḷ nūlakatti makkaḷ niṟattālum ceṉṉit toṭakka niliyālum karunakarāyirunta pakutiyil  oru civattirukkōvil muṉṉiliyil, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ taṉic coṟpoḻi vāṟṟiya tirumaṉiyil cērp pittu mēṟkoṇṭu aiyāyirattinnūṟu arunūlkaḷic cērttut tamiḻakattiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi ulaka muḻuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭumāṟum, aṭikaḷ peyar ulakuḷḷa aḷavum nilavumāṟum maṟimali 
yaṭikaḷ nūlniliyattit tāmaric celvar tiru. õ.cuð¢¬ðò£ð¢ piḷḷi avarkaḷ palvēṟu vakiyil arumperum pāṭupaṭṭu 24-8-1958 aṉṟu niṟuviyatu eṇṇippārkkiṉ iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭē eṉpatu pōtarum.
"ūruṇi nīrniṟin taṟṟē yulakavām
pēraṟi vāḷaṉ tiru"	(kuṟaḷ 215)
eṉpataṟkēṟpa, ulakiṉ palvēṟu nāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷarum vantu perum payaṉpeṟuvatum,
"payaṉmaram uḷḷūrp paḻuttaṟṟāṟ celvam
nayaṉuṭi yāṉkaṭ paṭiṉ"	(kuṟaḷ 216)
eṉpataṟkēṟpa, ceṉṉi vāṇarkkuc ciṟappākap payaṉpaṭuvatum, 
"maruntākit tappā marattaṟṟāṟ celvam
peruntaki yāṉkaṭ paṭiṉ"	(kuṟaḷ 217)
eṉpataṟkēṟpa ēṉi nūlniliyaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu ēmāṟivantavarkkut tappātu vēṭki niṟivēṟṟuvatum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlnili yattiṉ taṉic ciṟappākum.
iṅṅaṉam, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ peyari maṭṭum tāṅki ēṉiyar pottakaṅkaḷuṅkoṇṭu ellārkkum potuvākap payaṉpaṭuvatu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyam eṉpataṉ iraṇṭām niliyākum. iṉi, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷiyellām koṇṭu viḷaṅkuvatum vaḻaṅkuvatum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūl niliyam eṉpataṟku vēṟoru kāraṇamumākum.
maṟimalinakar eṉpatu pōl aṭikaḷ peyari oru niṟuvaṉat tiṟku iṭuvataṟku, niṉivukūrtal oṉṟē pōtumāyiṉum aṭikaḷiṉ conta nūlakattik karuvākak koṇṭatum aṭikaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷi virivuṟuppākak koṇṭatum kūṭutaṟ kāraṇaṅkaḷākum.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyatti niṟuviyataṟkut tamiḻ vaḷarcciyum paravalumē ciṟappuk kāraṇamātaliṉ, tāmarit tiru. va. cu. piḷḷiyavarkaḷ, ataṟkuriya muyaṟciyi ollum vakiyāṟ cellum vāyellām ceytu, mēṉmēlum pottakattokiyip perukki varuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. avar, palar karutukiṉṟavāṟu tamiḻilum āṅkilattilum tamiḻc cārpāṉa pottaka allatu nūl veḷiyīṭṭāḷar maṭṭumallar. ituvari accēṟāta ēṭṭuc cuvaṭi kiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭit tokuppāḷarum, putunūl iyaṟṟu vippāḷarum, pulavar veḷināṭṭut tamiḻttoṇṭar mutaliyōr varalāṟṟut tokuppāḷarum, avar kiyeḻuttuppaṭit tokuppāḷarum, avar uruvappaṭat tokuppāḷarum, perum pulavar kaṭitappōkkuvarattut tokuppāḷarum, mānāṭukaḷiṉ naṭavaṭikkit tokuppāḷarum niṉṟupōṉa paḻiya ceytittāḷ kiḻamiyaṉ, mātiki, kālāṇṭitaḻ, āṇṭumalar mutaliya tokuppāḷarum 
piṟamoḻikaḷil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa tirukkuṟaḷ, nālaṭiyār mutaliya nūṟṟokuppāḷarum āvar.
iṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyap pottakat toki muppattiyāyiram. itu piṟa peru nūlniliyaṅkaḷoṭu oppunōkkiṉ ciṟitēyāyiṉum avaṟṟil illāta aru nūlkaḷiyum toṉ nūl kaḷiyum koṇṭiruppatu pārāṭṭattakkatākum. nāṉ paṉmuṟi iti eṉ paṭṭaṟiviṟ kaṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. atōṭu tamiḻmoḻi yilakkiya nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟṟu arumporuṭ kaḷañciyamākavum kāṭci yakamākavum iḵtu oḷiviṭṭut tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ eṉakkaḷitta naṟcāṉṟit tavaṟaviṭṭapiṉ ataṉ paṭiyoṉṟi eṉakkut tantatum, tirukkuṟaḷ paḻamoḻi moḻipeyarppiyum avaṟṟiṉ palvēṟu patippiyum nūlniliyat til vittiruppatum tāmarit tiru. va. cu. piḷḷiyiṉ tiṟamiyi yum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nūlniliyattiṉ perumiyiyum ciṟappak kāṭṭum. itaṉ arumiyuṇarntu aracu pōṟṟuvatu mikavum takkatākum.
23
āṅkilatti akaṟṟuvatu aṟivuṭimiyā?
oṟṟumi oru kuṭumpattiṟku vēṇṭuvatupōṉṟē, oru nāṭ ṭiṟkum ulakattiṟkum vēṇṭum. oṟṟumiyillākkāl, uyartiṇi yeṉṟu uyarttik kūṟappaṭum māntarukkum maṟṟa uyiriṉaṅkaṭkum vēṟupāṭilli av voṟṟumi yuṇṭākkuvataṟku oru potumoḻi iṉṟiyamiyātatu. atu āṅkilamē. ōr ulakap potumoḻikku irukkavēṇṭiya iyalpukaḷellām āṅkilattiṟku oruṅkē amin tuḷḷaṉa. ammoḻi taṉṉērcciyāka intiya āṭci moḻiyākavum uyartarak kalvi moḻiyākavum ēṟkaṉavē amintuḷḷami, tamiḻar perumpēṟē. ati ārāyntupārātu virintu ati akaṟṟappārp patu, taliyāl vanta perunitiyik kālāl taḷḷuvatu pōṉṟatē. āṅkilattiṉ ariya iyalpukaḷip palar aṟiyātiruppatāl, avarkku avaṟṟi aṟivippatu ikkaṭṭuriyiṉ nōkkamākum.
ulaka moḻi
paṉṉāṭṭuk kaḻakamō, aikkiya nāṭṭamippō, ulaka nāṭukaḷō oṉṟu kūṭip pēci muṭivu ceyyāmalē, āṅkilam tāṉāka, iyaṟkiyāka, etirppiṉṟi mellamellat taṉ takutiyiṉāl ulaka moḻiyāki viṭṭatu. ulakil mūṉṟi loru paṅku āṅkilattit tāy moḻiyākka koṇṭuḷḷatu. aikkiya nāṭṭamippu nikaḻccikaḷ naṭipeṟum mum moḻikaḷuḷ āṅkilam oṉṟu. ulakiṟ peruvāriyāṉa nūlkaḷum ceytit tāṭkaḷum veḷivaruvatu āṅkilattilēyē. pala nāṭukaḷ āṅkila moḻiyik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamāka ākkiyuḷḷaṉa. ēṉi nāṭukaḷilum atu viruppap pāṭamāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. oruvar ulaka muḻutum cuṟṟivarat tuṇiceyvatu āṅkila moḻi oṉṟē. oṉṟiya (aikkiya) nāṭṭamippu naṭavaṭikkikaḷ mummoḻiyil naṭipeṟṟālum, airōppiya moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟil aṟiviyal ilakkiyam eḻutappaṭṭiruppiṉum, ulaka moḻiyāka oṉṟutāṉ irukka muṭiyum. atu āṅkilamē; iṉimēl ati vēṟoru moḻi mēṟ koḷḷavum muṭiyātu.
aṟiviyaṉ moḻi
iṟṟi yulaka nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa putuppuṉivu nīrāvippoṟi. atik kaṇṭupiṭittavar cēmcuvāṭṭu eṉṉum āṅkilēyar; ataṉvaḻiyāyp pukivaṇṭi puṇarttiyavarum ciyārcu tīpaṉcaṉ eṉṉum ōr āṅkilēyarē. miṉ 
viḷakku, olippeṭṭi, tirippaṭam, vāṉūrti mutaliya paṟpala aṟiviyaṟ putuppuṉivukaḷi amitta amerikkarum, āṅkila moḻiyiṉarum āṅkilar vaḻiyiṉarumē. ātalāl, iṟṟi aṟiviyal ilakkiyattiṟ perumpakuti āṅkilattilēyē eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
cerumāṉiyam, pireñciyam, iraciyam ākiya piṟa moḻikaḷilum aṟiviyal nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruppiṉum, avi attuṇip peru vāri yāṉaviyalla. mēlum, ēṟkaṉavē aṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷa taliciṟanta mēli moḻiyi vilakkiviṭṭu, ēṉ oru putiya airōppiya moḻiyi nāṭa vēṇṭum? itu, kiyilirunta veṇṇeyik koṭuttuviṭṭu neykkalinta katiyākattāṉē muṭiyum! āṅkilattiṉ mēmpāṭu terintiruntum, ati akaṟṟiviṭṭuc cerumāṉiyam pōṉṟa ōr airōppiya moḻi yilakkiyattik kaṟkalā
meṉṟu kūṟum intiyar, tamakku āṅkilattiṉ mītuḷḷa aḷaviṟanta, aṭāta veṟuppiyē kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. attakiyōr pittariṉpāṟpaṭṭava rātalāl, piṟarāl piṉpaṟṟappeṟat takkavarallar eṉa viṭukka.
colvaḷa moḻi
ulakamoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷum colvaḷamikkatu āṅkilamē. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka āṅkila makkaḷ kali nākarikap paṇpāṭṭil taliciṟantu vantiruppataṉālum, ataṟkēṟpap pala karuttukkaḷiyum uṇarttap palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟu ilakkakkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷit tirittuk koṇṭata ṉālum, āṅkilamoḻi viyakkattakka colvaḷam peṟṟuviṭṭatu.
paṇṭimoḻikaḷuḷ mikap paṇpaṭṭatum colvaḷamikkatum tamiḻē yāyiṉum, atilum paḻamoḻikaḷip pākupaṭuttavilli. āṅkilattilō, aphorism, axion, maxim, apothegm, adage, proverb, byword, saw, eṉa eḻu vakiyākap pākupaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pride, vanity, conceit, arrogance, assurance, presumption. hughtiness, insolence eṉpaṉa parupporuḷil oruporuṭ coṟkaḷāyiṉum, nuṇ
poruḷil vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. sensuous, sensual, sensitive, sensible, sentient, sentimental, eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, bravery, courage, gallantry, prowess, heroism eṉṉum coṟkaḷum ivi pōṉṟa eṇṇiṟanta coṟ kuḻukkaḷum, āṅkilattil mika nuṇṇiya poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṭiyavi.
nākarika vulakiluḷḷa ellāk kalikaṭkum nūlkaṭkum uriya coṟkaḷellām āṅkilattil uḷḷaṉa. ñālattil mēliyarkkut terinta ellāp poruḷkaḷum āṅkilattiṟ peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
intiyil āṅkilak kuṟiyīṭukaḷi moḻipeyarttaṟ kēṟṟa coṟkaḷilli, āṅkilac coṟkaḷittāṉ atilum āḷavēṇṭum. ativiṭa āṅkilattiyē vittuk koḷvatu nallatu.
paṇpaṭṭa makkaḷ moḻi
caṭṭa muṟiyil mutaṉ mutal makkaḷ urimi peṟṟatu āṅkila nāṭē. ulaka nāṭāṭcik kuḻukkaṭkellām tāyākavum vaḻikāṭṭiyāka vum uḷḷatu āṅkilap pārāḷumaṉṟam. mutaṉmutal aṭimittaṉatti akaṟṟiyatum, peṇkaḷ nalattiyum piḷḷikaḷ nalattiyum pēṇi yatum, ēḻikaḷiṉ nilimiyi uyarttiyatum, uḻippāḷikaḷiṉ pāṭṭik kuṟittatum, akavi (vayatu) vanta irupālārkkum kuṭa vōli yurimiyaḷittatum, kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaṭku viṭutali tantatum, ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalviyip pukuttiyatum anākarika makkaḷi nākarikappaṭuttiyatum, āṅkila nāṭē. pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṟ ciṟantuvarum āṅkila makkaḷiṉ paṇpaṭṭa moḻi. intiyā pōṉṟa piṟpaṭṭa nāṭukaḷāṟ puṟak kaṇikkappaṭat takkataṉṟu.
oṟṟumi moḻi
ciṟiyavum periyavumāka eṇṇūṟu nāṭukaḷākap pirintu kiṭanta intu tēyam, āṅkila āṭciyilēyē oru kuṭikkīḻk koṇṭuvarap paṭṭatu. nicām, micūr, tiruvāṅkūr pōṉṟa uḷnāṭṭuc cīmika ḷellām paṭi kuṟikkap paṭṭu āṅkilap pātukāppiṟkuṭpaṭṭatiṉā lēyē, kāṅkiracu eṉṉum pērāyak kaṭciyiyakkam avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷum pukuntu paravavum, āṅkilēyar nīṅkiṉa piṉ av vuḷnāṭṭaracukaḷ oḻiyavum intiyā (pākkittāṉ nīṅkalāka) oru kaṭci yāṭcippaṭavum, nērntateṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. iṉṟum intiyāvi oṉṟāy iṇittukkoṇṭiruppatu āṅkilamē. iti aṟiyāta inti veṟiyar āṅkilatti akaṟṟiṉ, nāḷaṭivil moḻivāri mākāṇaṅka ḷellām taṉi nāṭukaḷākap pirintu pōkum nilimiyē ēṟpaṭum.
intiya moḻi
inti veṟiyarum vēṟu cila vaṭavarum āṅkilatti ayaṉ moḻi eṉkiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilattit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭuḷḷa āṅkila vintiyar, intiyak kuṭikaḷum oṉṟarik kōṭiyarumāka viruppati avar eṇṇip pārātiruppatu ettuṇik kuṟukiya nōkkamum naṭunili tiṟampiya ceyalumākum! itu aṭippaṭik kuṭiyurimi maṟuppēyaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. 
potu moḻi
āṅkilam iṉṟu āṅkilēyarāl pēcappaṭṭu vantālum, ataṉ mūlamāṉa āṅkila cākacaṉiyam (Anglo-saxon) oru kālattil āṅkilē yarkkē ciṟappāṉa moḻiyāy iruntiruntālum, iṟṟi niliyil, collamippil, ulakap potu moḻiyāyuḷḷatu. āṅkila cākaca ṉiyac coṟkaḷ mottam irupattu nālāyiramē. iṟṟi yāṅkilac coṟkaḷō ēṟattāḻa aintilakka mākum. ivaṟṟuḷ, nūṟṟu mēṉi 80 viḻukkāṭu ilattīṉum kirēkkamum, 10 
viḻukkāṭu āṅkila cākcaṉiyam; eñciya 10 viḻukkāṭu ēṉi moḻikaḷ. iti ep poruṭṭuṟipaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭalām. iṅkup pārāḷumaṉṟa caṭṭacapit tērtali eṭuttukkoḷvōm.

English - āṅkilam
Poll	Upper
ilattīṉ, tāḻilattīṉ ākiya iraṇṭum ēṟattāḻa oṉṟē.
iṅku āṅkila mallāta ayaṟcoṟkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭavi yellām, avvav vayaṉmoḻi vaṭivilēyē uḷḷaviyalla; āṅkila viyalpiṟkum tēvikkum ēṟpat tirikkappeṟṟaviyākum.
e-ṭu : Pars allatu Partis eṉpatu ilattīṉ col, ataṉiṉṟu Part, Party eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkaḷ tirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅa ṉamē piṟavum. 
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟpaṭṭiyiṉiṉṟu, iṟṟi yāṅkilam āṅki lēyarkkuc ciṟappāka vuriyataṉṟeṉpatum, ilattīṉ coṟkaḷ mikunta kalavi moḻi yeṉpatum, ati veṟuppatu ilattīṉ kirēkkam mutaliya piṟa moḻikaḷi veṟuppatēyākumeṉpatum, aṟiyappaṭum.
āṭci moḻi
āṅkilam ēṟkaṉavē oṉṟari nūṟṟāṇṭāka intiya āṭci moḻiyāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. ataṉ toṭarcciyi intiyip putuvatākap pukuttaṟkoppākak karutuvatu, pētimiyēyām. inti putu moḻiyā yiruppatoṭu āṭcikkut takāta puṉmoḻiyākavu miruppatu karutaṟkuriyatu.
viṭutali moḻi
vaṭavar palarum teṉṉavar cilarum āṅkilatti aṭimip paṭuttiya moḻiyākak karutukiṉṟaṉar. āṅkilēyar tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku mīṭparāka vantaṉar eṉṉum uṇmiyip palar oppukkoḷḷātu pōyiṉum, āṅkilar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu intiyā viṭutali peṟutaṟkum avar moḻiyākiya āṅkilamē tuṇiyāyiruntateṉpati, evarum maṟukkamuṭiyātu. āṅkilēyar kaḷḷaṅkara viṉṟi intiyarellārkkum āṅkilak kalvi kaṟpittu aṟivu pukaṭṭiṉar. ataṉāl, nāṭṭu varalāṟ ṟiyum āṭci muṟikaḷiyum kuṭiyurimiyiyum aṟinta intiyap perumakkaḷ, tēciya iyakkattit toṭaṅkip potu makkaḷit tuṇikkoṇṭu pōrāṭi āṅkila āṭciyiṉiṉṟu viṭutali vēṇṭi ṉar. āṅkilēyarum paṭippaṭiyāka urimiyaḷittu vantu iṟutiyil muḻu viṭutaliyum muḻu maṉattoṭu tāmāka aḷittuviṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, viṭutali peṟṟa intiyāvum pirittāṉiyac celvākkuḷḷa potu nalvāḻvu nāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntuvarukiṉṟatu. āṅkilak kalviyillāviṭiṉ, kāntiyum nēruvum pōṉṟa talivarum tōṉṟiyirār; intiyāvum viṭutali peṟṟirukkātu. ākavē, intiyarkkuk kaṇ tiṟantatum, viṭutali peṟa vaḻikāṭṭiyatum, āṅkilaroṭu pōrāṭi veṟṟi peṟa āṟṟaluḷḷa karuviyāyiruntatum, āṅkilamē. ātalāl, āṅkilatti aṭimippaṭuttiya moḻiyeṉak karutuvatu aṟiyāmiyēyām.
payaṉpāṭṭu moḻi
oruvar ulakameṅkum cuṟṟutaṟkum, aluval peṟutaṟkum, aṟivaṭitaṟkum maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟaroṭu uriyāṭutaṟkum āṅkilam utavu kiṉṟatu. kalvi niliyaṅkaḷiṟ kallātavarkūṭak kēḷvi yaṟivit tuṇik koṇṭu ayal nāṭṭāroṭu āṅkilattil uriyāṭip payaṉ peṟukiṉṟaṉar. 
mēṟkāṭṭiya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl āṅkilam veḷināṭṭil maṭṭu maṉṟi in nāṭṭilum iṉṟum matippu moḻiyāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. oruvar ōr intiya moḻiyil ettuṇic ciṟanta pulavarāyiruppiṉum, āṅkila maṟiyātavarāyiṉ perumpālum pōṟṟappeṟuvatilli. avar ārāycciyaṟi vellām ūmiyaṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavē. tamiḻāciriyaroṭu kalvi niliyat talivarkaḷ ki kulukkāmiyum araciyalaluvalakaṅ kaḷil tamiḻil viṉavuvār viṭi peṟāmiyum, tamiḻukku allatu nāṭṭu moḻikkuḷḷa avamatippiyum, āṅkilattiṟkuḷḷa matippi yumē kāṭṭum. tamiḻiyē kaṟpippavarāyiṉum āṅkilap paṭṭam peṟātār kallūrikaḷil amarttap peṟuvatilli. nāṭṭup paṟṟum nāṭṭu moḻippaṟṟum mikkavarākat tammik kāṭṭik koḷvārkūṭa, āṅkilamaṟiyātavari uḷḷattil avamatippatik kuṟippāl uṇaralām.
vaṭanāṭṭil inti veṟiyiruppatāl, teṉṉāṭṭiṟpōl nāṭṭu moḻiyavamatippilli. āyiṉ aḷaviṟanta, aṭāta āṅkila veṟuppi an nāṭṭār koṇṭuḷḷatu aruvarukkattakka ceyalākum. āṅki lattiṉ mītu oruvar ettuṇi veṟuppuk koṇṭiruppiṉum, ata ṉāṟ peṟum payaṉip peṟātu viṭuvatu, kuḷattōṭu kōpittuk koṇṭu kuḷiyāmaṟpōṉa ceyalēyākum. ātalāl aṟiviyaṉ moḻikkum aṉmoḻikkum vēṟupāṭaṟintu, āṅkilattik kaṭip piṭikka. kālañ cellac cella āṅkilap parappu viriyumēyaṉṟic curuṅkātu. ākavē, āṅkilatti akaṟṟak karutuvār, ati akaṟṟuvārē (akalikkac ceyvārē)yaṉṟi akaṟṟār (nīkkār).

viḷakkam:
2. ēntu - vacati.
5. ciṟu kūṭṭattik kuṟikkum Squad eṉṉum āṅkilac col caturattik kuṟikkum Squadra eṉṉum ittāliyac colliṉ tiripākum. cataḷam eṉṉum tamiḻccollum aṅṅaṉamē. caṭṭam-caṭam-caṭal-caṭalam-caturam (caturam); cataḷam-kūṭṭam. cataḷakkāraṉ-peruṅkuṭumpakkāraṉ.
7. Dress eṉṉum āṅkilaccol dresser eṉṉum paḻam pireñcuc col vāyilāka directus eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintatākum. Direct, to put Stright.
10. naṭu-naṭuvu-naṭuvam. immūṉṟiyum peyarākavum kuṟippup peyareccamākavum payaṉpaṭuttalām. naṭuvam eṉpatu peyarecca vaṭivil naṭuva eṉṟu īṟu keṭum.
11. aciyal eṉpatu etirmaṟi viyaṅkōḷ; īreṇ potu.
21. variciyāka, variciyamika eṉṟum collalām.
30, 31 nīḷeṭṭu, kuṟṟeṭṭu eṉṟālum pōtum. vikka eṉṟum iṭuka eṉṟum collalām. eṭṭu nīḷka, eṭṭuk kuṟuka eṉṟum collalām.
33. aṭiyiraṭṭutal-iṭṭa aṭiyiṉmēl aṭiyiṭutal: To step without advancing, to mark time. "aṭiyiraṭṭit tiṭṭāṭumāṭṭu" (puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māli. 2.8).
34. oruki vaṇakkattiṟkuk kiyeṭuttal eṉṉum col mikap poruttamāṉatām.
45. nōṭu-vaṭi vanaḷava-uṇṇōṭu-to inspect.
47. tulavutal-tulākkōl pōl toṅkap piṭittal, to let rifles hang balanced in one hand. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa paḻañcol. tul-tulā-tulām. tul-tuli-oppu. tulākkōl (tarācu). tōl eṉṉum inticcol tul eṉpataṉ tiripē.
43. paṭikkalam eṉpatu arm eṉpatupōla potuccol, tuppākki eṉpatu tupak eṉṉum turukkic col. tumukki eṉpatu tamiḻccol.
44. kāvalmēl eṉpatu vēlimēl eṉpatu pōṉṟavaḻakku. ēvalviṉikaḷ ceyya, ceyka eṉṉum iruvaṭivil uḷḷaṉa. ivi īreṇ potu. cataḷam eṉṉum tokutip peyari orumiyākak koṇṭu cey eṉṉum orumi vaṭivilum ēvalām. payiṟciyāciriyar vēṇṭiya tiruttamum ceytukoḷḷalām.

26
matippuri māli
koyyākkaṉi
(peruñcittiraṉār)
.....Üë¢êø¢ kaṇakkarum, eṉ paḻiya māṇavarumāṉa turi māṇikkaṉār iyaṟṟiyuḷḷa 'koyyākkaṉi' eṉṉum paṉuvalip pārviyiṭṭēṉ. veṇpāvum, akavalum, āciriya maṇṭilamum (viruttamum) icippāṭṭum, ākiya nāṉmaṇikaḷi viravik kōtta kōvipōlum, ip paṉuvaluḷ varuñ ceyyuṭkaḷellām, naviṉ ṟōrk kiṉimiyum, kēṭṭōrk kiṉpamum payakkumaḷavu coṟcuvi poruṭcuvi ceṟintu, āṟṟoḻukkāṉa oḻukici naṭiyil iyaṉṟu, ellārkkum poruḷ viḷaṅkumāṟu muntirip patamāṉa cempākamāy amintuḷḷaṉa. aḻakum oḷiyum amintu paṭṭi tīrtta maṇi kaḷūṭu avaṟṟi iṇittuccellum māṟṟuyarnta poṟkampi pōl, ip pāviṉaṅkaḷūṭu toṭarntu cellum katiyum ciṟantatoṉṟāy irā niṉṟatu.
katic ciṟappu.
uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttal,
uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarttal,
illōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttal,
illōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarttal.
eṉṉum nālvakik katiyuḷ etuvāyiṉum, makkaḷ vāḻkki paṟṟiya tāyiṉ, iṉpantarutal, ulakoṭu poruntal, viḻumiyatu payattal eṉṉum mūviyalpukaḷik koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum, allākkāl kati ciṟavātu.
matattuṟiyil maṭampaṭṭa paṇṭik kālattil ulakiyaṟ kov vāta pala tēviyaṟ katikkūṟukaḷ oru payaṉōkkic ciṟantaṉavākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa vēṉum, pakuttaṟiviyakkam paravivarum ik kālat tiṟku avi eḷḷaḷavum ēṟkā. koyyākkaṉi eṉṉum ip paṉuval taḻīiya kati, illōṉ talivaṉāka illatu puṇarttalē yāyiṉum, uḷḷōṉ talivaṉāka uḷḷatu puṇarttal pōl ulakiyaṟ porut tam uṭiyatu; paṭippōrkkum, kēṭpōrkkum iṉpam payappatu; pala ciṟanta uṇmikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi aṟavoḻukkatti vaṟpuṟuttuvatu. tikkaṟṟavarkkut teyvamē tuṇi; allatu akatikku aṟamē tuṇi; keṭuvāṉ kēṭu niṉippāṉ; irutalik kātalē iṉpantarum; 'mey vellum, poy tōṟkum' eṉpaṉa ip paṉuvaṟ katiyāl uṇarttappeṟum uṇmikaḷākum.
oru paṉuvalāciriyaṉiṉ matinuṭpamum, aṟivup parappum, avaṉ ceyyuḷ tiṟamiyāl maṭṭumaṉṟik katiyamippālum veḷippaṭuvaṉa vām. ip paṉuvalāciriyar ceyyuṭ ceyvatiṟ pōṉṟē putukkatip puṉivilum tērcci peṟṟiruppatu pārāṭṭattakkatu. kula, mata, kaṭcic cārpiṉṟit tamiḻākkam oṉṟiyē karutum cāṉṟōr, piṟkālattup perum pāvēntarātaṟ kēṟṟa ivviḷam pāvalarip peritum ūkkak kaṭavar.
'koyyākkaṉi' yeṉṉum ip paṉuval ellāt tamiḻarkkum koyyuṅ kaṉiyākuka!				     21.9.1955
paḻantamiḻ ici
(ku. kōtaṇṭapāṇip piḷḷi)
ekittiyam uṭpaṭa ulakici yaṉittiṟkum tamiḻiciyē tāyāyi ṉum, kaṭanta aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷākat tamiḻ, tamarālum māṟṟārālum (perumpāṉmi ilakkiyamum ciṟupāṉmi moḻiyum) ciṟitu ciṟitāka, maṟippum kuṟippum aḻippum paḻippum uṟṟu, talimit tamiḻp pulavarālum takkavāṟu aṟiyappaṭātu pōyiṉamiyiṉ, iṉṟu, makaḷ tāyip peṟṟāḷ eṉṉum muṟiyil iyaṟṟamiḻ vaṭamoḻi vaḻiyateṉṟum, icit tamiḻ karunāṭaka caṅkīta vaḻiyateṉṟum, nāṭakat tamiḻ parata cāttira vaḻiyateṉṟum, uṇmikku māṟāṉa karuttukaḷ ulaviyum ōraḷavu nilaviyum varukiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkumēl muṟṟum maṟiyuṇṭupōṉa icit tamiḻilakkaṇatti ituvari ārāynta aṟiñar : kālañ ceṉṟa, tañci (irāv cākipu) āpirakām paṇṭitar, maturi icik kuḻal (nātacuram) poṉṉuccāmip piḷḷi, īḻattu vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ ākiyamūvarum; itupōtu vāḻum, kōvi iciyaṇikalam (caṅkīta pūṣaṇam) irāmanātaṉ, ceṉṉi (ōyvu peṟṟa tuṇit taṇṭalāḷar) irāv cākipu ku. kōtaṇṭapāṇip piḷḷi ākiya iruvarum āvar.
ivaruḷ; āpirakām paṇṭitar, āyappāli vaṭṭappālit tirivu muṟikaḷik kaṇṭār; poṉṉuccāmip piḷḷiyār tāyp paṇkaḷ (mēḷakarttākkaḷ) muppattiraṇṭē eṉṟum, ici iṭaṅkaḷ (curat tāṉaṅkaḷ) paṉṉiraṇṭē, patiṉā ṟallaveṉṟum kaṇṭār; vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ viḻumiya ārāycciyāḷarēṉum, aṭippaṭi tavaṟākak koṇṭamiyiṉ palavuṇmikaḷik kāṇaviyalātu pōyiṉar; irāmanātaṉār mayaṅkā marapiṉ icimuṟi kāṭṭik kuralē caṭcam eṉṉum paḻaṅkoḷkiyi nāṭṭi, mullip paṇ mōkaṉam eṉṟu kaṇṭār; kōtaṇṭapāṇip piḷḷiyār, ainticimuṟi ēḻiciyiṟ kiḷiyāyntu vakuttatē eṉṟum, pēricikaḷē aminta ēḻici muṟiyum, pēriciyum ciṟṟiciyum kalanta paṉṉīrici muṟi yum ivaṟṟiṉ iṭiveḷikaḷāṉa irupattīralaku muṟiyum toṉṟu toṭṭuvarum tamiḻici marapēyeṉṟum, tam 'paḻantamiḻici'yil valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār.
poṟuppu vāynta uyarpatavit tamiḻāciriyarellām ūmiya rāyum ceviṭarāyum "eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟemakku" eṉṟirukkum ikkālattil, ōyvu peṟṟa araciya latikāriyār oruvar, vempālit taṇcōliyum kaṭum pañcat tiṭimaḻiyumpōla etirpārātavakiyil icittamiḻi iṅṅaṉaṅ kākkat tuṇintatu, iṟivaṉ ēṟpāṭē eṉpatu eṉ karuttu.
paṭikkatterinta uṇmit tamiḻaṉparellām ivaratu 'paḻan tamiḻici'yit tappātu vāṅkippaṭittup payaṉpeṟuka; ivar kaṇṭa uṇmikaḷip pāreṅkum parappuka. iṉṉum toṭarntāyntu itu pōṉṟa pala ariya ārāycci nūlkaḷi ākkumāṟu, iṟivaṉ ivarkku nalattoṭum valattoṭum neṭuvāḻvaruḷka!	    20.3.1959
kallūrit tamiḻilakkaṇat teḷivu
«êôñ¢ Üñ¢ñ£ð¢«ðì¢¬ìõ£í¼ñ¢ âù¢ ªî£ô¢è£ð¢ð¤ò ñ£í õ¼ñ£ù ¹ôõ£¢ ².ku. aruṇācalaṉār. è.î., (M.A.) kī. ka.i. (B.O.L.), kaliyiḷiñaṉ (B.A.), aṟiviyal iḷiñaṉ (B.Sc.) ākiya iru tērvup pāṭattiṭṭatti yoṭṭi, kaliyiḷiñaṉ ciṟapput tamiḻ māṇavarkkum kalittalivaṉ (M.A) tamiḻ māṇavarkkum payaṉpaṭu māṟu, mutaṉ mutalāy varintuḷḷa 'kallūrit tamiḻilakkaṇat teḷivu' eṉṉum nūlip pārviyiṭṭēṉ. ōriru ciṟu kuṟikaḷiruppiṉum itu peritum pōṟṟattakkatākum.
ulakil mutaṉmutalāy ilakkaṇa nūl eḻuntatu tamiḻē. iṉṟum, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉṉum muppāṟpaṭṭa muḻuniṟi vilakkaṇam uḷḷatum tamiḻē. tamiḻiṉ piṟantakamāṉa kumarikkaṇṭam mūḻkip pōṉamiyālum, āriya varukikku muṟpaṭṭa taṉittamiḻi lakkiyam muṟṟum iṟantupaṭṭami yālum, tamiḻiṉ uṇmi varalāṟu maṟiyuṇṭu kiṭappatālum, tamiḻiṉ muṉmiyiyum toṉmiyiyum naṭunili vāynta mēliyaṟiñarum aṟiyum nilimiyilli iṉi, ivaṟṟoṭu iṟṟit tamiḻariṉ kumukiya (camutāya)t tāḻvum, tolkāppiya kaṭikkaḻaka vilakkiyap payiṟciyiṉmiyum, kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyāri, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkum vēṟṟumiyamippum camaṟ kirutattip piṉpaṟṟiyaviyeṉṟu talikīḻāka māṟṟikkūṟac ceytuviṭṭaṉa.
neṭuṅkaṇakku
tamiḻolikaḷ iyalpāṉavum melliyaṉavumāy vaṭamoḻi yolikaḷiṉum vēṟupaṭṭaviyeṉpatu, cevippulaṉuḷḷa evarkkum teḷivām. āriyar nāvalan tēyattiṟ kālvittapōtu, avarmoḻi ilakkiyamaṟṟatum eḻutāk 
kiḷaviyumāyiruntatu. avar varukikkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē, tamiḻ muttamiḻilakkaṇamum paltuṟi yilakkiyamum koṇṭi runtatu. tamiḻeḻut tilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ, muṟi eṉpatum oṉṟu. itu mēliyāriya moḻi kaṭkum ēṉi moḻikaṭkum iṉṟu milli. ṭa, ṇa, ḷa, eṉṉum varuṭolikaḷ (linguals or cerebrals) camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiya vēta āriyamum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭaṉavē. itupōtuḷḷa paḻantamiḻ nūlāṉa tolkāppiyamum camaṟkiruta vilakkaṇamāṉa pāṇiṉīyattiṟku muntiyatē. pāṇiṉīyat tiṟku muntiya aintiram eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇamum, tamiḻnāṭ ṭil tamiḻilakkaṇattip piṉpaṟṟi yeḻuntatē. ātalāl, vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkē tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟi yamintateṉa uṇmiyaṟika.
vēṟṟumiyamippu
iṉi, tamiḻvēṟṟumi eṭṭum iyaṟkiyākavum, ēraṇa muṟip paṭṭum, taṉittaṉi vēṟupaṭṭa poruḷuṭiyaṉavākavum, irukkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtōṭu, mūvēṟu vakiyil peyar koṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. eṇ vakiyil, mutal vēṟṟumi, 2ām vēṟṟumi, 3ām vēṟṟumi, 4ām vēṟṟumi, 5ām vēṟṟumi, 6ām vēṟṟumi, 7ām vēṟṟumi, 8ām vēṟṟumi eṉṟum; poruḷ vakiyil, eḻuvāy vēṟṟumi, ceyporuḷ vēṟṟumi, karuvi vēṟṟumi, koṭi vēṟṟumi, nīkka vēṟṟumi, kiḻami vēṟṟumi, iṭa vēṟṟumi, viḷi vēṟṟumi, eṉṟum; urupu vakiyil, peyar vēṟṟumi, ai vēṟṟumi, oṭu vēṟṟumi, kuvvēṟṟumi, iṉ vēṟṟumi, atu vēṟṟumi, kaṇ vēṟṟumi, viḷi peyar (viḷikkappaṭum peyar) vēṟṟumi eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyilō, piratamā vipatti, tuvitīyā vipatti, tirutīyā vipatti, caturttī vipatti, pañcamī vipatti, caṭṭī vipatti, cattamīvipatti, campōtaṉap piratamā vipatti, eṉa eṇpaṟṟiyē eṇvēṟṟumiyum peyar peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
 3ām vēṟṟumip poruḷkaḷāṉa karuvi, viṉimutal (karuttā) uṭaṉikaḻcci ākiya mūṉṟum muraṇpaṭṭavi eṉṟu kālṭu velār karutu kiṉṟār. am mūṉṟiyum āyntu nōkkuvārkku, avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumi tōṉṟāmaṟ pōkātu. viṉimutal eṉpatu uyartiṇik karuviyātalāl, karuviyuḷ aṭaṅkum. iṉi, karuviyuḷ uṭaṉikaḻcciyum uṭaṉikaḻcciyuṭ karuviyum nuṇṇitāyk kalantu kiṭappati, nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulaṅ koṇṭum vaḻakku nōkkiyum uṇarntē, avviraṇṭiyum oru vēṟṟumippaṭuttiṉar tamiḻ mutaṉūlāciriyar. oru karuvi koṇṭu oru viṉi ceyyumpōtu ak karuvi ceyvāṉuṭa ṉiruppatiyum, oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi oru viṉi ceyyumpōtu, am maṟṟoruvaṉ tuṇiyāyniṉṟu karuvippaṭuvatiyum, kūrntu nōkkuka. 
iṉi, vaḻakku varumāṟu:-
āṉ (āl) urupu :
maṇṇāṉ iyaṉṟa kuṭam - karuvi
ūrāṉ oru kōvil - uṭaṉikaḻcci.
(ūrāṉ = ūrtoṟum)
oṭu urupu :
ūciyoṭu kuyiṉṟa tūcu - karuvi
nāyoṭu nampi vantāṉ - uṭaṉikaḻcci.
āṅkilattilum iv vaḻakku ottiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
With (oṭu) :
Write with a pen - karuvi
Went with him - uṭaṉikaḻcci
By (āl) :
Live by bread - karuvi
Stand by him - uṭaṉikaḻcci
iṅṅaṉamē, 5ām vēṟṟumikkuriya nīṅkal, oppu, elli, ētu eṉṉum nāṉkum, nīkkap poruḷiṉ nuṭpa vēṟupāṭukaḷē. iṅku oppu eṉṟatu uṟaḻ poruvi (comparative degree). oppup poruvu (positive degree) 2ām vēṟṟumikkuriyatām.
"aintā kuvatē
iṉeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi
Þîù¤ù¢ Þø¢ø¤¶ âù¢Âñ¢ Ü¶«õ."	(559)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvi nōkkuka.
3ām vēṟṟumikkuk karuvip poruḷoṭu uṭaṉikaḻccip poruḷ vaṭamoḻiyilumuḷḷatu. iti, "iṉic cakārttamāy vanta tirutiyiyic cakārttat tirutiyi eṉṟum, cakārttameṉṟum.. .... kūṟuvar" eṉṉum pirayōka vivēka uriyālum (pakkam, 11), "saha governs the instrumental" eṉṟu irāma kiruṭṭiṇa kōpāla pantarkkār tam camaṟkiruta mutaṟ puttakam 24ām pakkattil eḻutiyirukkum aṭikkuṟippālum, aṟika.
āṅkilattil eḻuvāy, kiḻami (possessive), ceyporuḷ (objective) eṉa mūvēṟṟumiyum; cerumāṉiyattil eḻuvāy, cey poruḷ (accusative) kiḻami (genitive) koṭi eṉa nālvēṟṟumi yum; ilattīṉil eḻuvāy, viḷi, ceyporuḷ (accusative) kiḻami (genitive) koṭi eṉa aivēṟṟumiyum; kirēkkattil eḻuvāy, kiḻami (genitive) koṭi, ceyporuḷ (accusative) 
viḷi, nīkkam eṉa aṟuvēṟṟumiyumē iruppatāka; avvammoḻi ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, ammoḻikaṭkiṉa māṉa vēta moḻiyum camaṟkirutamumākiya kīḻiyāriyattil maṭṭum tamiḻt toṭarpiṉṟi eṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷ eṅṅaṉan tōṉṟiyirukka muṭiyum?
iṉi, tamiḻil ettaṉi urupō attaṉi vēṟṟumi eṉṟum, tamiḻilakkaṇiyar vēṟṟumiyamippil camaṟkirutattip piṉpaṟṟiya tāl pala vēṟṟumikaḷi oru vēṟṟumikkuṭ ceṟittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟum, kālṭuvelār kūṟiyiruppatu, iṭap poruḷurupukaḷiṉ paṉmi paṟṟiyō palvēṟu vēṟṟumi kuṟittu varum collurupu paṟṟiyō, aṟikilam. 3ām vēṟṟumiyiṉiṉṟu uṭaṉikaḻcciyip pirittuviṭiṉum, vēṟṟumi mottam oṉpatēyākum. vēṟṟumiyeṉ patu poruḷpaṟṟiyatē yaṉṟi urupupaṟṟiyataṉṟu. tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟiya iyalpu moḻiyātaliṉ, atil ovvoru vēṟṟumiyiyum perum pālum poruḷtarum pala coṟkaḷ uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. āriya moḻikaḷō tiripu moḻikaḷātaliṉ, avaṟṟuḷ ovvoru vēṟṟumi yiyum poruḷmaṟinta orē urupu uṇarttukiṉṟatu. tamiḻar vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavareṉṟum, nākarikattil āriyariṉum tāḻntavareṉ ṟum iru tavaṟāṉa karuttukaḷ kālṭuvelār uḷḷattil kuṭikoṇṭi runtamiyē, avar tamiḻip piṟaḻa vuṇarntataṟkuk karaṇiyamām.
vēṟṟumi vaṭivik kuṟikkum urupu eṉṉum colli nōkkiṉum, atu tūya tamiḻē. uruttal=tōṉṟutal. uru=tōṟṟam, vaṭivu, vaṭivuṭip poruḷ. uru-uruvu-urupu. o.nā: aḷavu-aḷapu. uruvu-uruvam-rūpa (vaṭa col).
vēṟṟumi mayakkam eṉpatu, urupu mayakkam, poruḷ mayakkam eṉa iru vakippaṭum. iv vēṟupāṭṭip pulavar aruṇācalaṉār eṭuttukkāṭṭi yiruppatu pārāṭṭattakkatu. 
ceyviṉi, ceyappāṭṭuviṉi
tamiḻiṟ ceyviṉi pōṉṟē ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyum uṇṭeṉpatu, vaḻakkiṉālum, 
"viṉiyē ceyvatu ceyappaṭu poruḷē"
................ .................. ..................... .................
āyeṭṭeṉpa toḻiṉmuta ṉiliyē"	(596)
"ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōlat
ªî£ö¤ø¢ðìè¢ è¤÷î¢î½ñ¢ õöè¢è¤òô¢ ñó«ð."	(731)
eṉṉum, tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷālum, avaṟṟiluḷḷa "ceyappaṭu poruḷ" eṉṉum collāṭciyālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
āyiṉum, tamiḻil uṇmiyāṉapaṭi ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyē illi yeṉṟu kālṭuvelār tavaṟākak koṇṭār. itaṟkut tamiḻiṟ ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyiṉ arukiya vaḻakkum ataṉ palvēṟu vakiyumē karaṇiyam (kāraṇam). 
ceyappāṭṭuviṉip poruḷip perumpālum ceyviṉi vaṭivāṟ kuṟippatē, toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum tamiḻ vaḻakkām.
e-ṭu :	puli koṉṟa māṉ (=puliyāṟ kollappaṭṭa māṉ). 
	taccaṉ ceyta peṭṭi (=taccaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa peṭṭi).
āṅkilattilum pala piṟa moḻikaḷilum ceyappāṭṭuviṉi vaṭivam perumpālum oṉṟē. tamiḻilō, av viṉi nikaḻkāla viṉiyeccattōṭu kūṭiya tuṇiviṉi, mutaṉilit toḻiṟ peya rōṭu kūṭiya tuṇiviṉi, eṉṉum mūvaṭivu koḷḷum.
iṉi, cila ceyalkaḷ paṟṟi, eḻuvāyum ataṉ viṉiyumiṉṟic ceyappaṭu poruḷiyē ceytatupōlac ceyviṉi koṭuttuk kūṟuvatum, tamiḻ vaḻakkām. itaṉiyē
"ceyappaṭu poruḷic ceytatu pōlat
toḻiṟpaṭak kiḷattalum vaḻakkiyaṉ marapē"
eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
e. ṭu: tiṇṇi meḻukiṟṟu, kalaṅ kaḻuviṟṟu.
itaṉi, ippōtutāṉ vantiṟṟu, caṟṟu muṉputāṉ tūṅki eḻuntiṟṟu, eṉat taṉmi yorumi viṉiyip paṭarkki yoṉ ṟaṉpāl viṉiyākak kūṟum tamiḻ vaḻakkiṟ koppākak koḷḷiṉum kuṟṟamiṉṟu. vantiṟṟu, eḻuntiṟṟu eṉpaṉa, kocci naṭiyil vanticcu eḻunticcu eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṅkilattiṟ ceyappāṭṭuviṉiyiṉ peyarāyuḷḷa passive eṉṉum colliṉ aṭiyāṉa pat eṉṉum ilattīṉ vērc collē, paṭu eṉṉum tamiḻc colli oliyālum poruḷālum ottiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
uriccol
peyarccol, viṉiccol, peyarkkum viṉikkum uṟuppāka varum iṭiccol eṉa ilakkaṇavakic col uṇmiyil mūṉṟē. peyariyum viṉiyiyum taḻuvivarum collellām peyarecca mākavum viṉiyecca mākavum niṉṟu, peyaruḷḷum viṉiyuḷḷum aṭaṅkum. 
tolkāppiyattil uriccollākak kāṭṭappeṟṟavi yellām peyar viṉi yiṭiyuḷ aṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉi mūṉṟiṟkup pōl uriccoṟkut taṉi iyal variyaṟiyilli.
uriccoṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappukunta tolkāppiyar, 
"payilā tavaṟṟip payiṉṟavi cārtti
............................................................................
âê¢ªê£ô¢ô£ò¤Âñ¢ ªð£¼÷¢«õÁ è¤÷î¢îô¢."	
(uriyiyal, 1)
eṉpatiyē ciṟappākak kūṟi, ati viḷakkum mukamākap pala ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷit taṉittaṉi niṟutti, akaramutali (akarāti)  muṟiyiṟ poruḷ kūṟic celkiṉṟār. 
iciyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟal, peyariṉum viṉiyiṉum mey taṭumāṟal, oru coṟ palaporuṭkurimi tōṉṟal, palacol oru poruṭkurimi tōṉṟal, eṉṉum nāṉkum ellāc coṟkum potu vilakkaṇamātal aṟika. 
tolkāppiyar uriccoṟkuk kūṟum ilakkaṇattiyum, ataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭum coṟkaḷiyum ūṉṟi nōkkumpōtu, ceyyuḷi lēyē ciṟappāy vaḻaṅkum collum, ceyyuḷiṟ ciṟappāṉa poru ḷik koḷḷum collumē, uriccolleṉat teriya varukiṉṟatu.

eṉpatu, veḷippaṭṭa kuḻūuk kuṟiyum veḷippaṭāta kuḻūukkuṟiyum pōṉṟa iruvakic coṟkaḷip paṟṟiyatākum.

palvakip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki

oruvā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa uriccol".	(442)

eṉak kūṟiṉār eṉka. itaṉāl, tolkāppiya uriyiyalē iṭikkāla nikaṇṭu eṉṉum uriccoṟṟokuti vāyilāy iṟṟi akaramutalikku mūlam eṉa aṟika.
uriccoṟkaḷi yellām tokuttup poruḷ kūṟum coṟ ṟokutikaḷ (nikaṇṭukaḷ), vaṭanūlāriṉ vērc coṟṟokutiyi (tātu rūpā vaḷiyi) orupuṭiyottiruttalāl, perumpālum vērc collāyirukkum viṉiyaṭikaḷi yellām uriccollāka mayaṅki ṉar civañāṉa muṉivar.
cila uriccoṟkaḷ kuṟippup peyareccamākavum kuṟippu viṉi yeccamākavum iruttal paṟṟi, oru, iru mutaliya kuṟippup peyareccaṅ kaḷiyum cilar tamiḻilakkaṇap pāṭap pottakaṅkaḷil uriccol leṉak kuṟippā rāyiṉar. ivi, potuvaḷavup paṇpuṇarttum ciṟu, peru eṉpaṉa pōl, eṇṇaḷavup paṇpuṇarttum kuṟippup peyarec caṅkaḷē (adjectives) eṉa aṟika.
ticiccol
ticiccol eṉpaṉa, paṇṭic centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻavi runta pala ticik koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkiya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷ. ākavē, centamiḻ nāṭṭuc col ticiccol eṉappaṭātu. 
tamiḻiṉ piṟantakam teṉperuvāriyil mūḻkippōṉa paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭātalālum, tamiḻi vaḷartta mukkaḻakamum pāṇṭi nāṭṭi lēyē iruntami yālum, iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭilēyē ōraḷavu ciṟanta tamiḻ payiṉṟu varutalālum, pāṇṭināṭṭit tamiḻ nāṭeṉ ṟum, pāṇṭiyaṉit tamiḻ nāṭaṉeṉṟum uriccoṟṟokutikaḷ (nikaṇṭukaḷ) ciṟappittuk kūṟuvatālum,
"cantaṉap potiyac centamiḻ muṉiyum
cavuntara pāṇṭiya ṉeṉuntamiḻ nāṭaṉum
caṅkap pulavarun taḻittiṉi tirukkum
ñé¢èôð¢ ð£í¢® õ÷ï£ ªìù¢ð."
"vēḻa muṭittu malināṭu mētakka
cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭittu-pūḻiyarkōṉ
teṉṉāṭu muttuṭittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭi
ïù¢ù£´ ê£ù¢«ø£ ¼¬ìî¢¶."	(auviyār)
"nallampar nalla kuṭiyuṭittu cittaṉvāḻ
villan toṟumūṉ ṟeriyuṭittu-nallaravap 
pāṭṭuṭittu cōmaṉ vaḻivanta pāṇṭiyaniṉ
ï£ì¢´¬ìî¢¶ ïô¢ô îñ¤ö¢."
eṉṉum paḻam pāṭṭukkaḷālum, pāṇṭināṭē centamiḻ nāṭeṉpatu teḷivām.
tamiḻnāṭu parappilum moḻittiṟattilum kuṉṟikkoṇṭē vantatiṉāl, centamiḻ nāṭṭaḷavu kālantōṟum māṟikkoṇṭēyi runtirukkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, piṟkālattār kūṟukiṉṟavāṟu, vikiyiṉ vaṭakku centamiḻ nāṭākātu.
centamiḻ nāṭṭic cūḻnta paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nilamāvaṉa: "kumari yāṟṟiṉ teṉkarippaṭṭa paḻantīpamum kollamum kūpakamum ciṅkaḷamum; ciyattiṉ mēṟkuppaṭṭa koṅkaṇamum tuḷuvamum kuṭakamum kuṉṟakamum, kiḻakkuppaṭṭa karunaṭamum, vaṭukum teliṅkum kaliṅkamum" eṉṟār teyvac ciliyār. ciṅkaḷamoḻi tōṉṟumuṉ, koṭuntamiḻ nilam iṉṉum vēṟupaṭṭiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. naṉ ṉūlār, tolkāppiyar eṇṇiya paṉṉiru nilattiṟku mēlum āṟu cērttup patiṉeṇṇilam eṉṟatu peruntavaṟām. itaṉāl, ticic colli ayaṉmoḻic colleṉap perumpulavarum mayaṅka nērntatu.
ticiccol eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc collē. palaticiyi ṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkiyatāl, oru kuṟippiṭṭa nāṭṭiṉ allatu kiḷimoḻiyiṉ peyarāl atik kuṟiyātu, potuppaṭat ticic col eṉṟaṉar ilakkaṇiyar. ticic col, ciṟappuccollum ciṟappup poruḷiḻanta collum eṉa iru vakippaṭum.
e-ṭu : teluṅku :	irāyacam - ciṟappuc col. 
			ceppu - ciṟappup poruḷiḻanta col.
tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya ayaṉmoḻic col vaṭacol oṉṟē. ataṉāl ati vaṭacol eṉap pirittuk kūṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉamē, piṟa ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷiyum avvam moḻip peyarāṟ kuṟittal vēṇṭum. muṉpu koṭuntamiḻāyiruntaṉa iṉṟu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ eṉat tirintum vaṭamoḻiyoṭu kalantum uḷḷaṉa. itaṉālum, ticiccol eṉpatu ayaṉmoḻic col leṉac cila ilakkaṇap pulavarum piṟaḻntuṇara nērntatu. cila tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ āriyamākavum māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. itip pañca tiraviṭam eṉ ṉum paṇṭi vaḻakkum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻi varalāṟum uṇarttum.

atu, aḵtu.
atu, aḵtu eṉṉum iru collum orē colliṉ iruvēṟu vaṭivām. iṅṅaṉamē, itu, iḵtu, utu, uḵtu eṉpaṉavum.
"muṉṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
maṉṉal vēṇṭum alvaḻi yāṉa"	(423)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāppaṭi, aḵtāṭi, iḵtili, uḵtīyam eṉa, uyirmutaṟ collāṉa varumoḻimuṉ āytamiṭikkoṇṭa cuṭṭuppeyarē alvaḻiyil nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu puṇaral vēṇṭum.
itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, "muṉṉeṉṟataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum ivviti koḷka. aḵtaṭivu. aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭā murupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ēṉi ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka" eṉa vilakkuk kūṟiṉār. iv vilakkuppaṭiyē, 
"viḻuppēṟṟiṉ aḵtoppa tilliyār māṭṭum"	(162)
"aḵtaṟi kallātavar"	(427)
eṉṉum kuṟaḷaṭikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ivi vēṟṟumit tokiyām.
itaṉāl, aḵtu eṉṉum āytam peṟṟa vaṭivam perum pāṉmi eḻuvāy niliyilum, ciṟupāṉmi piṟa vēṟṟumittoki niliyilum, uyirmutaṟ colloṭu nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu puṇarum eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
muṟṟukaram iyalpāṉa uyirātalāl, atu eṉṉuñ col,
"ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal variyār"	(140)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviṟkēṟpa, uyiroṭu puṇariṉ uṭampaṭu mey peṟum.

mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, eṉṟum uyirmutaṟ colmuṉ aḵtu eṉ ṉum āytampeṟṟa cuṭṭuc collē varavēṇṭumeṉpatu tavaṟeṉ patum, atu eṉṉum muṟṟukara vīṟṟuc collum varalāmeṉpatum, aḵtu eṉpatu uyir mutaṟcol muṉṉarēyē perumpāṉmi eḻuvāyākavum ciṟupāṉmi 
vēṟṟumit tokiyākavum varumeṉpatum, atu eṉpatu perum pāṉmi uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum ciṟupāṉmi ukaraṅ keṭṭum uyiroṭu puṇaru meṉpatum, aṟiyappaṭum.

iṅku aḵtu eṉṉum coṟkuk kūṟiyatu iḵtu, uḵtu eṉpavaṟ ṟiṟkum, atu eṉṉum coṟkuk kūṟiyatu itu, utu eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum, okkum eṉa aṟika.
vēṇṭum, vēṇṭām, vēṇṭā.
tamiḻmoḻi muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭiyātirunta kālattil, ceytu, ceykiṉṟu, ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiccoṟkaḷē paṇṭic cēra nāṭākiya maliyāḷattiṟpōl muṟiyē mukkālamum paṟṟiya muṟṟukkaḷā yiruntuvantaṉa. avi pālīṟupeṟṟu iṟṟi vaṭivuṟṟapiṉ, iṭikkālattil ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum vāypāṭu yātu karaṇiyam paṟṟiyō ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṟṟuppōy, ataṟkīṭākac ceyyum eṉṉum vāypāṭē nikaḻkāla viṉiyāy vaḻaṅki vantiruk kiṉṟatu. ilakkaṇa vuriyāciriyar ataṉ kuṟipāṭṭi nīkkac ceyyā niṉṟāṉ eṉṟoru vāypāṭṭip paṭit tirukkiṉṟaṉar. iti vīracōḻiyamum ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷumē oppukkoṇṭuḷḷaṉa.
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu, iṟutiyil palarpāloḻinta paṭarkki nāṟpāṟkē variyaṟukkappaṭ ṭiruntirukkiṉṟatu. iti, 
"pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi
avvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻuṅ kālattuc

ēvalorumi.
tamiḻil ēvalorumi īṟu peṟṟum peṟātum varum. iyalpāṉa peyar vaṭivam orumiyāyum, ataṉ īṟu peṟṟa vaṭivam paṉmiyāyum iruttal pōl; iyalpāṉa viṉivaṭivam ēvalorumiyāyum, ataṉ īṟupeṟṟa vaṭivam ēvaṟ paṉmiyāyum, varum eṉka.
iruvaki ēvalorumiyuḷ, īṟu peṟātatē iyalpāṉatum ciṟappuṭiyatumākum. ulakavaḻakkil īṟu peṟātatē orumiyēvalā yiruttal kāṇka. ovvoru moḻiyum potumakkaḷamippeṉṟum, ataṉ ilakkiyamē pulamakka ḷamippeṉṟum, ulaka vaḻakku moḻiyē ilakkiya vaḻakku moḻikku muntiyatum mūlamumāku meṉṟum, teṟ ṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
oruvaṉi nōkki oṉṟic ceyyumāṟu ēvumpōtu, avaṉ maṟuppiṉ, iti yeṉakkuc ceyyamāṭṭāyā eṉṉum viṉāpporu ḷil, itic ceyyamāṭṭāy eṉṟu kural māṟṟi irappatu vaḻakkam. ak kuṟippu viṉāviṉ poruḷ iticcey eṉpatē. itaṉiyē,

tamiḻaṉ eṅkē?
(teṉpulōliyūr mu.kaṇapatip piḷḷi)
ekittiyam, cumēriyam, pāpilōṉiyam, yūtam, kirēkkam, urō māṉiyam mutaliya paṇṭi nākarikaṅkaṭkellām muntiyatāyum aṭip paṭiyāyum, iṉṟu aṟiviyaṟ kuṉṟēṟi niṟkum mēlināṭukaḷum iruṇṭirunta niliyil teruṇṭu niṉṟatāyum uḷḷa ulaka mutal nākarikattiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum kaṇṭu avaṟṟip pala ticiyum parappiya oppuyarvaṟṟa tamiḻaṉ eṅkē?
antō! vantēṟikaḷāl mayakkuṇṭu, maṇavuṟavum uṇavuṟavum aṟṟa palvēṟu ciṉṉañciṟu pakutikaḷākac citikkappaṭṭu, oṟṟumi yiḻantu, mēṉmēlum vevvēṟu ayalārk kaṭimippaṭṭut tam muṉṉōriṉ perumiyi muṟṟum maṟantu, nāṭiḻantu nakariḻantu nūliḻantu colliḻantu, maṟamāṉa matikeṭṭut tāynāṭṭil urimi yiṉṟip perumpālum taṟkuṟikaḷāy uṭalōṭu kūṭi niṟkum tamiḻaruk kum viṭutaliyuṇṭō veṉṟu kavaṉṟu kalaṅkum niliyil, avarkku mayakkan teḷivittu matikūṭṭum maruntāka, ilaṅki araciyal moḻi peyarppāḷar, teṉpulōliyūr mu. èíðî¤ð¢ð¤÷¢¬÷ Üõ£¢è÷¢ â¿î¤ ªõ÷¤ò¤ì¢´÷¢÷ 'îñ¤öù¢ âé¢«è?' eṉṉum tamiḻ varalāṟṟārāycci nūl eḻuntu iṉkāṟṟu vīcukiṉṟatu.
in nūlil, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamākiya 'ilemūriyā' (Lemuria) eṉṉum kumari nāṭṭu varalāṟum, ilaṅki ataṉ eñciya pakutiyeṉ patum, yāḻppāṇam eṉṉum peyar vanta vakiyum, aṅku vaḻaṅkum cila paḻamiyāṉa tamiḻ vaḻakkāṟukaḷum piṟavum collappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
itaṉkaṇ uḷḷa cila ariya ceytikaḷuḷ iraṇṭu varumāṟu:
1. paṉāṭṭu
"paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭuvaru kāli
niliyiṉ ṟākum aiyeṉ uyirē
ākāram varutal āvayi ṉāṉa"	(tol. eḻu. 284)
'paṉi' eṉṉum nilimoḻiyiṉ muṉ 'aṭṭu' eṉṉum varu moḻi variṉ, muṉṉataṉ aikāram keṭṭu avviṭattu ākāram vantēṟip 'paṉāṭṭu' eṉṟu muṭiyum eṉpatu, in nūṟpāviṉ poruḷ.
'paṉāṭṭu' eṉpatu, iṟṟit tamiḻpperu nilattil collaḷavi lāyiṉum poruḷavilāyiṉum eṅkēṉum vaḻakkililli. atu paṉaṅ karuppukkaṭṭikkup paṇṭik kālattu vaḻaṅkiya collāyirukkalā meṉṟu cilar uṉṉippāyk kūṟiṉar. Ýò¤ù¢, Ü¶ Üçîù¢ªøù¢ð¶, 'îñ¤öù¢ âé¢«è?' eṉṉum nūlāl teriyavarukiṉṟatu.
"paṉampaḻatti eṭuttu urittu, puḷiṅkāṭi viṭṭup picintu, kaḷiyākap pāyil ūṟṟi, veyiliṟ kāyaviṭṭu tayārikkappaṭuvatu; tōṟṟattil cokkilēṟṟip (Chocolate) pōṉṟatāyirukkum" eṉṟu in nūlāciriyar kūṟukiṉṟār. (pak. 49,50)
itaṉāl, mēṉāṭṭār pōṉṟē pala ariya uṇporuṭ kaḷip paṇṭit tamiḻar uruvākkiṉar eṉpatum, avaṟṟuṭ pala iṟṟit tamiḻakattil vaḻakkiṟantaṉa eṉpatum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

"paṉiyeṉa varuuṅ kālavēṟ ṟumik(ku)

in nūṟpākkaḷ, 'paṉi', 'maḻi', 'veyil' eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyil, 'paṉiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ', 'maḻiyattuc ceṉṟāṉ', 'veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ' eṉa attuc cāriyi peṟum eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ittakiya vaḻakku iṟṟit tamiḻanāṭākiya cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷi lillāviṭiṉum, paḻañ cēranāṭākiya maliyāḷa nāṭṭil iṉṟumuḷḷatu. 'paṉiyattup pōkarute', 'maḻiyattup pōkarute', 'veyilattup pōkarute' eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻakkukaḷ aṅku iṉṉum iyalpākavuḷḷaṉa. ('pōkarute' eṉpatu 'pōkātē' eṉṉum etirmaṟi yēvaliṉ maṟuvaṭivam.)
ituvari, iv vaḻakku maliyāḷa nāṭṭiṟkē ciṟappeṉak karutiyiruntōm. āyiṉ, tiru. mu. kaṇapatippiḷḷi avarkaḷ itu yāḻppāṇattilu muḷḷateṉat terivikkiṉṟārkaḷ.

(pak. 47) eṉpatu avarkaḷ kūṟṟu.
iṉi, āciriyar 'mū', 'eḻu' eṉṉum eṇṇup peyareccaṅkaḷi nāṭṭup peyarākak koḷḷum putuk karuttu, karuttu vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kiṭamāṉatu.

tamiḻ nūl
(ta. ca. tamiḻaṉ)
tiruvārūr iyaṟṟamiḻp payiṟṟakattiṉ tamiḻāciriyar pulavar ta. caravaṇat tamiḻaṉār iyaṟṟiya potumuṟit tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlip pārviyiṭṭēṉ.
āciriyar nūli eḻuttiyal, colliyal, uṟuppiyal, puṇariyal, potuviyal, oḻipiyal, toṭariyal eṉa ēḻiyalkaḷākap pakuttu, ik kāla māṇavar tamiḻip piḻiyaṟap pēcutaṟkum, eḻututaṟkum iṉṟiya miyāta ilakkaṇaṅ kaḷiyellām 433 nūṟpāvālum avaṟṟiṉ uriyālum viḷakki yirukkiṉṟār.
ilakkaṇaviḷakka nūlāciriyarum ilakkaṇakkottu nūlāciriya rum pōla āciriyarē uri varintiruppatāl, mūlattiṉ uṇmip poruḷip piṟaḻa uṇarntu palvēṟu urikaḷ tōṉṟi māṇavari mayakkaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟām.
coṟkaḷiṉ tirivu muṟikaḷiyum marūukkaḷiṉ mūlattiyum kāṭṭalum, niṟuttakkuṟiyilakkaṇatti nūṟpāvilamittalum, tamiḻiṉ tūymiyip pōṟṟalum, kulamatakaṭcic cārpiṉṟip pulamiyip pārāṭṭalum, in nūliṉ ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷām.
vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttut tiripik kūṟumpakuti, tamiḻiṉ tūymi yip pōṟṟaloṭu muraṇpaṭutaliṉ, atu koḷḷattakka taṉṟu. tamiḻeḻuttu varivaṭivu māṟṟamum tamiḻukkut tēviyilli.
"kariyāṭak keṇṭi kayattāṭa maññi
curiyāḻa ammi mitappa-variyaṉiya
yāṉikku nīttu muyaṟku niliyeṉpa
è£ùè ï£ìù¢ ²¬ù."
eṉṉum paḻiya nērici veṇpāvi, piṟarellām mutalaṭi yiṉṟic cintiyal veṇpāvākavē kāṭṭuvar. innūlāciriyar ataṉ muḻuvaṭiviyum varintiruppatu pārāṭṭattakkatu. āyiṉ atip pāṭiyavariṉ karuttu, koṭuṅkōl maṉṉaṉiṉ allatu āṭci muṟiyaṟiyā aracaṉiṉ nāṭṭu vāḻkkiyiṉ tāṟumāṟāṉa nilimiyi uṇarttuvatēyaṉṟi, moḻimāṟṟup poruḷkōṭku eṭuttukkāṭ ṭamippataṉṟu eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppākak koṇṭavaṟṟuḷ, oṉṟi raṇṭi māṟṟikkoḷvatu naṉṟām. e-ṭu : nirupar-maṭalar. itu aṟikkiyāḷar eṉṟiruppiṉ mikap poruttamām. oru cila marūukkaḷiṉ mūlavaṭivum māṟṟappaṭal vēṇṭum.
potuvāka, cila naṟkūṟukaḷip putuvatākavum, tuṇiccalākavum kiyāṇṭa in nūlāciriyari ūkkuvatu takkatām. aṭutta patippu muṟṟun tiruntiya muṟiyil veḷivarumeṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.	9.4.1972

kēḷvic celvam
kiruṭṭiṇacāmi, ceṉṉappanāyakkaṉ pāḷiyam.
*collukku 'naṭuvil' 'ra' mutal 'ru' īṟāka uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ varumā?
'ra' mutal 'ru' īṟākavuḷḷa uyir meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ, ra, rā, ri, ru, ri, rō eṉpavi taṉic colliṭiyāka varum.
pālakiruṭṭiṇaṉ, maturi-1.
* tamiḻ moḻiyiṉiṉṟu ēṉiya teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, maliyāḷa moḻikaḷ pirintiruppiṉ avaṟṟukkut tamiḻ eḻuttiṉṟi maṟṟōr eḻuttu muṟi vantatu eppaṭi?
teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ tamiḻil allāta vallolikaḷip peṟṟataṉālum, āriyac cērkkikaḷālum, āriyamuṟi yoṭṭiya neṭuṅkaṇakkik koṇṭaṉa.
* tamiḻnāṭu taṉiyākap pirintāl, iṉṟiya poruḷiyal niliyil ceḻippōṭu vāḻa muṭiyumā?
tamiḻnāṭu taṉiyākap pirintu taḻittōṅkuvataṟkuriya ceḻumika ḷellām ataṟkirukkiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭik kālattil cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷum piṟkālattil nāyakka marāṭṭiya aracukaḷum, taṉittu vāḻntirukka, cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭukaḷ iṇinta pakuti iṉi ēṉ taṉittu vāḻamuṭiyātu? ik kālattil iraciyā, amerikkā, iṅkilāntu, pirāṉcu mutaliya cila nāṭukaḷ tavira ēṉiyavellām perumpālum mēṉāṭukaḷiṉ tuṇikoṇṭē vāḻvaṉavākum. intiyā mēlit tuṇi koṇṭu vāḻvatu pōṉṟē tamiḻnāṭum mēlit tuṇi koṇṭu vaḷampeṟa vāḻum.
kā. ira. pakalavaṉ, ceṉṉi.
* akattiyar tamiḻarā? vaṭamoḻiyāḷarā? akattiyam tol kāppiyattiṟku muntiyatā? mutaṉ mutalil tamiḻukku ilakkaṇam vakuttavar akattiyarā?
akattiyar āriyar. akattiyam tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatu. akattiyar kālam ēṟattāḻa ki. mu. 1500. tamiḻukku mutalilakkaṇam varinta tamiḻ muṉivar kālam ki. mu. 10,000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ. āriya varu kikku muṟpaṭṭa taṉit tamiḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām aḻintum aḻikkap paṭṭum pōyiṉa. tamiḻ mutaṉūliṉ toṉmi varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭātatu.
* 'tuṇaṅki' eṉum nāṭṭiya vaki taṟkālattuk kāṇappaṭum "Rock'n Roll" vakiyic cērntatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvatu uṇmiyā? illeṉiṉ aḵtu evvakiyic cērntatu?
tuṇaṅki kuravik kūttu vakiyic cērntatu.
ā. vēlāyutam, koḻumpu.
* vaḷḷuvar tirunāḷ koṇṭāṭuvatu paṟṟi nālvakik karuttukaḷ iṅku nilavukiṉṟaṉa. (1) ti mutal nāḷ (2) māci uttaram (3) paṅkuṉit tiru vōṇam (4) vikāci aṉuṭam - ākiya nāṭkaḷil koṇṭāṭalām eṉa nāṉku piriviṉar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivipaṟṟit taṅkaḷ karuttukaḷ yāvi?
vaḷḷuvar tirunāḷippaṟṟi iṟṟi niliyil tiṭṭamāy oṉṟum collaviyalātu. ti mutal nāḷ poṅkal viḻāvātalāl vēṟoru nāḷil vaḷḷuvar tirunāḷik koṇṭāṭuvatu nallatu. ati aṟiñarkūṭi muṭivu ceytal vēṇṭum.
caravaṇakkumār, ceṉṉi.
* kāmam eṉpatu tamiḻc collā? kāmattiṟkum kātalukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭeṉṉa? kāmam iḻintateṉṟāl vaḷḷuvar "kāmattuppāl" eṉṟu amittatēṉ? ati 'iṉpattuppāl' eṉṟu cilar koḷvatu poruntumā?
'kāmam' tamiḻc collē. kāmam kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyariṭippaṭṭa pēraṉpu; kātal piṟariṭippaṭṭa pēraṉpu. vaḷḷuvar kālattil kāmam eṉṉum col piṟkālattiṟpōl iḻivaṭiyavilli. 'iṉpam' potuc collātalāl, kāmam eṉṉum ciṟappuc colli āḷvatē naṉṟu.
kō. maliyaracaṉ, malāyā.
* tamiḻiṉ pīṭaḻikkum intiyi etirttaḻikkum nōkkōṭu cilar āṅkilattiyum etirkka viḻivatu aṟivuṭimiyā?
āṅkilatti etirppatu aṟivuṭimiyākātu.
irācacuntaram, cittiraccāvaṭi.
* 'iṉimi'yi luṟinal leḻilcēr 'ḻa'vuṭaṉ ilaṅkum 'ḵ'-um emmoḻi uṭittu?

muṉiyappaṉ, karttārappaṭṭi.
* ti. mu. kaḻakattip paṟṟit taṅkaḷ karutteṉṉa?
ti. mu. kaḻakam tamiḻ nāṭṭil āṭciyik kippaṟṟum vāyppuḷḷa talimiyāṉa etirppuk kaṭci.
aṟivaṉ, paṟampi.
* caṅkiṉam kūṭi vāḻvatāl 'caṅkam' eṉṟa col tamiḻk kaḻakattik kuṟippatākak koḷvatu poruntumā?
kaḻakattik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉṉum col sangha eṉṉum vaṭa colliṉ tiripākum. nantik kuṟikkum caṅkam eṉpatē, vaṭa collākak kūṟappaṭiṉum, tūya teṉcollām.
curi-cari-carukku, carukkutal = vaḷital carukku + am = carukkam (vaṭṭam). carukku + aram = carukkaram-cakkaram = vaṭṭam. vaṇṭi (Wheel). carukkaram-carukkari = vaṭṭamāṉa accu vellam. ō nō: vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa paṉi vellam.
carukkari-carkkari-cakkari. carkkari yeṉpatu piṟkālattil piṟa vaṭivāṉa vella accukkaḷiyum utiri vellattiyum, maṇaṟ cīṉiyiyum kuṟittatu. cakkaram-akkaram-akkāram = carkkari.
carukku-cakku-cekku = vaṭṭamāy āṭum eṇṇey āli.
cakku-caṅku = valamākavum iṭamākavum vaḷintatu (valampuri, iṭampuri)
ō.nā: puri = caṅku, vaḷi = caṅku. purital = vaḷital.
caṅku-caṅkam = periya caṅku, caṅku. 'am' eṉpatu oru perumip poruṭ piṉṉoṭṭu. e-ṭu: nili-niliyam, mati-matiyam, viḷakku viḷakkam.
aṟivaraci, iḷiyāṉkuṭi.
* colliṉ celvar irā. pi. cētu (ppiḷḷi) tamiḻukkuc ceyta toṇṭupaṟṟi uṅkaḷ karutteṉṉa?
eṉatu 'ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyiṉ cīrkēṭu' eṉṉum tiṟaṉāyvuc cuvaṭiyip pārkka.
* tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ etir kālam eppaṭiyirukkum?

meymmoḻi, mukavi.
* viyāpuri (ppiḷḷi) 'āriya aṭimi' eṉpatu tavira avar ciṟanta ārāycciyāḷar eṉṟum avarum palavakiyil tamiḻppaṇi purintuḷḷār eṉṟum oppukkoḷḷalāmā?

ka. nākarācaṉ, maturi.
* ivvulakil enta oru makkaḷ tokutiyilāvatu piṟa moḻik kalappaṟṟa oru taṉi moḻi vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatā? ammoḻi vaḻaṅku vatu uṇmiyāyiṉ ataṉ kālam, varalāṟu ivaṟṟic curukkamākak kūṟa muṭiyumā? avvāṟu illiyeṉiṉ, nām taṉit tamiḻ iyakkam toṭaṅkuvatu ulaka vaḻakkukkup poruttamākap paṭukiṉṟatā? kālap pōkkil moḻik kalappum iṉak kalappum nāṭṭuk kalappum tōṉṟattāṉē ceyyum? avaṟṟi ēṟṟuk koḷvatil tavaṟeṉṉa? eṉpavaṟṟi virivāka eḻuta vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
makkaḷ moḻikaḷ, avaravar nākarika nilikkēṟṟavāṟu ciṟitāyum peritāyum paṇpaṭṭum paṇpaṭātum colvaḷaṅ koṇṭuṅ koḷ ḷātu mirukkum. paṇpaṭṭa moḻiyāyiṉ colvaḷaṅ koṇṭē yirukkum. paṇpāṭu ilakkaṇa vamitiyum nuṇ poruḷ vēṟupāṭum paṟṟiyatākum.
paṇpaṭāta, āttirēliya āppirikka moḻikaḷum, amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ moḻikaḷum, nakkavāram pāliṉīciyam pōṉṟa nākarikamaṟṟa tīvu moḻikaḷum, cila malivāṇar moḻikaḷum ēṟattāḻa aimpatu mutal mūvāyiram vari coṟkaḷ koṇṭavi. avi taṉi moḻikaḷāyiruppiṉum colvaḷa miṉmiyāṟ ciṟappaṟṟavi. ikkāla nākarika makkaḷiṉ ulaka vaḻakkup pēcciṟkuk kuṟinta pakkam irupati ṉāyiram coṟkaḷ vēṇṭum. āṅkila cākacaṉiyac coṟkaḷ (Anglo Saxon) ēṟattāḻa irupattunālāyiram. iṟṟi yāṅkilac coṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa aintilakkam. ivaṟṟuḷ, nūṟṟiṟkup pattē āṅkilam; eṇpatu kirēkka vilattīṉam (Greeko Latin); ēṉip pattu ēṉi moḻikaḷ. camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa īrilakkam. ivaṟṟuḷ, nūṟṟiṟ kirupatu teṉcoṟkaḷ; irupatu vaṭa tiraviṭam; muppatu mēliyāriyam; irupatu puṉi coṟkaḷ; pattu iṭukuṟikaḷ. kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻc coṟkaḷ ēṟattāḻa ōrilakkam. kumari nilam muḻukiya piṉpum paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyam iṟantupaṭṭa piṉpum, iṉṟu eñci niṟkum ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum ariyilakkam. iviyellām taṉit tamiḻ.
paṇpaṭṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ ilakkak kaṇakkāy eṇṇappaṭṭi ruppiṉum, avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉa puṇar coṟkaḷum toṭarc coṟkaḷumāṉa kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷē. taṉic coṟkaḷ cila pallāyiramē. avaṟṟuḷḷum vērc coṟkaḷ ciṉṉūṟē.
moḻikaḷ, iyaṉmoḻi, tirimoḻi eṉa iruvakippaṭum. tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu iyaṉmoḻi; oṉṟiṉiṉṟu tirintatu tirimoḻi. tamiḻ, intu māvāriyil mūḻkippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattil ki. mu. aimpatiṉāyi ram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē tōṉṟi muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭintirunta taṉimoḻi. ki. mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭut tōṉṟiya talikkaḻakattilēyē muttamiḻilakkiya vilakkaṇaṅkaḷ muṟṟiyiruntaṉa. ilakkaṇattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu ilakkiyam. ilakkiyattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu moḻi. ilakkiyamum eḻutappaṭṭa nilikku muntiyatu eḻutap paṭānili. aci nili, puṇar nili, paku 
coṉṉili, koḷuvu nili eṉa nāṉilikaḷ koṇṭu vaḷarntatu tamiḻ moḻi. in nāṉilikkum pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟi ruttal vēṇṭum. māntaṉ tōṟṟam ki. mu. aintilakkam āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatu. cāli (jāvā)t tīviṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa māntaṉelumpu akkālattatu. āppirikkāvil taṅkaṉikkā nāṭṭil aṇmiyiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa māntaṉelumpu, ki.mu. āṟilakkam āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatu. iv vīriṭaṅkaḷum muḻukippōṉa kumarik kaṇṭattic cārntavi. paṉimali (imayam) eḻātu kaṭalukkuḷḷirunta pōtu, āttirēliyā, āppirikkā, intiyā ākiya muk kaṇṭaṅ kaḷiyum oṉṟāyiṇittuk koṇṭirunta toṉmutu paḻanilam kumarik kaṇṭam.
tamiḻ tāṉē tōṉṟiya teṉmoḻi, toṉmoḻi, muṉmoḻi, meṉmoḻi, vaḷamoḻi, tāymoḻi. ulakap perumoḻikaṭkuḷ tūymi pēṇakkūṭiya taṉimoḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē.

taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ"

* "The more popular word in Tamil derived from Acarya is Asiriyer" eṉṟu par. es. kē. nāyar eṉpavar 'pērāciriyar irā. pi. cētu' malaril kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āṉāl, em āciriyar, āciriyar eṉuñ colli ācu + iriyar eṉṟu pirittuk kāṭṭik kaṟpittuḷ ḷār. enta muṭivu ēṟpuṭittu?
āciriyaṉ eṉṉum colli ācu + iriyaṉ eṉṟu pirippatē ēṟpuṭittu. cētup piḷḷi niṉivu malark kaṭṭuri yāciriyarel lārum moḻinūṟ pēraṟiñarallar.

* tamiḻiṟ pulamipeṟa muṟiyākap payilavēṇṭiya nūṟkaḷ yāvi?
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷiṉ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ, taṉippāṭaṟ ṟiraṭṭu, nālaṭiyār, tirukkuṟaḷ, tirukkōvi, cilappatikāram, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, pattup pāṭṭu, eṭṭut toki, naṉṉūl, yāpparuṅkalak kāriki, tolkāppiyam ākiya nūlkaḷi maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ vaḻit tamiḻāciri yarpāṟ kaṟka.
irā. pālakiruṭṭiṇaṉ, maturi.
* tamiḻ moḻiyil eḻuttuc cīrttiruttam tēviyā? ām eṉil atu evvāṟu ākkavēṇṭum?
ī eṉṉum eḻutti muṉpōl '    ' eṉṉuṅ kuṟiyāṟ kuṟikkalām, au, ku mutaliya aukāra eḻuttukaḷiṉ 'ḷa' eṉṉum tuṇivariyi, ḷakara vaṭiviṉiṉṟum pirittaṟitaṟ poruṭṭuc caṟṟuc ciṟitākkal vēṇṭum. ivi tavira vēṟu tiruttam vēṇṭiyatilli.
ni. ku. kuppucāmi, cēlam.
* iṉṟu ciṟantatākak karutappaṭṭuvarum paṇṭik kālat tamiḻc cuvaṭikaḷit tēṭiṉāl iṉṉum avaṟṟil cilavēṉum kiṭikkap peṟuvōmā? taṟpōtu (u.va.cā.viṟkup piṟaku) paḻiya nūlkaḷit tēṭuvatil īṭuṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ yāvar?
iṉṟit tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭumaṉṟi maliyāḷa nāṭṭilum yāḻp pāṇattilum tēṭiṉāl, ētēṉum yāṅkēṉum paḻantamiḻc cuvaṭi kiṭikkalām. mēṉāṭukaḷilum tēṭiṟ kiṭikkum. eṉakkut terintavari, paḻantamiḻ ēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷit tēṭuvatil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa var iṉṟu oruvarumilli.
pe. kiruṭṭiṇacāmi, ceṉṉappanāyakkaṉpāḷiyam.
* teṉṉāṟkāṭu, teṉṉārkkāṭu ivaṟṟil etu cari? ēṉ?
'teṉṉārkkāṭu' eṉpatē cari. iṅṅaṉam 'vaṭārkkāṭu' allatu 'vaṭavārkkāṭu' eṉpatum. ārkkāṭu eṉṉum ūrāṟ peyar peṟṟi runta oru perumāvaṭṭam piṉpu vaṭakkum teṟkumāka iru māvaṭṭaṅka ḷākap pirikkap paṭṭatu.
ārkkāṭu eṉṉum ūr vēlūr vaṭṭattic cērntatu; mikap paḻami yāṉatu. "aḻici yārkkāṭu" eṉṟu kuṟuntokiyilum (258), "ariyalaṅ kaḻaṉi yārkkāṭu" eṉṟu naṟṟiṇiyilum (190), vantuḷḷatu. ār = ātti. ārkkāṭu= āttikkāṭu. ic collip purāṇikar āṟukāṭu eṉṟu piṟaḻak koṇṭu, vaṭamoḻiyilum caṭāraṇiyam eṉṟu moḻipeyarttu viṭṭaṉar. ṣaṭ = āṟu. āraṇiyam = kāṭu.
tāyammi, cēlam 9.
* kaṭalkōḷāl aḻinta ilakkiya nūlkaḷuḷ "paṇṇatti" eṉpatuvum oṉṟu eṉappaṭukiṟatu. an nūl paṟṟiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kiṭikkumāyiṉ aruḷkūrntu teriviyuṅkaḷ.
"pāṭṭiṭik kalanta poruḷa vākip
ð£ì¢® ù¤òô ðí¢íî¢î¤ ò¤òô¢«ð."	(1436)
eṉpatu tolkāppiya nūṟpā.
"paḻampāṭṭiṉūṭu kalanta poruḷē taṉakkup poruḷākap pāṭṭum uriyum pōlac ceyyappaṭuvaṉa paṇṇatti eṉṟavāṟu..... aviyāvaṉa: nāṭakac ceyyuḷākiya pāṭṭu maṭiyum vañcippāṭṭum mōtirappāṭṭum kaṭkaṇṭum mutalāyiṉa........ avi vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka" eṉpatu pērāciriyar uri.
kālañ ceṉṟa paṇṭārakar (Dr.) perum pērāciriyar (mahā mahōpāttiyāya) u.va. cāminātiyar avarkaḷ paṇṇattiyi nāṭōṭip pāṭṭeṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nāṭōṭip pāṭṭeṉṟatu nāṭṭuppuṟap pāṭṭi.
paṇṇattiyeṉpatu, intuttāṉip pāṭṭukaḷil varum tokiya rāvāyirukkalām.
* "pāvam" "pāvi" eṉpaṉa tamiḻc coṟkaḷtāmā?
'pāvam' 'pāvi' eṉpaṉa vaṭacoṟkaḷ. aṟaṅkaṭi, karicu, tīviṉi eṉpaṉa pāvattik kuṟikkum tamiḻc coṟkaḷ.
* "kuṇakuṇi campantam" (maṟi - tirunāvukkaracu eḻutiya maṟimali aṭikaḷ varalāṟu 64ām pakkam) eṉṟa viḷakkattiṉpaṭi nallaṟiñar aḻakiya tōṟṟaṅkaḷil viḷaṅka vēṇṭiyirukka, cākraṭīcu, āpirakām liṅkaṉ ākiya ciṟanta aṟiñar aḻakaṟṟa tōṟṟam peṟṟi runtatēṉ?
akattaḻakum mukattaḻakum eṉṟum ottirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu iyaṟki neṟiyaṉṟu. oṭṭumāviyum kūṭṭumāveṉṉum koṭṭi māviyum oppu nōkkik kāṇka.
ka. paḻaṉiyappaṉ, poṉmali, tirucci - 4.
* tamiḻil paṉṉeṭuṅkālamāka viravi vaḻaṅkivarum vaṭamoḻi (āriya eḻuttukaḷāṉa ja, ṣa, sa, kṣa, ha eṉṟa) eḻuttukaḷ vaṭa moḻiyil vēṟu vaṭivam peṟṟirukkat tamiḻil, ja, ṣa, sa, kṣa, ha eṉṟa vaṭivam vantatu evvāṟu? eppoḻutu? ēṉ?
āriyar teṉṉāṭṭiṟku vanta piṉṉarē tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakki yoṭṭik kirantāṭcaram eṉṉum āriya veḻuttukaḷi amittuk koṇṭaṉar. ak kālam ki.mu. ēṟattāḻa 1500. ka + ṣa = kṣa.
* kallūrikaḷil aṉittum tamiḻp payiṟṟumoḻi ēṟpaṭuttu vatupaṟṟit taṅkaḷ karutteṉṉa? aṟiviyal nūlkaḷ tamiḻil pōtumāṉa aḷavu illātirukka, māṇavarkaḷ avaṟṟit tamiḻil paṭittu eṅṅaṉam mēli nāṭṭu aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷukku nikarāka viḷaṅka muṭiyum?
iv viṉāviṟku viṭi ēṟkaṉavē eḻutappeṟṟuḷatu. muntiya viṉā viṭi mālikaḷik kāṇka.
na. taṭcu, maṇalūr.
* mukattik kuṟikkumpōtu "mūñci" eṉṟum colkiṟōm. ivi iraṇṭum tamiḻc coṟkaḷtāmā? viḷakka vēṇṭukiṟēṉ.
'mukam' 'mūñci' iraṇṭum tamiḻc coṟkaḷē.
mukam makkaḷatu; mūñci (Muzzle) vilaṅkukaḷatu.
mukam eṉṉum col varalāṟṟit tamiḻppoḻiliṟ kāṇka.
mūcu - mūñcu - mūñci. mūcutal - mūccuviṭutal, mūcu - mūccu.
* "piṟaku" eṉpataṟku 'mallākka', eṉṟum 'poṟakki' eṉṟum colli varukiṉṟaṉar. avi entac coṟkaḷiṉ tirivu?
mal - mallā - mallākka. mallāttal - malartal. piṟaku - puṟaku - puṟakē - puṟakēkku - puṟakikku - puṟikku.
kōvi. ka. vēlaṉ, tañci.
* ippoḻutu puttāṇṭu eṉṟu cittiri mutalnāḷak koṇṭāṭu kiṟārkaḷē, itu uṇmiyil tamiḻp puttāṇṭā? ām eṉiṉ etaṉ aṭippaṭiyil iti vakuttaṉar?
tamiḻāṇṭu eṉṟu toṭaṅkuvateṉpatu tiṭṭamāyt teriya villi. aṟuvakip perumpoḻutukaḷuḷ (paruvaṅkaḷuḷ) iḷa vēṉil ciṟantateṉṉum ilakkiya aṭippaṭiyil tamiḻāṇṭu cittiriyeṉṉum mēḻa mātattil toṭaṅkiyatu pōlum!
mukavi. āṭalvallāṉ.
* ulaka moḻiyāka viḷaṅka esperanto eṉum moḻi kaṇṭu piṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ulaka moḻikaḷiṉ tiṟamyāvum vaṭitteṭukkap paṭṭa moḻiyitu veṉpa taṟivōm. tamiḻiṉ tiṟam, vaḷam - im moḻiyākkattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatā? illāviṭil ulaka moḻi yākat tamiḻi ākkiṭa iyalum vaḻivakikaḷik kūṟiṭa vēṇṭu kiṟēṉ. ayal nāṭṭārum nayantu ēṟṟiṭum vakiyil tamiḻi eḷi tākkit taṟpōtiya esperantoviṉ iṭattippeṟa āyvu naṭatta muṭiyumā?
āṅkilam ulaka moḻiyākap paravāta 17ām 18ām 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷil, mēṉāṭṭu moḻiyaṟiñar perumpālum airōppiya moḻic coṟkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, volapuk, esperanto, ido, esperantido, interlinqua, novial mutaliya ceyaṟki moḻikaḷi ulaka moḻikaḷākap payaṉpaṭumāṟu paṭittamittaṉar. ikkālattō, āṅkilam ulaka moḻiyākiviṭṭatu. iṉi ataṉ iṭatti vēṟoru moḻi peṟa muṭiyātu. cu.ā. tiruvārūraṉ, tiruppātirippuliyūr.
* cilappatikārattil 'kolik kaḷak kāti'yil 'ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyum ceytēṉ' eṉṟu kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyiṭam kūṟiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṟu mutuk kuṟivi eṉpatu yārik kuṟikkiṉṟatu? kaṇṇakiyiyā? allatu mātaviyiyā? kaṇṇakiyik kūṟuvatāṉāl atu ēṉ mātaviyik kūṟiyatākak karutak kūṭātu?
"ciṟu mutukkuṟivi" eṉpatu kaṇṇakiyiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. kōvalaṉ muṟippaṭi maṇanta kula maṉivi kaṇṇakiyē. avaṉ avaḷi maṭantip paruvattilēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu mātaviyoṭu kūṭip patiṉintāṇṭu pōṟ kaḻittāṉ. kāṉal varippāṭṭiṟutiyil, mātaviyi "māyap poy pala kūṭṭu māyattāḷ" eṉṟu kūṟit tuṟan tāṉ. maturi vaḻippōkkil kucikaṉ koṇarntu nīṭṭiya mātavi muṭaṅkalip paṭitta piṉpum, "taṉṟītilaḷ" eṉṟu maṭṭum coṉ ṉāṉē yoḻiya avaḷi mīṇṭuṅ kātalittāṉallaṉ. kūṭi vāḻātum cilampoḻinta aṇikalaṉkaḷi yellām kavarntum, kaṇṇakikkē koṭumi ceytāṉ. aṅṅaṉamiruntum avaḷ āṟṟiyiruntatum, "cilampuḷa koṇm" eṉac coṉṉatum, maturik keḻuka eṉṟavuṭaṉ eḻuntatum, āṟiṅkātam aruvaḻi naṭantatum, kuravariyum piṟa uṟaviṉariyum pirintu ayalār ūril taṉittirukka nērntatum, kaṇavaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṟ kaṭukaḷavuṅ 
kuṟiyāmiyum, kōvalaṉ koṭumikaḷiyellām ōruruvākki avaṉmuṉ koṇarntu niṟuttiṉa. avaṉ kariyillāk kaḻivirakkaṅ koṇṭu, avaḷi āṟṟit tēṟṟumāṟu.
"miyī rōtiyi varukeṉap poruntik
kallata rattaṅ kaṭakka yāvatum
valluna kollō maṭantimel laṭiyeṉa
vemmuṉi yaruñcuram pōntataṟ kiraṅki
............................................................................
ciṟumutuk kuṟivikkuc ciṟumiyuñ ceytēṉ
vaḻuveṉum pārēṉ mānakar maruṅkīṇ
ṭeḻukeṉa veḻuntā yeṉcey taṉiyeṉap"
pulampi uḷḷamaḻintāṉ. iv vamiyattil "ciṟu mutukkuṟivi" ev vakiyilum eḷḷaḷavum ēṉi yoruttiyik kuṟiyāteṉpatu teḷitaru tēṟṟam.
ā. vēlāyutam, koḻumpu - 6.
* taṅkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ evi? avaṟṟi eṅkup peṟṟuk koḷḷalām?
kaṭṭuri variviyal, uyartarak kaṭṭuri yilakkaṇam (2 pākam), collārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷ, tiraviṭattāy, mutaṟṟāymoḻi, paḻan tamiḻāṭci, tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷiyāṭṭukaḷ, ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyiṉ cīrkēṭu, tamiḻar tirumaṇam mutaliyaṉa. ivi, appar accakam, 2/140, pirāṭuvē ceṉṉi-1 eṉṟa mukavari koṇṭa civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattiṟ kiṭikkum. aṅkup peṟṟukkoḷka.
* tamiḻariṉ poṟkālam etu?
taṉittamiḻē taḻittōṅkiyatum, muttamiḻilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅ kaḷum palkali nūlkaḷum muṟṟa vaḻaṅkiyatum, vaṟumi yaṟiyappaṭāti runtatum, piṟappāl, ciṟappillātiruntatum, āriyat toṭarpu aṇu vaḷavumillātatumāṉa, (kumarik kaṇṭat teṉ maturit) talik kaḻakak kālamē tamiḻariṉ poṟkālam.
* tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiya auviyārkaḷ ettaṉip pēr? ententak kālaṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar?
iṟṟi yilakkiyattāl teḷivā yaṟiyappaṭṭa auviyār iruvar. oruvar kaṭik kaḻakak kālattavar (ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu). iṉṉoruvar 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉar. vāṉkōḻi in nāṭṭiṟkup patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatāyiṉ, ātticūṭi, koṉṟi vēntaṉ, nalvaḻi, mūturi ākiya aṟa nūlkaḷi iyaṟṟiya vari 16ām allatu 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta 3ām auviyā rākak koḷḷavum iṭamuṇṭu.
* "tolkāppiyam" taṉittamiḻil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatā?
tolkāppiyam ōr āriyarāl eḻutappaṭṭa cārpiṟ cārpu nūl. ataṟkumuṉ taṉittamiḻ mutaṉūlum vaḻinūlum nūṟṟuk kaṇak kāṉa cārpu nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭup pōyiṉa. tolkāppiyatti luḷḷa vaṭacoṟkaḷum irupatiṟku mēmpaṭṭaviyalla. an nūliṉ virivi nōkkumpōtu avi poruṭpaṭuttat takkaṉavākā. irup pāṇiyuḷḷa mara nāṟkāliyum mara nāṟkāli yeṉṟē collappaṭuvatu pōl, oru cila vaṭa coṟkaḷuḷḷa tolkāppiyamum taṉittamiḻ nūleṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum.
mēlum, tolkāppiya vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻukkiṉṟiyamiyātaṉavu malla. avi vēṇṭātu pukuttappaṭṭaṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟi vilakka vuṅ kūṭum.
ulakil, piṟa moḻic colliṉṟi muḻut tūymiyāy eḻutak kūṭiya moḻi tamiḻ oṉṟē. vaṭa moḻic coṟkaḷuḷ aintiliru pakuti tamiḻāyiruppati nōkkumpōtu, tolkāppiya vaṭacoṟ ṟokiyāl ataṉ taṉittamiḻ naṭi iḻukkuṟuvataṉṟeṉka.
* kamparāmāyaṇam pukaḻ peṟṟatupōl pāmara makkaḷiṭam kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pukaḻ peṟātatiṉ ētuveṉṉa?
kaḻaka vilakkiyam kamparāmāyaṇam pōṟ pukaḻ peṟāmikkuk karaṇiyaṅkaḷ (kāraṇaṅkaḷ),
1)	āriyam uyarttappeṟṟatum tamiḻ tāḻttappaṭṭatum.
2)	irāmāyaṇap pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ iṟivaṉ tōṟṟaravu (avatāram) eṉṉum nampikki.
3)	kampaṉ kalvit tiṟam.
4)	irāmāyaṇak katiyiṉ virivum poruṭ paṉmiyum.
5)	iṟṟit tamiḻp potumakkaḷiṉ tāḻnta aṟivu nili.
* tāymoḻikkut tīṅku iḻippavarkaḷi eṉṉa peyariṭṭu aḻikkalām?
tāymoḻik koliñar eṉṟaḻikka.
* "maṟi"yiṉ poruḷ viḷakkameṉṉa?
kallā makkaṭku maṟivāyirukkum poruḷkaḷi viḷakkuvatu maṟi.
ca. ki. vēṇi iḷampariti.
* "irāvaṇa kāviyam" paṟṟit taṅkaḷ karuttu yātu? ataṉit taṭi ceytatu cariyā?
irāmāyaṇak katiyāl teṉṉāṭṭārkku allatu tamiḻarkku iḻivu ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷateṉṟu karuti, ataṉi nīkkuvatu irāvaṇa kāviyam. irāmāyaṇam oru vakuppārāl maṟi nūlotta mata nūlākak koḷḷappeṟuvatāl, avar 
maṉattip puṇpaṭuttak kūṭāteṉṟu ataṉit taṭi ceytuḷḷaṉar araciyalār.
cīti irāmaṉukkut taṅkiyeṉṟum, irāmaṉ taṉ taṅkiyi maṇantāṉeṉṟum, irāmaṉ irāvaṇaṉ maṉiviyik kavarntā ṉeṉṟum, palvēṟāka nāṟpatu irāmāyaṇak katikaḷiruppatākac collappaṭuvatālum, irāmayaṇam kaṭṭuk katiyeṉṟu ārāycci yāḷarāṟ karutap peṟuvatālum, enta irāmāyaṇak kati vēṟupāṭṭi yum taṭi ceyyak kūṭāteṉpatu oru cārār karuttu.
aṟivaṉ, paṟampi.
* moḻi vaḷarccikkuriya taṅkaḷiṉ varuṅkālat tiṭṭameṉṉa?
tamiḻiluḷḷa ayaṟ coṟkaḷellām kaḷin teṟiyappaṭal vēṇṭum. vaḻakku vīḻttappaṭṭa paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷ putukkap peṟavum, ik kālattiṟkēṟṟa putuc coṟkaḷ taṉittamiḻiṟ puṉiyap peṟavum vēṇṭum.
āṭ peyar, poruṭ peyar, iṭap peyar ellām tamiḻc coṟkaḷā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
ayaṟ coṟpōṉṟē ayaleḻuttum vēṇṭā.
mēli aṟiviyal nūlkaḷellām tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkap peṟal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ nāṭṭu āṭciyum tamiḻar caṭaṅkum tirumaṇamum kōyil vaḻipāṭum tamiḻil naṭipeṟal vēṇṭum.
uyar mēṟ kalvi kaṟkavum veḷināṭu cellavum virumpuvārkku āṅkilattilum, uḷnāṭṭiṟ piḻippiṟkāka maṭṭum kalvi kaṟpārkkut tamiḻilum, āka iru moḻiyilum, mutaliliruntu iṟutivari kalvi kaṟpikkap peṟal vēṇṭum.
intiyum camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyum tamiḻarkku vēṇṭā. tamiḻ keṭṭatē camaṟkirutattāltāṉ. mēli yāriyattic cērnta āṅkilam kīḻiyāriyamākiya camaṟkirutattiṉum tamiḻukku mika nerukkamākum. iv vuṇmiyi ārāycciyillār aṟiyār.
intiya āṭci āṅkilattilēyē naṭipeṟal vēṇṭum. vaṭavar itaṟ kiciyāviṭiṉ, tamiḻnāṭu moḻiyiyal taṉṉāṭci (linguistic autonomy) peṟal vēṇṭum. ataṉpaṭi, tamiḻnāṭṭoṭu naṭuvaṇ āṭcikkum (Central Govt.) piṟa cīmikaṭkum (mākāṇaṅkaṭkum) āṅkilattilēyē toṭarpirukka muṭiyum.
intiyip potu moḻiyākat tamiḻaṟiñar oppuk koḷḷa villiyeṉṟum, atu potu moḻiyākum takutiyuṭiyataṉ ṟeṉṟum, atuvum āṅkilampōl tamiḻarkku ayaṉmoḻiyum āriyamoḻiyu māku meṉṟum, āṅkilam aṟiviyaṉ moḻiyum intiya moḻiyum ulakap potu moḻiyumā 
yuḷḷateṉṟum, māntaṉ vāḻnāḷ kuṟukiyuḷḷa ik kālattiṟku iru moḻik koḷkiyē ēṟkumeṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
ce. pāṇṭiyaṉ, kōvi - 2.
* tamiḻ olik kuṟiyīṭukaḷil tēvippaṭuṅkāl vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷāṉa j, h, s, ṣ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum; āṅkila eḻuttukaḷāṉa J, F, H, G mutaliya vaṟṟiyum, eḻutikkāṭṭa eṉṉa muṟiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭum? nam eḻut tamippil ētāvatu māṟṟam ceytu koḷḷalāmā? avvāṟāyiṉ, am muṟiyit teṉmoḻiyil eḻutuvīrkaḷā?
ovvoru moḻikkum ovvōr olittokutiyuṇṭu. ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa olikaḷ ēṟattāḻa irupattintē. perumoḻikaḷuḷ mikak kuṟinta olikaḷuḷḷavi tamiḻum, mika niṟinta olikaḷuḷḷatu vaṭamoḻiyu mākum. tamiḻiṉ aṭippaṭi yolikaḷ muppatu. vaṭamoḻi yolikaḷ nāṟpat teṭṭu mutal aimpattu mūṉṟuvari palavāṟu collappeṟum.
ovvoru peru moḻikkum cila ciṟappolikaḷuḷa. moḻika ḷellām valliyal, melliyal eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, valliyaṉ moḻikaḷ ēṉi moḻic ciṟappolikaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa vaṟṟi ēṟkun tiṟattaṉa. melliyaṉ moḻiyō at tiṟattataṉṟu. tamiḻ, melliyaṉ moḻikaḷuḷ tali ciṟantatu. ātalāl, piṟamoḻi vallolikaḷi ēṟkātu. melloliyuṭaṉ valloliyi iṇippatu. melliya mallāṭiyuṭaṉ valliya kampaḷiyi iṇippatu pōṉṟatē. āṭavar peṇṭir mēṉikaḷpōl, valliyaṉ moḻikaḷum melliyaṉ moḻikaḷum eṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭēyirukkum. tamiḻil vallolikaḷ kalappiṉ ataṉ taṉmi muṟṟum māṟiviṭum. ataṉpiṉ atu tamiḻākātu.
tamiḻiṉ meṉmiyi yuṇarntē, ki.mu. 6ām allatu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tolkāppiyar tamiḻc ceyyuṭku vaṭacolli vēṇṭātu vakutta viṭattum,
"vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi
eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collā kummē"	(884)
eṉṟu vaṭaveḻutti vilakkuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, 
"iṭiyil nāṉkum īṟṟi liraṇṭum
allā acci varukka mutalīṟu
yavvāti nāṉmi ḷavvākum aiyim
potuveḻut toḻinta nālēḻun tiriyum"	(146)
eṉṟu vaṭacoṟkaḷ peruvāriyāyt tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅku vataṟku vaḻi vakutta pavaṇantiyārum.

19ām nūṟṟāṇṭil takka pulavarum, puravalarum iṉmiyāl, tamiḻ uri naṭiyilum ceyyuḷilum vaṭacoṟkaḷuṭaṉ vaṭaveḻuttu kaḷum tārāḷamāy vantu kalantuviṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiyellām niṟi tamiḻ vāṇarāṉa maṟimali yaṭikaḷ kaḷinteṟintār.
oru tamiḻp pēraṟiñar āyta veḻuttiṉiyalpip piṟaḻa vuṇarntu, ataṉikkoṇṭu āriya volikaḷiyellām tamiḻiṟ kuṟikka voṇṇu meṉṟum, ataṟkākavē atu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil vakukkappaṭṭateṉṟum karutiṉār. aḵtāyiṉ tamiḻ oru valliyaṉ moḻiyāyum ataṉ neṭuṅ kaṇakku āriya moḻikaḷellām tōṉ ṟiyapiṉ ēṟpaṭṭatāyumiruttal vēṇṭum. tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum muṉmiyum meṉmiyum ak koḷkikku muṟṟum māṟāyuḷḷami kāṇka.
āytam eṉpatu oru vaki nuṇṇiya kakaravoliyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. āytal - nuṇṇiyatātal.
"ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam"	(813)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka.
āyta voliyip piṟaḻa vuṇarntum, oli vaṭiviṟkum varivaṭi viṟkum iyipiṉmiyi aṟiyātum, āyta varivaṭivit tuṇi koṇṭu ku, ṣ  pōṉṟa āṅkila volikaḷic cilar tamiḻiṟ kuṟittu varukiṉṟaṉar. eḻut teṉpatu uṇmiyil oliyēyaṉṟi variyaṉṟu. tamiḻ varivaṭivāl ōr ayaloliyi iṭarppaṭṭuk kuṟikka muyalvatiṉum, av volikkuriya ayaṉ moḻi varivaṭiviyē taḻuvuvatu naṉṟāyirukkumē! ōr oliyit taḻuvumpōtu ēṉ ataṉ variyit taḻuvutal kūṭātu? āṅkilam ulaka moḻikaḷellā vaṟṟiṉiṉṟum coṟkaḷik kaṭaṉ koṇṭiruntum, avaṟṟi yellām taṉṉoliyālum taṉ variyālumaṉṟō iṉṟum kuṟittu varukiṉṟatu.
moḻiyeṉpatu olit tokutiyēyaṉṟi varit tokutiyaṉṟu. vari māṟalām, oli māṟātu. oli māṟiṉ moḻi māṟi viṭum. cevippulaṉāya voliyik kaṭpulaṉākkuṅ kuṟiyē variyām.
mutalil vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum piṉpu vaṭa veḻuttukaḷiyum ovvoṉṟākap pukuttuvatiyē, koṭuntamiḻ moḻikaḷi āriya vaṇṇamāṉa tiraviṭamākkum vaḻiyāka, toṉṟutoṭṭu vaṭa moḻi yāḷar kiyāṇṭu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. cēra nāṭṭuc centamiḻ cōḻa pāṇṭi nāṭṭoṭu toṭarpaṟṟuk koṭun tamiḻākip piṉpu, āriyac cērkkiyāl maliyāḷam allatu kēraḷam eṉṉum tiraviṭa moḻiyākat tirintuḷḷami kāṇka. koṭuntamiḻkaḷi muṉṉar āriya vaṇṇamākkiyatu pōṉṟē, iṉṟu cen tamiḻiyum ākka muyaṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. ataṉoṭu āṅkila eḻuttukaḷum coṟkaḷum cēriṉ, tamiḻ virintu aḻintu pōvatu tiṇṇam. aracaṉ, nakiccuvi, pottakam allatu cuvaṭi, pū, paṟavi eṉṉum teṉ coṟka ḷirukka, avaṟṟiṟku māṟāka ēṉ rājaṉ, hāsya racam, pustakam, puṣpam, pēḷa eṉṉum vaṭa coṟkaḷiyum vaṭa col vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum taḻuva vēṇṭum?
iyaṟki yolikaḷum ceyaṟki yolikaḷum mikuntu neṭuṅ kaṇakku nīṇṭa vaṭamoḻiyuḷḷum, e, o eṉṟa uyirk kuṟilkaḷum, ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukaḷum ākiya tamiḻolikaḷum, F, Z eṉṉum āṅkilavolikaḷum, cila arapiyolikaḷumilli. ākavē, ovvoru moḻikkum cila ciṟappolikaḷuḷa. avaṟṟi yellām taḻuvuvatu āṅkilamākiya ulaka moḻi oṉṟaṟkē takum. ellā moḻikaḷum taḻuva vēṇṭiyatilli; taḻuviṉ, ellām tattam taṉit taṉmiyiḻantu oṉṟākiviṭum.
oru moḻiyiṉ vaḷam allatu valimi ataṉ coṟkaḷā lāyatu. poruḷtarum colliṟku uṟuppākum oliyē eḻuttām. atu taṉṉaḷaviṟ poruḷ tarātu. ataṉālākum collē poruḷ taruvatu. kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar, muppatolikaḷik koṇṭē, ak kālattu māntaruḷḷattileḻunta karuttukaḷik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷi yum, piṟkālattileḻuṅ karuttukaḷi kuṟittaṟkēṟṟa coṟkaruvikaḷi yum, amittuc ceṉṟaṉar. ātalāl, olik kuṟiviṉāl tamiḻiṟku ētum moḻikkuṟivilli. āyiraṅ kāycciyāṉa teṉṉikku ōlik kuṟiviṉāl oru kuṟivumilli aḻakiya colvaḷa mikka tamiḻukku olik kuṟiviṉāl oru kuṟivumilli. ovvoṉṟum ataṉataṉ iyalpil niṉṟē vaḷartal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, tamiḻukku evvakiyilum eḻuttu māṟṟam tēviyiṉṟeṉa aṟika. atu tamiḻukku iṟuti viḷikkumeṉṟē maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum viṭuttaṉar. atuvē uṟutiyeṉṟu kaṭippiṭikka.
28
ī.va. irā. periyārukku viṭutta veḷippaṭi vēṇṭukōḷ
(tamiḻnāṭṭut tanti ī.va.irā.periyār avarkaṭku 
ñā.tavanēyaṉ eḻutuvatu: vēṇṭukōḷ.)
aṉpārnta aiya,  vaṇakkam,
tāṅkaḷ ituvari ari nūṟṟāṇṭākak kumukāya (camutāya)t tuṟiyilum matattuṟiyilum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuc ceytuvanta arum perun toṇṭu aṉivarum aṟintatē. āyiṉ, moḻittuṟiyil oṉṟum ceyyavilli. oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ muṉṉēṟum orēvaḻi avar tāymoḻiyē. āciriyap payiṟcik kallūri tāṅkaḷē oṉṟu niṟuviṉīrkaḷ. āṅkilak kalikkallūri oṉṟiṟku aintilakkam urupā māṉiyamāka utaviṉīrkaḷ. in nāṭṭu moḻiyākiya tamiḻi vaḷarkka oru kallūriyum niṟuvavilli.
ātalāl, taṅkaḷ peyar eṉṟum maṟiyāmalum taṅkaḷ toṇṭiṉ payaṉ ciṟitum kuṟiyāmalum iruttaṟkuk kīḻkkāṇumāṟu periyār taṉmāṉap pakuttaṟivup palkalikkaḻakam eṉac ceṉṉiyil oru kalvi niṟuvaṉam iyaṉṟa virivil niṟuvumāṟu taṅkaḷi vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
tiṭṭam
tamiḻiyum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, teluṅku, kuṭaku, tuḷu, kōttam, tōṭam, kōlāmi, nāykki, parci, kaṭapā, kōṇṭi, koṇṭā, kūy, kuvī, kurukku, mālattō, pirākuvī eṉṉum patiṉeṇ tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiyum tamiḻ vāyilākavum āṅkila vāyilākavum kaṟpittal.
matam kaṟpikkappaṭātu. kaṭavuḷ vāḻttup pāṭappeṟātu. ataṟkīṭākap periyār vāḻttu allatu pukaḻē pāṭappaṭum. kallūri naṭappiṟkuriya caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷit tāṅkaḷē amittut taralām.
āciriyar kuḻu
mutalvar : pērā. ti.va. ªê£è¢èð¢ð£ âñ¢.ã., âô¢.î¤.,
tamiḻp pērāciriyar: ñā. tēvanēyaṉ.
tuṇivar : tamiḻ maṟavar vi. poṉṉampalaṉār. piṟamoḻi kaṭkut takunta āciriyar viḷamparam ceytu amarttap peṟuvar.
māṇavar
paḷḷiyiṟuti allatu ataṟkuc camamāṉa tērvu mutal vakuppil tēṟiya aimpatiṉmar kula mata kaṭci yiṉa nāṭu vēṟupāṭiṉṟit terinteṭukkap peṟuvar.
veḷiyūr māṇavarkkum veḷināṭṭu māṇavarkkum uṇṇavum taṅkavum viṭuti yirukkum.
kalvik kaṭṭaṇamum viṭutik kaṭṭaṇamum piṉṉart terivikkap paṭum.
māṉiyam
ceṉṉiyil cūḻnilattoṭu kūṭiya māḷikiyoṉṟum aittilakkam urupāvum.
kaṭavi (Course)
ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam, moḻinūl, ēraṇam (Logic), ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum āṟum aintāṇṭu kaṟpikkappeṟum. āṅkilam, aṟiviyal ivaṟṟōṭu periyāriyal (Periyarism) eṉṟa pakuttaṟivuk koḷkiyum kaṟpikkappeṟum.
pāṭattiṭṭam piṉṉar vakukkappeṟum.
payaṉ
tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu mīṭkappeṟṟut tūyanaṭiyil kaṟpikkavum ulaka muḻuvatum parappavum peṟum.
tamiḻarum tiraviṭarum āriya aṭimittaṉam aṭiyōṭu nīkki muṉṉēṟṟap pātiyil aṭiyiṭṭu virintu naṭappar.
aiyāṭṭik kaṭavi muṭittup paṭṭam peṟṟa māṇavar uḷnāṭ ṭilum veḷināṭṭilum moḻiyāciriyarum moḻinūlāciriyarum ici yāciriyarum nāṭaka vāciriyarumākap paṇiyāṟṟum vāyppup peṟuvar.
aṉpaṉ
ñā. tēvanēyaṉ
kuṟippu : tiruvaḷḷuvarāṇṭu 2000 āṭavi 13ām pakkal (25.6.1969) aṉṟu itaṉ curukkam vēlūr nakaracapit talivar tiru. mā. pā. cārati avarkaḷiṉ tampi makaṉ tiru. aṉpaḻakaṉ tirumaṇa viḻāviṟkut talimi tāṅkiya periyār avarkaḷiṭam eṉṉāl nēriṟ koṭukkappeṟṟatu. iṉṉum maṟumoḻiyilli.
- teṉmoḻi
29
piṟanta nāṭ ceyti
intumāvāriyil mūḻkiyuḷḷa kumarikkaṇṭat teṉkōṭiyil ki.mu.50,000 (aimpatiṉāyiram) āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē muḻuvaḷarcci aṭinta tamiḻē ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyum tiraviṭat tāyum āriya mūlamum ākum. tēvamoḻiyeṉṟu ēmāṟṟit tamiḻakat tiṟ pukuttappaṭṭa camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyālēyē tamiḻ tāḻttappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl tamiḻaṉum tāḻttappaṭṭāṉ. tamiḻaṉ mīṇṭum muṉṉēṟuvataṟkut tamiḻ vaṭamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟum viṭutali yaṭital vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu tamiḻi mīṭpatē eṉ vāḻkkik kuṟikkōḷ. tamiḻ uyarntāltāṉ tamiḻaṉ uyaramuṭiyum. ataṟkut tamiḻar iṉi ellā vakiyilum tamiḻiyē pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum. mutaṟkaṇ tamiḻar aṉivarum tamiḻp peyarē tāṅkal vēṇṭum. āṇṭil oru nāḷip peyar māṟṟat tirunāḷ eṉṟu iṉi āṇṭutoṟum koṇṭāṭi varuvatu naṉṟu. piṟanta aṇmiyiṟ piṟamoḻip peyar peṟṟavarellārum an nāḷil tam peyarit taṉit tamiḻp peyarāka māṟṟikkoḷḷalām. ati illattil viḻāvākak koṇṭāṭuvatuṭaṉ ūrvalattālum ūrārkkaṟi vikkalām.
tamiḻppeyar tāṅkupavarē uṇmit tamiḻarāvar. tamiḻp peyar ēṟṟapiṉ tamiḻilēyē tirumaṇattiyum iruvakic caṭaṅku kaḷiyum ceytalum ceyvittalum vēṇṭum. kaṭavuḷ nampikki yuḷḷavar tiruvaḻipāṭṭiyum tamiḻilēyē āṟṟutal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu ceytuvariṉ iṉṉum aintāṇṭiṟkuḷ tamiḻartam aṭimittaṉamum, aṟiyāmiyum aṭiyōṭu nīṅki mēliyarpōl putuppuṉivu talippaṭuvar eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
āṅkilar nīṅkiyatāl tamiḻnāṭu viṭutaliyaṭiyavilli. āriyam nīṅkuvatē uṇmiyāṉa tamiḻar viṭutaliyām.
